  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>Zran4h — Chinese Vocabulary</title>
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&amp;family=Noto+Sans+Thai:wght@400;500;600;700&amp;family=Noto+Sans+Lao:wght@400;500;600;700&amp;family=Noto+Sans+SC:wght@400;500;600;700&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/xlsx@0.18.5/dist/xlsx.full.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/lucide@0.577.0/dist/umd/lucide.min.js"></script>
  <style>
    :root{--bg:#EDEBDD;--paper:#FAF9F5;--ink:#173F35;--forest:#24594A;--muted:#7C8981;--line:#d9ded3;--red:#a55049;--soft:#e7ede5;--gold:#C6A36B}
    *{box-sizing:border-box}html{scroll-behavior:smooth}body,button,input,select{font-family:"Noto Sans Thai",sans-serif}body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font-size:14px}button,input,select{font-size:inherit}button{cursor:pointer}button:disabled{cursor:not-allowed;opacity:.48}button:focus-visible,input:focus-visible,select:focus-visible{outline:3px solid #dfae70;outline-offset:3px}svg{width:19px;height:19px;flex:none}h1,h2,h3,p{margin-top:0}.app{max-width:1240px;margin:auto;padding:0 30px 60px}.top{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:20px;padding:27px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}.brand{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;font-weight:700;font-size:18px;letter-spacing:-.4px}.seal{width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:13px;background:var(--red);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font:700 22px "Noto Sans SC",sans-serif}.top-note{color:var(--muted);font-size:12px}.nav{display:flex;gap:7px;overflow:auto;padding:23px 0 12px}.nav button{white-space:nowrap;border:0;background:transparent;color:#627870;padding:11px 17px;border-radius:11px;font-weight:600}.nav button.active{background:var(--ink);color:white}.page{display:none;animation:fade .25s ease}.page.active{display:block}@keyframes fade{from{opacity:.5;transform:translateY(5px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}.eyebrow{color:var(--red);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1.5px;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase}.heading{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:end;gap:20px;margin:20px 0 25px}.heading h1{font-size:clamp(29px,4vw,43px);line-height:1.25;letter-spacing:-1.5px;margin:8px 0}.heading p{color:var(--muted);margin:0}.grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:16px}.card,.panel{background:var(--paper);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:20px;box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(23,55,47,.035)}.stat{padding:24px}.stat .icon{width:39px;height:39px;display:grid;place-items:center;border-radius:11px;background:var(--soft);color:var(--ink);margin-bottom:22px}.stat strong{font-size:34px;display:block;line-height:1.1}.stat span{color:var(--muted)}.two{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.3fr .7fr;gap:16px;margin-top:16px}.panel{padding:28px}.panel h2{font-size:20px;margin-bottom:8px}.panel p{color:var(--muted);line-height:1.75}.btn{border:0;border-radius:11px;padding:12px 18px;background:var(--ink);color:white;font-weight:700;display:inline-flex;gap:9px;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:transform .2s,background .2s}.btn:not(:disabled):hover{transform:translateY(-2px);background:#28564e}.btn.secondary{background:var(--soft);color:var(--ink)}.btn.secondary:hover{background:#dfeadf}.btn.red{background:var(--red)}.btn.small{padding:9px 13px;font-size:12px}.actions{display:flex;gap:9px;flex-wrap:wrap}.empty{text-align:center;padding:45px 20px;border:1px dashed #cbd8cf;border-radius:17px;background:#fbfcf8}.empty .empty-icon{margin:auto auto 15px;width:58px;height:58px;border-radius:18px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:var(--soft);color:var(--ink)}.empty h3{font-size:18px;margin-bottom:8px}.empty p{margin:0;color:var(--muted)}.upload{border:1px dashed #b9cbbd;border-radius:15px;padding:22px;background:#f8faf5}.upload label{display:block;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:10px}.upload input{width:100%;max-width:100%;background:white;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:9px;padding:10px}.hint{font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);line-height:1.7;margin:10px 0 0}.message{margin-top:12px;white-space:pre-line;line-height:1.65;color:var(--red)}.message.ok{color:#24765c}.toolbar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:20px}.toolbar input,.toolbar select{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:11px;background:white;padding:12px 14px;color:var(--ink);min-width:180px}.toolbar input{flex:1}.study-layout{max-width:740px;margin:auto}.progress-wrap{margin:0 0 20px}.progress-label{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;color:var(--muted);font-weight:600;margin-bottom:9px}.track{height:9px;border-radius:20px;background:#e2eae2;overflow:hidden}.fill{height:100%;background:var(--red);width:0;transition:width .3s}.flip-scene{perspective:1100px;cursor:pointer}.flashcard{height:355px;position:relative;transform-style:preserve-3d;transition:transform .65s cubic-bezier(.2,.7,.2,1)}.flashcard.flipped{transform:rotateY(180deg)}.face{position:absolute;inset:0;backface-visibility:hidden;-webkit-backface-visibility:hidden;border-radius:24px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--paper);box-shadow:0 18px 45px rgba(25,64,54,.10);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;text-align:center;padding:25px}.face.back{transform:rotateY(180deg);background:#f0f5ee}.card-top{position:absolute;top:22px;left:25px;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px}.hanzi{font:600 clamp(54px,10vw,88px) "Noto Sans SC",sans-serif;line-height:1.3;margin:0}.pinyin{font-size:20px;color:var(--red);margin:5px 0}.tap{position:absolute;bottom:20px;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px}.back h2{font-size:29px;margin:10px 0}.detail{font-size:13px;color:#50665e;line-height:1.65;max-height:145px;overflow:auto}.study-controls{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:20px 0}.study-controls .actions{justify-content:center}.status-actions{display:flex;justify-content:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap}.vocab-list{display:grid;gap:10px}.vocab-row{padding:18px 20px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:16px;align-items:center}.vocab-row h3{font:600 24px "Noto Sans SC",sans-serif;margin:0 0 4px}.vocab-row p{margin:0;color:var(--muted)}.tag{display:inline-block;border-radius:20px;padding:5px 10px;background:var(--soft);color:var(--ink);font-size:11px;font-weight:700}.quiz-box{max-width:750px;margin:auto}.quiz-prompt{font:600 58px "Noto Sans SC",sans-serif;text-align:center;margin:30px 0}.options{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px}.option{border:1px solid var(--line);background:#fff;border-radius:13px;padding:18px;text-align:left;color:var(--ink);font-weight:600}.option:hover:not(:disabled){border-color:var(--ink)}.option.correct{background:#e5f4e9;border-color:#6aa47c}.option.wrong{background:#fbe9e5;border-color:var(--red)}.quiz-feedback{min-height:28px;margin:16px 0;font-weight:700}.quiz-feedback.wrong{color:var(--red)}.footer{border-top:1px solid var(--line);margin-top:65px;padding-top:22px;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px}.hidden{display:none!important}@media(max-width:760px){.app{padding:0 17px 38px}.top{padding:19px 0}.top-note{display:none}.grid,.two{grid-template-columns:1fr}.heading{align-items:start;flex-direction:column}.panel{padding:21px}.stat{padding:19px}.stat .icon{margin-bottom:10px}.flashcard{height:340px}.study-controls{justify-content:center}.options{grid-template-columns:1fr}.vocab-row{align-items:start;flex-direction:column}.nav{margin-right:-17px;padding-right:17px}}
  </style>
  <style>
    /* Zran4h visual identity */
    body,button,input,select,textarea{font-family:"Inter","Noto Sans Thai","Noto Sans Lao","Noto Sans SC",sans-serif}
    html[lang="lo"] body,html[lang="lo"] button,html[lang="lo"] input,html[lang="lo"] select{font-family:"Inter","Noto Sans Lao","Noto Sans SC",sans-serif}html[lang="zh"] body,html[lang="zh"] button,html[lang="zh"] input,html[lang="zh"] select{font-family:"Inter","Noto Sans SC",sans-serif}
    .app{max-width:none;min-height:100vh;margin:0;padding:0 42px 36px 0;display:grid;grid-template-columns:242px minmax(0,1fr);grid-template-rows:auto 1fr auto;column-gap:42px}
    .top{grid-column:2;grid-row:1;padding:25px 0 21px;min-width:0}.brand{font-family:"Inter",sans-serif;font-size:23px;letter-spacing:-.8px;line-height:1.2}.brand small{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase}.seal{background:var(--forest);border:1px solid var(--gold);border-radius:11px;color:var(--gold)}
    .nav{grid-column:1;grid-row:1/4;position:sticky;top:0;align-self:start;height:100vh;overflow-y:auto;overflow-x:hidden;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;padding:105px 15px 28px;background:var(--ink);box-shadow:8px 0 35px rgba(23,63,53,.08)}
    .nav:before{content:'ZRAN4H  /  学习空间';position:absolute;top:37px;left:25px;color:var(--gold);font:600 12px "Inter","Noto Sans SC",sans-serif;letter-spacing:1.7px}
    .nav button{width:100%;text-align:left;border-radius:9px;padding:12px 14px;color:#d4dfd6;font-weight:500;transition:background .2s,color .2s}.nav button:hover{background:#24594A;color:#fff}.nav button.active{background:var(--forest);color:#fff;box-shadow:inset 3px 0 0 var(--gold)}
    main{grid-column:2;grid-row:2;min-width:0;padding-top:24px}.footer{grid-column:2;grid-row:3}.heading{margin:12px 0 30px}.heading h1{color:var(--ink);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-1.4px}.eyebrow{color:#8b714b;letter-spacing:2px}.card,.panel,.face{background:var(--paper);border-color:#dfe3d9;box-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(23,63,53,.045)}.card,.panel{border-radius:18px}.panel{padding:30px}.stat{border-top:3px solid var(--gold)}.stat strong{color:var(--ink)}.btn{background:var(--forest)}.btn:not(:disabled):hover{background:var(--ink)}.btn.secondary{background:#e3ebe3}.btn.secondary:hover{background:#d5e2d6}.btn.red{background:#a55049}.fill{background:var(--forest)}.pinyin{color:var(--forest);font-family:"Inter",sans-serif;letter-spacing:.3px}.hanzi{font-size:clamp(64px,10vw,100px)}.face.back{background:#f0f2e9}.option.correct{background:#e3f1e5}.option.wrong{background:#f9e7e3}.tag{background:#e8eee6}.upload{background:#f4f5ee}.empty{background:#f7f8f1}
    @media(max-width:1000px){.app{grid-template-columns:205px minmax(0,1fr);column-gap:25px;padding-right:24px}.top{flex-wrap:wrap}.language-control{margin-left:0}}
    @media(max-width:760px){.app{display:flex;flex-direction:column;padding:0 17px 35px}.top{order:0;padding:18px 0}.nav{order:1;position:static;width:auto;height:auto;min-height:0;margin:0 -17px;padding:12px 17px;display:flex;flex-direction:row;overflow-x:auto;overflow-y:hidden;box-shadow:none;gap:5px}.nav:before{display:none}.nav button{width:auto;text-align:center;padding:10px 14px}.nav button.active{box-shadow:inset 0 -3px 0 var(--gold)}main{order:2;padding-top:18px}.footer{order:3}.brand{font-size:21px}.heading{margin:12px 0 23px}.panel{padding:21px}}
  </style>
  <style>
    /* Quiet, illustrated atmosphere behind every learning view */
    body{background:radial-gradient(ellipse at 90% 12%,#fffaf0 0%,transparent 36%),radial-gradient(ellipse at 18% 85%,#e4f0df 0%,transparent 42%),#f1f3e9}
    .learning-world{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:0;overflow:hidden;pointer-events:none;contain:layout paint}
    .app{position:relative;z-index:1}
    .world-motif{position:absolute;display:block;will-change:transform;opacity:.78}
    .world-motif svg{width:100%;height:100%;overflow:visible}
    .world-panda{width:88px;height:100px;right:6px;top:24%;animation:world-bob 7s ease-in-out infinite}
    .world-rabbit{width:91px;height:108px;right:5px;bottom:6%;animation:world-bob 8s ease-in-out -3s infinite}
    .world-dragon{width:95px;height:70px;right:-110px;top:64%;animation:dragon-visit 28s ease-in-out 7s infinite}
    .world-cloud{width:92px;height:44px;right:3%;top:10%;opacity:.46;animation:world-drift 14s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
    .world-cloud.second{width:65px;right:1%;top:49%;opacity:.4;animation-delay:-6s}
    .world-spark{font:20px "Noto Sans SC",sans-serif;color:#c9aa72;right:3%;top:42%;animation:world-bob 9s ease-in-out -2s infinite}
    .world-spark.second{right:7%;top:78%;font-size:16px;animation-delay:-5s}
    .world-word{font:600 14px "Noto Sans SC",sans-serif;color:#89a894;right:2%;top:19%;letter-spacing:2px;animation:world-bob 10s ease-in-out infinite}
    .world-word.second{right:2%;top:58%;font-size:12px;animation-delay:-4s}
    .world-word.third{right:7%;top:88%;font-size:12px;animation-delay:-7s}
    .world-book{right:6%;top:34%;font-size:19px;animation:world-bob 8s ease-in-out -4s infinite}
    .world-eye{transform-origin:center;animation:world-blink 6s infinite}
    .world-wave{transform-origin:73px 57px;animation:world-wave 4s ease-in-out infinite}
    @keyframes world-bob{0%,100%{transform:translate3d(0,0,0) rotate(-2deg)}50%{transform:translate3d(-5px,-14px,0) rotate(2deg)}}
    @keyframes world-drift{from{transform:translate3d(0,0,0)}to{transform:translate3d(-35px,8px,0)}}
    @keyframes dragon-visit{0%,65%,100%{transform:translate3d(0,0,0)}75%{transform:translate3d(-75vw,-18px,0)}84%{transform:translate3d(-105vw,5px,0)}84.01%{transform:translate3d(0,0,0)}}
    @keyframes world-blink{0%,44%,48%,100%{transform:scaleY(1)}46%{transform:scaleY(.12)}}
    @keyframes world-wave{0%,100%{transform:rotate(0)}50%{transform:rotate(-18deg)}}
    body.world-paused .world-motif,body.world-paused .world-motif *{animation-play-state:paused!important}
    body.night-mode .learning-world{opacity:.25}
    @media(max-width:1100px){.learning-world{opacity:.55}.world-panda,.world-rabbit{right:-26px}.world-dragon{display:none}}
    @media(max-width:760px){.learning-world{opacity:.38}.world-panda{width:58px;height:66px;right:-28px;top:35%}.world-rabbit{width:62px;height:74px;right:-31px;bottom:7%}.world-word,.world-book,.world-spark{right:1%;font-size:10px}.world-cloud{right:-26px}}
    @media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.world-motif,.world-motif *{animation:none!important}}
    #quiz-home .quiz-mode-field{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:8px;margin:22px 0 20px;max-width:420px}#quiz-home .quiz-mode-field label{font-weight:700;color:var(--ink)}#quiz-home .quiz-mode-field select{width:100%;min-height:46px;padding:11px 14px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:11px;background:#fff;color:var(--ink)}#quiz-home .quiz-settings{padding-top:18px;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}@media(max-width:760px){#quiz-home .quiz-mode-field{max-width:none}#quiz-home .quiz-settings{align-items:flex-start}}
    .quiz-exit{margin-left:auto;white-space:nowrap}.quiz-exit-overlay{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:1000;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:20px;background:rgba(13,38,31,.63);backdrop-filter:blur(8px);animation:exit-fade .22s ease}.quiz-exit-card{position:relative;width:min(100%,430px);padding:36px 30px 30px;text-align:center;background:var(--paper);border:1px solid var(--gold);border-radius:24px;box-shadow:0 26px 80px rgba(6,26,18,.28);animation:exit-pop .3s cubic-bezier(.18,.9,.24,1.2)}.quiz-exit-icon{width:68px;height:68px;margin:0 auto 18px;border-radius:20px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:#f9e9dc;color:var(--red);transform:rotate(-7deg)}.quiz-exit-icon svg{width:32px;height:32px}.quiz-exit-card h2{font-size:24px;margin:0 0 10px}.quiz-exit-card p{color:var(--muted);line-height:1.8;margin:0 0 24px}.quiz-exit-actions{display:flex;justify-content:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap}.quiz-exit-actions .btn{min-width:145px}.quiz-exit-actions .btn.red{background:var(--red)}.quiz-exit-actions .btn.red:hover{background:#85423d}@keyframes exit-fade{from{opacity:0}to{opacity:1}}@keyframes exit-pop{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(18px) scale(.93)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0) scale(1)}}@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.quiz-exit-overlay,.quiz-exit-card{animation:none}}
  </style>
  <script src="/_sdk/ea2423e0a01a756f.data_sdk.js" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha512-Sg0g23oG9zpxeVGJw6Q935Wt19sv7pbBtYnmz0dqpxqMmhlUFeBYJjrBQheq1xeE/u69m7uZ8NgZRq4P2+K57w=="></script>
  <script src="/_sdk/a03dce2118604c50.editing_sdk.js" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha512-46sNF42LFaPn7btFIGQBmSR71vjynyOsc5USW8U39AL/x6ZIHAGWe0uHrH+ebFKQRMPNQspbZDL5hclteFjUzA=="></script>
  <script src="/_sdk/a202d01287f70bbd.resizing_sdk.js" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha512-1HSymNrRbmVrPINeyKeKDVHS1r/3KBjQvJ/IjvgAGUrP4+gPCV6nzQEbyDs7SEReA7mlPuHyFKB9Zk2K9KmXhQ=="></script>
 </head>
 <body>
  <div class="learning-world" aria-hidden="true"><span class="world-motif world-cloud">
    <svg viewbox="0 0 100 48">
     <path d="M18 39C-1 39 2 17 19 16 22 2 42 0 51 12 66 1 82 12 81 22 104 19 105 40 84 40Z" fill="#fffdf4" stroke="#d7e7d7" stroke-width="2" />
    </svg></span> <span class="world-motif world-cloud second">
    <svg viewbox="0 0 100 48">
     <path d="M18 39C-1 39 2 17 19 16 22 2 42 0 51 12 66 1 82 12 81 22 104 19 105 40 84 40Z" fill="#fffdf4" stroke="#d7e7d7" stroke-width="2" />
    </svg></span> <span class="world-motif world-panda">
    <svg viewbox="0 0 90 100">
     <ellipse cx="45" cy="77" rx="29" ry="20" fill="#fffdf5" stroke="#8fa99b" stroke-width="2" /><ellipse cx="25" cy="25" rx="12" ry="15" fill="#67877b" /><ellipse cx="65" cy="25" rx="12" ry="15" fill="#67877b" /><circle cx="45" cy="44" r="32" fill="#fffdf5" stroke="#8fa99b" stroke-width="2" /><ellipse cx="32" cy="42" rx="10" ry="13" fill="#67877b" transform="rotate(17 32 42)" /><ellipse cx="58" cy="42" rx="10" ry="13" fill="#67877b" transform="rotate(-17 58 42)" /><g class="world-eye" fill="#fff">
      <circle cx="33" cy="42" r="3" />
      <circle cx="57" cy="42" r="3" />
     </g><ellipse cx="45" cy="55" rx="5" ry="3.5" fill="#516b60" /><path d="M39 61q6 6 12 0" fill="none" stroke="#516b60" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" /><circle cx="22" cy="56" r="4" fill="#efd3c9" /><circle cx="68" cy="56" r="4" fill="#efd3c9" /><ellipse cx="19" cy="82" rx="9" ry="7" fill="#67877b" /><ellipse cx="70" cy="82" rx="9" ry="7" fill="#67877b" /><path class="world-wave" d="M69 67q14-13 11-19" fill="none" stroke="#67877b" stroke-width="10" stroke-linecap="round" />
    </svg></span> <span class="world-motif world-rabbit">
    <svg viewbox="0 0 90 108">
     <path d="M26 43Q13 3 25 3q12 0 15 39M52 42Q56 2 68 4q10 3-4 44" fill="#fffdf5" stroke="#aabdad" stroke-width="2" /><path d="M28 32L24 10M61 31l6-20" stroke="#f4cfc9" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round" /><ellipse cx="45" cy="82" rx="28" ry="23" fill="#fffdf5" stroke="#aabdad" stroke-width="2" /><circle cx="45" cy="52" r="29" fill="#fffdf5" stroke="#aabdad" stroke-width="2" /><g class="world-eye" fill="#577a69">
      <ellipse cx="35" cy="51" rx="2.5" ry="4" />
      <ellipse cx="55" cy="51" rx="2.5" ry="4" />
     </g><path d="M42 61l3 3 3-3M45 64v4" fill="none" stroke="#bf9290" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" /><circle cx="26" cy="61" r="4" fill="#f6d9d0" /><circle cx="64" cy="61" r="4" fill="#f6d9d0" /><path d="M27 77q17-9 36 0v22q-17-7-36 0Z" fill="#b4cdb5" stroke="#779b81" stroke-width="2" /><path d="M45 75v22" stroke="#779b81" stroke-width="2" /><path d="M34 84h6m10 0h6" stroke="#fffdf5" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" />
    </svg></span> <span class="world-motif world-dragon">
    <svg viewbox="0 0 100 70">
     <path d="M12 46Q0 58 7 64q5-9 15-8 22 11 39-2 22 4 27-17" fill="none" stroke="#8db9a0" stroke-width="14" stroke-linecap="round" /><path d="M32 42l6-12 8 9 8-12 8 10" fill="#b8d5af" stroke="#75a78a" stroke-width="2" /><path d="M66 30q-8-24 11-22 18-2 17 19 10 12-4 18-20 5-25-15Z" fill="#b8d5af" stroke="#75a78a" stroke-width="2" /><path d="M72 12l-3-9 9 7m7 0 9-7-2 13" fill="#e5cb83" stroke="#9bb487" stroke-width="2" /><g class="world-eye">
      <circle cx="81" cy="26" r="2.5" fill="#385b4d" />
     </g><circle cx="91" cy="34" r="3" fill="#e6a9a2" /><path d="M70 40q9 7 17 0" fill="none" stroke="#628d72" stroke-width="2" />
    </svg></span> <span class="world-motif world-spark">✦</span><span class="world-motif world-spark second">✧</span><span class="world-motif world-word">学习</span><span class="world-motif world-word second">你好</span><span class="world-motif world-word third">加油</span><span class="world-motif world-book">📖</span>
  </div>
  <div class="app">
   <header class="top canva-header">
    <div class="brand">
     <div class="seal" aria-hidden="true">
      中
     </div>
     <div class="brand-copy">
      <span class="brand-name">Zran4h</span><small>中文 · Learn with intention</small><span class="top-note">เรียนรู้ทีละคำ · ทบทวนอย่างมั่นใจ</span>
     </div>
    </div>
    <div class="top-right">
     <div class="language-control">
      <label for="interface-language">ภาษา / ພາສາ / 语言</label><select id="interface-language" class="canva-dropdown" aria-label="ภาษา / ພາສາ / 语言"><option value="th">ไทย</option><option value="lo">ລາວ</option><option value="zh">中文</option><option value="en">English</option></select>
     </div>
    </div>
   </header>
   <nav class="nav canva-tabs" aria-label="เมนูหลัก">
    <button class="active canva-button" data-page="dashboard"><i data-lucide="layout-dashboard"></i> ภาพรวม</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="study"><i data-lucide="layers-3"></i> Flashcards</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="library"><i data-lucide="library-big"></i> คลังคำศัพท์</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="review"><i data-lucide="rotate-ccw"></i> ต้องทบทวน</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="quiz"><i data-lucide="list-checks"></i> Quiz</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="smart"><i data-lucide="sparkles"></i> 智能复习</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="wrongbook"><i data-lucide="book-x"></i> 错题本</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="favorites"><i data-lucide="star"></i> 收藏夹</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="compare"><i data-lucide="git-compare-arrows"></i> 词语辨析</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="statistics" data-newlabel="statistics">学习统计</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="advanced" data-newlabel="advanced">高级搜索</button><button class="canva-button" data-page="management" data-newlabel="management">数据管理</button><button id="settings-nav" class="canva-button" data-page="settings"><i data-lucide="settings-2"></i> ตั้งค่า</button>
   </nav>
   <main>
    <section id="dashboard" class="page active canva-section">
     <div class="heading">
      <div><span class="eyebrow">YOUR LEARNING SPACE</span>
       <h1 id="dashboard-greeting">เรียนภาษาจีนในจังหวะของคุณ</h1>
       <p>จัดการคำศัพท์ ฝึกจำ และติดตามความก้าวหน้าในที่เดียว</p>
      </div>
     </div>
     <p id="verification-status" class="message ok canva-banner" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>
     <p id="dashboard-reminder" class="daily-reminder canva-banner hidden" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>
     <div class="grid">
      <div class="card stat canva-card">
       <div class="icon"><i data-lucide="library-big"></i>
       </div><strong id="total-count">0</strong><span>คำศัพท์ทั้งหมด</span>
      </div>
      <div class="card stat canva-card">
       <div class="icon"><i data-lucide="circle-check"></i>
       </div><strong id="known-count">0</strong><span>จำได้แล้ว</span>
      </div>
      <div class="card stat canva-card">
       <div class="icon"><i data-lucide="rotate-ccw"></i>
       </div><strong id="review-count">0</strong><span>ต้องทบทวน</span>
      </div>
     </div>
     <div class="dashboard-insights">
      <div class="panel canva-panel"><span class="eyebrow">DAILY RITUAL · 每日学习</span>
       <div class="goal-layout">
        <div class="goal-copy">
         <h2>เป้าหมายวันนี้ <span id="dash-goal-count">0 / 20</span></h2>
         <div class="track canva-progress">
          <div id="dash-goal-fill" class="fill"></div>
         </div>
         <p id="dash-tip" class="hint">เริ่มจากคำศัพท์สัก 10 คำ แล้วทบทวนอีกครั้งในตอนเย็น</p>
        </div>
        <div id="dash-goal-ring" class="goal-ring canva-progress" role="img" aria-label="ความคืบหน้าเป้าหมายวันนี้ 0 เปอร์เซ็นต์">
         <div class="goal-ring-inner"><strong id="dash-goal-percent">0%</strong><small>DAILY GOAL</small>
         </div>
        </div>
       </div>
      </div>
      <div class="panel canva-panel"><span class="eyebrow">YOUR MOMENTUM</span>
       <h2><span id="dash-streak">0</span> วันต่อเนื่อง 🔥</h2>
       <p id="dash-recent">ยังไม่มีกิจกรรมล่าสุด</p>
      </div>
     </div>
     <div class="panel canva-panel dash-week"><span class="eyebrow">THIS WEEK</span>
      <h2 id="dash-week-title">การเรียนรู้ 7 วันล่าสุด</h2>
      <div id="dash-week-chart" class="week-bars" role="img" aria-label="สถิติการเรียน 7 วันล่าสุด"></div>
     </div>
     <div class="two">
      <div class="panel canva-panel"><span class="eyebrow">GET STARTED</span>
       <h2>เริ่มเรียนรู้</h2>
       <p id="dashboard-description">ยังไม่มีข้อมูลคำศัพท์ กรุณาเพิ่มไฟล์คำศัพท์</p>
       <div class="actions"><button id="start-study" class="btn canva-button" disabled><i data-lucide="layers-3"></i> เรียน Flashcards</button><button id="start-quiz" class="btn secondary canva-button" disabled><i data-lucide="list-checks"></i> เริ่ม Quiz</button>
       </div>
       <p id="quiz-requirement" class="hint">Quiz ต้องมีคำศัพท์อย่างน้อย 4 คำ</p>
      </div>
      <div class="panel canva-panel"><span class="eyebrow">ADD VOCABULARY</span>
       <h2>เพิ่มไฟล์คำศัพท์</h2>
       <div class="upload"><label for="file-input">เลือกไฟล์ CSV, JSON หรือ XLSX</label><input id="file-input" class="canva-input" type="file" accept=".csv,.json,.xlsx,.xls">
        <p class="hint">ระบบจะตรวจคำซ้ำและเลขลำดับตกหล่นก่อนบันทึก รองรับคอลัมน์คำศัพท์และรายละเอียดตามโปรเจกต์</p>
       </div>
       <div id="import-message" class="message" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>
      </div>
     </div>
    </section>
    <section id="study" class="page canva-section">
     <div class="heading">
      <div><span class="eyebrow">LEARN &amp; REMEMBER</span>
       <h1>Flashcards</h1>
       <p>แตะการ์ดเพื่อพลิกดูความหมายและตัวอย่าง</p>
      </div>
     </div>
     <div id="study-empty" class="empty canva-panel"></div>
     <div id="study-content" class="study-layout hidden">
      <div class="toolbar"><label for="study-day">หมวดหมู่ Day</label><select id="study-day" class="canva-dropdown"></select><button id="shuffle-btn" class="btn secondary small canva-button"><i data-lucide="shuffle"></i> สุ่มลำดับ</button>
      </div>
      <div class="progress-wrap canva-progress">
       <div class="progress-label"><span id="card-position">คำที่ 1 / 1</span><span id="study-percent">0% จำได้แล้ว</span>
       </div>
       <div class="track">
        <div id="study-fill" class="fill"></div>
       </div>
      </div>
      <div id="flip-scene" class="flip-scene" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="พลิกการ์ด">
       <div id="flashcard" class="flashcard">
        <div class="face front canva-card"><span id="card-day" class="card-top"></span>
         <h2 id="card-hanzi" class="hanzi"></h2>
         <p id="card-pinyin" class="pinyin"></p><span class="tap">แตะเพื่อดูความหมาย ↻</span>
        </div>
        <div class="face back canva-card"><span id="card-type" class="card-top"></span>
         <h2 id="card-thai"></h2>
         <div id="card-detail" class="detail"></div><span class="tap">แตะเพื่อกลับด้าน ↻</span>
        </div>
       </div>
      </div>
      <div class="study-controls"><button id="prev-btn" class="btn secondary canva-button"><i data-lucide="arrow-left"></i> ก่อนหน้า</button>
       <div class="actions"><button id="speak-btn" class="btn secondary canva-button"><i data-lucide="volume-2"></i> ฟังเสียง</button>
       </div><button id="next-btn" class="btn secondary canva-button">ถัดไป <i data-lucide="arrow-right"></i></button>
      </div>
      <div class="status-actions"><button id="review-mark" class="btn secondary canva-button"><i data-lucide="rotate-ccw"></i> Need Review</button><button id="known-mark" class="btn canva-button"><i data-lucide="check"></i> Known</button><button id="favorite-mark" class="btn secondary canva-button"></button><button id="flip-button" class="btn secondary canva-button"></button><button class="btn secondary canva-button" data-rate="again" id="rate-again"></button><button class="btn secondary canva-button" data-rate="hard" id="rate-hard"></button><button class="btn secondary canva-button" data-rate="good" id="rate-good">จำได้</button><button class="btn secondary canva-button" data-rate="easy" id="rate-easy"></button>
      </div>
     </div>
    </section>
    <section id="library" class="page canva-section">
     <div class="heading">
      <div><span class="eyebrow">VOCABULARY COLLECTION</span>
       <h1>คลังคำศัพท์</h1>
       <p>ค้นหาและดูรายละเอียดคำศัพท์ทั้งหมด</p>
      </div>
     </div>
     <div class="toolbar"><label for="search-input">ค้นหา</label><input id="search-input" class="canva-input" type="search" placeholder="ค้นหาคำศัพท์ พินอิน หรือคำแปล"><label for="library-day">Day</label><select id="library-day" class="canva-dropdown"></select>
     </div>
     <div class="toolbar library-tools"><label for="library-hsk">HSK</label><select id="library-hsk" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="">ทุกระดับ</option><option>1</option><option>2</option><option>3</option><option>4</option><option>5</option><option>6</option></select><label for="library-type">ชนิดคำ</label><select id="library-type" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="">ทุกชนิด</option></select><label for="library-status">สถานะ</label><select id="library-status" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="">ทั้งหมด</option><option value="known">จำได้แล้ว</option><option value="review">ต้องทบทวน</option><option value="new">ยังไม่ได้เรียน</option></select><label for="library-sort">เรียงตาม</label><select id="library-sort" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="number">ลำดับเดิม</option><option value="alpha">พินอิน A–Z</option><option value="newest">วันที่เพิ่มล่าสุด</option></select>
     </div>
     <div class="actions library-actions"><button id="library-add" class="btn canva-button" type="button">+ เพิ่มคำศัพท์</button><button id="library-import" class="btn secondary canva-button" type="button">นำเข้า CSV / Excel</button><button id="library-export" class="btn secondary canva-button" type="button">คัดลอก CSV</button><button id="library-delete-selected" class="btn secondary canva-button" type="button" disabled>ลบที่เลือก</button>
     </div>
     <p id="library-message" class="message" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>
     <p id="library-count" class="hint"></p>
     <div id="library-list" class="vocab-list canva-list"></div>
     <div class="library-pagination"><button id="library-prev" class="btn secondary small canva-button" type="button">← ก่อนหน้า</button><span id="library-page-label"></span><button id="library-next" class="btn secondary small canva-button" type="button">ถัดไป →</button>
     </div>
     <div id="library-editor" class="panel canva-panel hidden" role="region" aria-label="แก้ไขคำศัพท์">
      <h2 id="library-editor-title">เพิ่มคำศัพท์</h2>
      <form id="library-form">
       <div id="library-fields" class="editor-grid"></div>
       <div class="actions"><button id="library-save" type="submit" class="btn canva-button">บันทึกคำศัพท์</button><button id="library-cancel" type="button" class="btn secondary canva-button">ยกเลิก</button>
       </div>
      </form>
     </div>
    </section>
    <section id="review" class="page canva-section">

       <p>กลับมาฝึกคำที่ยังไม่แม่นอีกครั้ง</p>
      </div>
     </div>

     <div id="review-list" class="vocab-list canva-list"></div>
    </section>
    <section id="quiz" class="page canva-section">
     <div class="heading">
      <div><span class="eyebrow">TEST YOURSELF</span>
       <h1>Quiz ทดสอบความจำ</h1>
       <p>เลือกคำแปลไทยที่ถูกต้องจาก 4 ตัวเลือก</p>
      </div>
     </div>
     <div id="quiz-home" class="panel canva-panel">
      <h2>พร้อมทดสอบหรือยัง?</h2>
      <p id="quiz-intro">ยังไม่มีข้อมูลคำศัพท์ กรุณาเพิ่มไฟล์คำศัพท์</p>
      <p id="last-score" class="hint"></p>
      <div class="quiz-mode-field"><label for="quiz-mode" data-i18n="quizMode">โหมดแบบทดสอบ 多种测验模式</label><select id="quiz-mode" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="meaning">中文 → ความหมาย</option><option value="reverse">ความหมาย → 中文</option><option value="audio">เสียง → 中文</option><option value="pinyin">拼音 → 中文</option><option value="pinyin-type">พิมพ์พินอิน · 输入拼音</option><option value="fill">เติมคำศัพท์</option><option value="lao">中文 → ລາວ</option><option value="sentence">เลือกประโยคที่ถูกต้อง</option><option value="comparison">เปรียบเทียบคำศัพท์</option></select>
      </div>
      <div class="toolbar quiz-settings">
       <label for="quiz-count">จำนวนข้อ</label><input id="quiz-count" class="canva-input" type="number" min="1" max="100" value="10" style="width:80px"><label for="quiz-hsk">HSK</label><select id="quiz-hsk" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="">ทั้งหมด</option><option>1</option><option>2</option><option>3</option><option>4</option><option>5</option><option>6</option></select><label for="quiz-difficulty">ความยาก</label><select id="quiz-difficulty" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="all">ทั้งหมด</option><option value="new">ใหม่</option><option value="hard">ต้องฝึก</option><option value="known">จำได้แล้ว</option></select><label for="quiz-timed">จับเวลา</label><input id="quiz-timed" class="canva-input" type="checkbox"><label for="quiz-minutes">เวลาที่กำหนด (นาที)</label><input id="quiz-minutes" class="canva-input" type="number" min="1" max="180" step="1" value="5" style="width:80px" disabled>
      </div>
      <p id="quiz-setup-message" class="hint" role="status"></p><button id="quiz-begin" class="btn canva-button" disabled>เริ่มทำ Quiz <i data-lucide="arrow-right"></i></button>
     </div>
     <div id="quiz-play" class="panel quiz-box hidden canva-panel">
      <div class="actions" style="justify-content:flex-end;margin-bottom:15px"><button id="quiz-exit" class="btn secondary small quiz-exit canva-button" type="button"><i data-lucide="log-out"></i> ออกจากการทดสอบ</button>
      </div>
      <div class="progress-label"><span id="quiz-position"></span><span id="quiz-live-score"></span><span id="quiz-clock" role="timer"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="track">
       <div id="quiz-fill" class="fill"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="quiz-word" class="quiz-prompt"></div><button id="quiz-audio" class="btn secondary canva-button hidden" type="button" data-i18n="replay">ฟังอีกครั้ง</button>
      <div id="quiz-options" class="options"></div>
      <div id="quiz-fill-answer" class="toolbar hidden"><label for="quiz-input" data-i18n="answer">คำตอบภาษาจีน</label><input id="quiz-input" class="canva-input" autocomplete="off"><button id="quiz-submit" class="btn canva-button" data-i18n="check">ตรวจคำตอบ</button>
      </div>
      <p id="quiz-feedback" class="quiz-feedback" aria-live="polite"></p><button id="quiz-next" class="btn canva-button hidden">คำถัดไป <i data-lucide="arrow-right"></i></button>
     </div>
     <div id="quiz-results" class="panel hidden canva-panel"><span class="eyebrow">RESULT</span>
      <h2 id="result-title"></h2>
      <p id="result-detail"></p>
      <div id="wrong-list" class="vocab-list"></div>
      <div class="actions" style="margin-top:20px"><button id="quiz-retry" class="btn canva-button">ทำอีกครั้ง</button><button id="review-wrongs" class="btn secondary canva-button hidden">ทบทวนคำที่ตอบผิด</button>
      </div>
     </div>
    </section>

     <div class="quiz-exit-card canva-panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="quiz-exit-title" aria-describedby="quiz-exit-description">
      <div class="quiz-exit-icon" aria-hidden="true"><i data-lucide="triangle-alert"></i>
      </div><span class="eyebrow">QUIZ IN PROGRESS</span>
      <h2 id="quiz-exit-title">ยืนยันการออกจากการทดสอบ?</h2>
      <p id="quiz-exit-description">หากออกตอนนี้ ความคืบหน้าและคะแนนของรอบนี้จะไม่ถูกบันทึก</p>
      <div class="quiz-exit-actions"><button id="quiz-exit-cancel" type="button" class="btn secondary canva-button">ทำต่อ</button><button id="quiz-exit-confirm" type="button" class="btn red canva-button">ยืนยันออก</button>
      </div>

     <div class="toolbar"><label for="advanced-query" data-i18n="search">ค้นหา</label><input id="advanced-query" class="canva-input" type="search"><label for="advanced-field" data-i18n="field">ช่องค้นหา</label><select id="advanced-field" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="all">ทั้งหมด</option><option value="汉字">中文</option><option value="拼音">拼音</option><option value="แปลไทย">ไทย</option><option value="แปลลาว">ລາວ</option></select><label for="advanced-day">Day</label><select id="advanced-day" class="canva-dropdown"></select><label for="advanced-type" data-i18n="type">ชนิดคำ</label><select id="advanced-type" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="">ทั้งหมด</option></select><label for="advanced-status" data-i18n="status">สถานะ</label><select id="advanced-status" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="all">ทั้งหมด</option><option value="known">Known</option><option value="review">Need Review</option><option value="favorite">Favorite</option><option value="smart">Smart Review</option></select>
     </div>

     <div id="advanced-results" class="vocab-list canva-list"></div>

    <section id="management" class="page canva-section">
     <div class="heading">
      <div><span class="eyebrow">YOUR DATA</span>
       <h1 data-i18n="management">数据管理</h1>
      </div>
     </div>
     <div class="panel canva-panel">
      <h2 data-i18n="import">นำเข้าคำศัพท์</h2><label for="manage-file" data-i18n="selectFile">เลือกไฟล์ Excel หรือ CSV</label><input id="manage-file" class="canva-input" type="file" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv"><label for="duplicate-action" data-i18n="duplicates">รายการที่มีเลขเดิม</label><select id="duplicate-action" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="skip">ข้าม</option><option value="update">อัปเดต</option></select>
      <p id="manage-preview" role="status"></p>
      <div id="manage-samples" class="vocab-list"></div><button id="manage-commit" class="btn canva-button hidden" data-i18n="confirmImport">ยืนยันนำเข้า</button>
      <p id="manage-message" class="message" role="status"></p>
     </div>

      <div class="actions"><button id="export-csv" class="btn secondary canva-button">CSV</button><button id="export-xlsx" class="btn secondary canva-button">Excel</button>
      </div>
     </div>

    <section id="settings" class="page canva-section" aria-labelledby="settings-title">
     <div class="heading">
      <div><span class="eyebrow">PERSONALIZE YOUR LEARNING</span>
       <h1 id="settings-title">ตั้งค่า · 设置</h1>
       <p id="settings-intro">ปรับพื้นที่เรียนรู้ให้เหมาะกับคุณ</p>
      </div>
     </div>
     <div class="settings-grid">
      <div class="panel canva-panel">
       <h2 id="settings-appearance-title"><i data-lucide="palette"></i> รูปลักษณ์และภาษา</h2><label for="settings-language" id="settings-language-label">ภาษาของเว็บไซต์</label><select id="settings-language" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="th">ไทย</option><option value="lo">ລາວ</option><option value="zh">中文</option><option value="en">English</option></select><label for="settings-theme" id="settings-theme-label">โหมดการแสดงผล</label><select id="settings-theme" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="light">โหมดสว่าง</option><option value="dark">โหมดกลางคืน</option></select><label for="world-motion" id="world-motion-label">ภาพเคลื่อนไหวพื้นหลัง</label><select id="world-motion" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="on">เปิด</option><option value="off">ปิด</option></select>
      </div>
      <div class="panel canva-panel">
