  const bundled = (bundledText+'\n'+additionalText+'\n'+newDaysText+'\n'+attachedDaysText+'\n'+latestDaysText+'\n'+finalDaysText+'\n'+importedDaysText+'\n'+day22to24Text+'\n'+day25to27Text+'\n'+day28to30Text+'\n'+day31to33Text+'\n'+day34to36Text).split('\n').map(line=>{const [id,word,pinyin,type,thai,example1,thai1,example2,thai2]=line.split('|');return {'序号':id,Day:'Day'+String(Math.floor((Number(id)-3601)/50)+1).padStart(2,'0'),HSK:'6','汉字':word,'拼音':pinyin,'词性':type,'แปลไทย':thai,'ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน':example1,'แปลประโยคไทย':thai1,'ตัวอย่างประโยค 2':example2,'แปลไทย 2':thai2,'中文解释':'','พินอินประโยค':'','近义词':'','反义词':'','词语搭配':''}});
  const bundleIssues=[];if(bundled.length!==1800)bundleIssues.push('จำนวนรายการไม่ครบ 1800');for(const [day,start] of [['Day22',4651],['Day23',4701],['Day24',4751],['Day25',4801],['Day26',4851],['Day27',4901],['Day28',4951],['Day29',5001],['Day30',5051],['Day31',5101],['Day32',5151],['Day33',5201],['Day34',5251],['Day35',5301],['Day36',5351]]){const rows=bundled.filter(w=>w.Day===day);if(rows.length!==50)bundleIssues.push(day+' มี '+rows.length+' รายการ (ต้องมี 50)');for(let n=start;n<start+50;n++)if(!rows.some(w=>Number(w['序号'])===n))bundleIssues.push(day+' ขาดเลข '+n)}const ids=new Set(), duplicateWords=[];const seenWords=new Map();bundled.forEach(w=>{if(ids.has(w['序号']))bundleIssues.push('เลขลำดับซ้ำ '+w['序号']);ids.add(w['序号']);if(!w['汉字']||!w['拼音']||!w['แปลไทย']||!w['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน']||!w['แปลประโยคไทย']||!w['ตัวอย่างประโยค 2']||!w['แปลไทย 2'])bundleIssues.push('ข้อมูลไม่ครบ '+w['序号']);if(seenWords.has(w['汉字']))duplicateWords.push(`${w['汉字']} (#${seenWords.get(w['汉字'])}, #${w['序号']})`);else seenWords.set(w['汉字'],w['序号'])});for(let n=3601;n<=5400;n++)if(!ids.has(String(n)))bundleIssues.push('ขาดเลข '+n);
  const aliases = {'序号':['序号','ลำดับ'],'Day':['Day','day'],'汉字':['汉字','词语'],'拼音':['拼音'],'词性':['词性'],'แปลไทย':['แปลไทย'],'中文解释':['中文解释'],'ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน':['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน','ตัวอย่างประโยค 1'],'พินอินประโยค':['พินอินประโยค'],'แปลประโยคไทย':['แปลประโยคไทย','แปลไทย 1'],'近义词':['近义词'],'反义词':['反义词'],'词语搭配':['词语搭配'],'ตัวอย่างประโยค 2':['ตัวอย่างประโยค 2'],'แปลไทย 2':['แปลไทย 2']};
  let records=[], ready=true, page='dashboard', order=[], index=0, quiz=[], qi=0, score=0, wrong=[], answered=false, busy=false;
  const words=()=>{if(bundleIssues.length)return [];const saved=records.filter(r=>r.record_type==='word').map(r=>{try{return {...JSON.parse(r.payload),_record:r}}catch{return null}}).filter(Boolean);const byId=new Map(bundled.map(w=>[String(w['序号']),w]));saved.forEach(w=>{if(w._record.status==='deleted')byId.delete(String(w['序号']));else byId.set(String(w['序号']),{...byId.get(String(w['序号'])),...w,HSK:w.HSK||w['ระดับ HSK']||byId.get(String(w['序号']))?.HSK||''})});return [...byId.values()].sort((a,b)=>Number(a['序号'])-Number(b['序号']))};
  // Read-only bridge for the Practice add-on; the vocabulary source is never mutated.
  window.zranPracticeWords=()=>words();
  const marks=()=>records.filter(r=>r.record_type==='mark');
  const markFor=w=>marks().find(r=>r.record_key===String(w['序号']));
  const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const empty=(text=EMPTY)=>`<div class="empty canva-panel"><div class="empty-icon"><i data-lucide="book-open"></i></div><h3>${esc(text)}</h3><p>เพิ่มไฟล์คำศัพท์ที่หน้า “ภาพรวม” เพื่อเริ่มใช้งาน</p></div>`;
  function icons(){if(window.lucide)lucide.createIcons()}
  function message(text,ok=false){$('import-message').textContent=text;$('import-message').className='message'+(ok?' ok':'')}
  let pendingQuizExit=null,quizExitReturnFocus=null;
  function askQuizExit(to){pendingQuizExit=to;quizExitReturnFocus=document.activeElement;$('quiz-exit-overlay').classList.remove('hidden');$('quiz-exit-cancel').focus()}
  function closeQuizExit(){pendingQuizExit=null;$('quiz-exit-overlay').classList.add('hidden');quizExitReturnFocus?.focus()}
  function navigate(to){if(page==='quiz'&&to!=='quiz'&&!$('quiz-play').classList.contains('hidden')){askQuizExit(to);return}page=to;document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',el.id===to));document.querySelectorAll('.nav button').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',el.dataset.page===to));render();window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'})}
  $('quiz-exit').onclick=()=>askQuizExit('quiz-home');$('quiz-exit-cancel').onclick=closeQuizExit;
  $('quiz-exit-confirm').onclick=()=>{let destination=pendingQuizExit;pendingQuizExit=null;$('quiz-exit-overlay').classList.add('hidden');clearInterval(quizTimer);quizTimer=null;if('speechSynthesis'in window)speechSynthesis.cancel();$('quiz-play').classList.add('hidden');$('quiz-home').classList.remove('hidden');quiz=[];qi=0;score=0;wrong=[];if(destination&&destination!=='quiz-home')navigate(destination);else $('quiz-begin').focus()};
  $('quiz-exit-overlay').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===$('quiz-exit-overlay'))closeQuizExit()});document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if($('quiz-exit-overlay').classList.contains('hidden'))return;if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();closeQuizExit()}if(e.key==='Tab'){let buttons=[$('quiz-exit-cancel'),$('quiz-exit-confirm')],i=buttons.indexOf(document.activeElement);if(e.shiftKey&&i===0){e.preventDefault();buttons[1].focus()}else if(!e.shiftKey&&i===1){e.preventDefault();buttons[0].focus()}}});
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>navigate(b.dataset.page)));
  function dayOptions(select){let current=select.value;let days=[...new Set(words().map(w=>String(w.Day||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,undefined,{numeric:true}));select.innerHTML='<option value="">ทุก Day</option>'+days.map(d=>`<option value="${esc(d)}">${esc(d)}</option>`).join('');select.value=days.includes(current)?current:''}
  function render(){
    $('verification-status').textContent=bundleIssues.length?'⚠ ตรวจสอบข้อมูลไม่ผ่าน: '+bundleIssues.slice(0,5).join(', '):`✓ ข้อมูลจากไฟล์ XLSX ที่แนบ: Day34 ${bundled.filter(w=>w.Day==='Day34').length} รายการ (5251–5300), Day35 ${bundled.filter(w=>w.Day==='Day35').length} รายการ (5301–5350), Day36 ${bundled.filter(w=>w.Day==='Day36').length} รายการ (5351–5400) · เพิ่ม 150 รายการ · Day01–Day33 เดิม 1650 รายการ · รวม ${words().length} รายการ · เลข 序号 ซ้ำ 0 · คำศัพท์ซ้ำ ${duplicateWords.length}${duplicateWords.length?' ('+duplicateWords.join(', ')+')':''} · ช่องที่ไม่มีในไฟล์เว้นว่าง`;
    let ws=words(), known=marks().filter(m=>m.status==='known'&&words().some(w=>String(w['序号'])===m.record_key)).length, rev=marks().filter(m=>m.status==='review'&&words().some(w=>String(w['序号'])===m.record_key)).length;
    $('total-count').textContent=ws.filter(w=>markFor(w)||extraFor(w).daily).length;$('known-count').textContent=known;$('review-count').textContent=rev+dueWords().filter(w=>markFor(w)?.status!=='review').length;
    let hour=new Date().getHours();$('dashboard-greeting').textContent=(hour<12?'สวัสดีตอนเช้า':hour<17?'สวัสดีตอนบ่าย':'สวัสดีตอนเย็น')+' · เรียนรู้ในจังหวะของคุณ';let activity={};ws.forEach(w=>Object.entries(extraFor(w).daily||{}).forEach(([d,v])=>{(activity[d]??=new Set()).add(String(w['序号']))}));let goal=Number(records.find(r=>r.record_type==='setting'&&r.record_key==='goal')?.payload)||20,today=localDay(),done=activity[today]?.size||0;$('dash-goal-count').textContent=`${done} / ${goal}`;let goalPercent=Math.min(100,Math.round(done/goal*100));$('dash-goal-fill').style.width=goalPercent+'%';$('dash-goal-ring').style.setProperty('--progress',goalPercent+'%');$('dash-goal-percent').textContent=goalPercent+'%';$('dash-goal-ring').setAttribute('aria-label',`ความคืบหน้าเป้าหมายวันนี้ ${goalPercent} เปอร์เซ็นต์`);let streak=0,d=new Date();if(!activity[today])d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);while(activity[[d.getFullYear(),String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')].join('-')]){streak++;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1)}$('dash-streak').textContent=streak;let last=Object.keys(activity).sort().at(-1);$('dash-recent').textContent=last?`ล่าสุด ${last} · ${activity[last].size} คำ`:'ยังไม่มีกิจกรรมล่าสุด';$('dash-tip').textContent=done>=goal?'ยอดเยี่ยม! ทบทวนคำที่ยังไม่แม่นเพื่อรักษาความต่อเนื่อง':'เรียนทีละน้อยอย่างสม่ำเสมอ แล้วกลับมาทบทวนคำที่ยังไม่แม่น';let dates=Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>{let x=new Date();x.setDate(x.getDate()-(6-i));return [x.getFullYear(),String(x.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),String(x.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')].join('-')});$('dash-week-chart').innerHTML=dates.map(day=>`<div class="week-item"><strong>${activity[day]?.size||0}</strong><div class="week-bar" style="height:${Math.max(5,Math.min(76,(activity[day]?.size||0)/goal*76))}px"></div>${day.slice(5)}</div>`).join('');
    $('dashboard-description').textContent=ws.length?`มีคำศัพท์ ${ws.length} คำ พร้อมเริ่มเรียนและทบทวน`:EMPTY;
    $('start-study').disabled=!ready||!ws.length;$('start-quiz').disabled=!ready||ws.length<4;$('quiz-begin').disabled=!ready||ws.length<4;
    $('quiz-intro').textContent=!ws.length?EMPTY:ws.length<4?'ต้องมีคำศัพท์อย่างน้อย 4 คำเพื่อสร้างตัวเลือก Quiz':`มีคำศัพท์ ${ws.length} คำ พร้อมทดสอบความจำ`;
    $('quiz-requirement').textContent=ws.length<4?'Quiz ต้องมีคำศัพท์อย่างน้อย 4 คำ':'Quiz พร้อมใช้งาน';
    const latest=records.filter(r=>r.record_type==='score').sort((a,b)=>String(b.created_at).localeCompare(String(a.created_at)))[0];
    $('last-score').textContent=latest?`คะแนนครั้งล่าสุด: ${latest.score}/${latest.payload}`:'ยังไม่มีคะแนน Quiz';
    dayOptions($('study-day'));dayOptions($('library-day'));
    if(!order.length&&ws.length)order=ws.map(w=>String(w['序号']));
    if(page==='study')renderStudy();
    if(page==='library')renderLibrary();
    if(page==='review')renderReview();
    if(['smart','wrongbook','favorites','compare'].includes(page))renderExtra();
    updateCardExtras();icons();
  }
  function filteredOrder(){let ws=words(),day=$('study-day').value;return order.map(id=>ws.find(w=>String(w['序号'])===id)).filter(w=>w&&(!day||String(w.Day)===day))}
  function current(){return filteredOrder()[index]}
  function renderStudy(){
    let list=filteredOrder();$('study-empty').classList.toggle('hidden',!!list.length);$('study-content').classList.toggle('hidden',!list.length);
    if(!list.length){$('study-empty').innerHTML=empty(words().length?'ไม่มีคำศัพท์ใน Day นี้':EMPTY);return}
    index=Math.min(index,list.length-1);let w=list[index];$('flashcard').classList.remove('flipped');
    $('card-day').textContent=`${w.Day||'—'} · #${w['序号']}`;$('card-hanzi').textContent=w['汉字'];$('card-pinyin').textContent=w['拼音'];$('card-type').textContent=w['词性']||'คำศัพท์';$('card-thai').textContent=w['แปลไทย'];
    let detail=[['ຄຳແປພາສາລາວ',w['แปลลาว']||w['ຄຳແປລາວ']||'—'],['拼音',w['拼音']],['词性',w['词性']||'—'],['中文解释',w['中文解释']||'—'],['ตัวอย่างประโยค',w['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน']],['พินอินประโยค',w['พินอินประโยค']],['แปลไทย 1',w['แปลประโยคไทย']],['ແປປະໂຫຍກລາວ',w['แปลประโยคลาว']||'—'],['ตัวอย่างประโยค 2',w['ตัวอย่างประโยค 2']],['แปลไทย 2',w['แปลไทย 2']],['近义词',w['近义词']],['反义词',w['反义词']],['词语搭配',w['词语搭配']]].filter(x=>x[1]).map(x=>`<div><b>${esc(x[0])}:</b> ${esc(x[1])}</div>`).join('');
    $('card-detail').innerHTML=detail;$('card-position').textContent=`คำที่ ${index+1} / ${list.length}`;
    let pct=Math.round(list.filter(x=>markFor(x)?.status==='known').length/list.length*100);$('study-percent').textContent=`${pct}% จำได้แล้ว`;$('study-fill').style.width=pct+'%';
    $('prev-btn').disabled=index===0;$('next-btn').disabled=index===list.length-1;
    $('known-mark').textContent=markFor(w)?.status==='known'?'✓ Known แล้ว':'✓ Known';
    $('review-mark').textContent=markFor(w)?.status==='review'?'↻ อยู่ในรายการทบทวน':'↻ Need Review';
    updateCardExtras();icons();
  }
  function row(w,review=false){return `<article class="card vocab-row canva-card"><div><span class="tag canva-tag">${esc(w.Day||'—')} · #${esc(w['序号'])}</span><h3>${esc(w['汉字'])}</h3><p>${esc(w['拼音'])} · ${esc(w['词性'])} · ${esc(w['แปลไทย'])}</p></div><button class="btn secondary small canva-button" data-open="${esc(w['序号'])}">${review?'ทบทวนคำนี้':'เปิด Flashcard'} →</button>${review?`<button type="button" class="btn small canva-button" data-review-known="${esc(w['序号'])}" aria-label="รู้คำนี้แล้ว ${esc(w['汉字'])}">รู้คำนี้แล้ว</button>`:''}<button class="btn secondary small canva-button" data-favorite="${esc(w['序号'])}" aria-label="Favorite ${esc(w['汉字'])}">${extraFor(w).favorite?'★':'☆'}</button></article>`}
  let libraryPage=0,selectedWords=new Set(),editingId=null;const pageSize=20;
  function renderLibrary(){let q=$('search-input').value.trim().toLowerCase(),day=$('library-day').value,type=$('library-type').value,types=[...new Set(words().map(w=>w['词性']).filter(Boolean))].sort();$('library-type').innerHTML='<option value="">ทุกชนิด</option>'+types.map(t=>`<option value="${esc(t)}">${esc(t)}</option>`).join('');$('library-type').value=type;let ws=words().filter(w=>(!day||String(w.Day)===day)&&(!type||w['词性']===type)&&(!$('library-hsk').value||String(w.HSK||w['ระดับ HSK']||'')===$('library-hsk').value)&&(!$('library-status').value||($('library-status').value==='new'?!markFor(w):markFor(w)?.status===$('library-status').value))&&(!q||[w['汉字'],w['拼音'],w['แปลไทย'],w['แปลลาว'],w['ຄຳແປລາວ'],w['序号']].some(v=>String(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))));let sort=$('library-sort').value;if(sort==='alpha')ws.sort((a,b)=>String(a['拼音']).localeCompare(String(b['拼音'])));if(sort==='newest')ws.sort((a,b)=>String(b._record?.created_at||'').localeCompare(String(a._record?.created_at||''))||Number(b['序号'])-Number(a['序号']));libraryPage=Math.min(libraryPage,Math.max(0,Math.ceil(ws.length/pageSize)-1));$('library-count').textContent=`พบ ${ws.length} คำ · เลือก ${selectedWords.size} คำ`;$('library-page-label').textContent=`หน้า ${libraryPage+1} / ${Math.max(1,Math.ceil(ws.length/pageSize))}`;$('library-prev').disabled=libraryPage===0;$('library-next').disabled=(libraryPage+1)*pageSize>=ws.length;$('library-delete-selected').disabled=!selectedWords.size;$('library-list').innerHTML=ws.length?ws.slice(libraryPage*pageSize,(libraryPage+1)*pageSize).map(w=>`<article class="card vocab-row canva-card"><input class="row-select canva-input" type="checkbox" data-select="${esc(w['序号'])}" aria-label="เลือก ${esc(w['汉字'])}" ${selectedWords.has(String(w['序号']))?'checked':''}><div class="row-main"><span class="tag canva-tag">${esc(w.Day||'—')} · ${esc(w.HSK?'HSK '+w.HSK:'HSK —')} · #${esc(w['序号'])}</span><h3>${esc(w['汉字'])}</h3><p>${esc(w['拼音'])} · ${esc(w['词性']||'—')} · ${esc(w['แปลไทย'])} · ${esc(w['แปลลาว']||'')}</p></div><div class="actions"><button class="btn secondary small canva-button" data-open="${esc(w['序号'])}">Flashcard</button><button class="btn secondary small canva-button" data-favorite="${esc(w['序号'])}" aria-label="Favorite ${esc(w['汉字'])}">${extraFor(w).favorite?'★':'☆'}</button><button class="btn secondary small canva-button" data-edit="${esc(w['序号'])}">แก้ไข</button><button class="btn secondary small canva-button" data-remove="${esc(w['序号'])}">ลบ</button></div></article>`).join(''):empty(words().length?'ไม่พบคำศัพท์ที่ตรงกับการค้นหา':EMPTY);icons()}
  function renderReview(){let ws=words().filter(w=>markFor(w)?.status==='review');$('review-list').innerHTML=ws.length?ws.map(w=>row(w,true)).join(''):empty(words().length?'ยังไม่มีคำที่ต้องทบทวน':EMPTY);icons()}
  $('review-list').addEventListener('click',async e=>{const button=e.target.closest('[data-review-known]');if(!button)return;const id=button.dataset.reviewKnown,w=words().find(word=>String(word['序号'])===id);if(!w)return;const existing=markFor(w);if(!existing||existing.status!=='review'){renderReview();return}button.disabled=true;$('review-message').textContent='';try{const data={...existing,status:'known'},result=await window.dataSdk.update(data);if(!result.isOk){$('review-message').textContent='ไม่สามารถยืนยันการบันทึกได้ โปรดรีเฟรชก่อนลองอีกครั้ง';return}records=records.map(record=>record.__backendId===existing.__backendId?data:record);document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('zran-mastered',{detail:id}));render()}catch(error){$('review-message').textContent='บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ โปรดลองอีกครั้ง'}finally{button.disabled=false}});
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{let b=e.target.closest('button[data-open]');if(!b)return;let id=String(b.dataset.open),source=b.closest('#library-list,#review-list,#smart-list,#wrongbook-list,#favorites-list,#advanced-results');if(!source)return;let available=new Set(words().map(w=>String(w['序号'])));let ids=[...source.querySelectorAll('button[data-open]')].map(button=>String(button.dataset.open)).filter(key=>available.has(key));let target=ids.indexOf(id);if(target<0)return;$('study-day').value='';order=ids;index=target;navigate('study')});
  const editFields=[['序号','ลำดับ'],['Day','Day'],['HSK','HSK'],['汉字','ตัวอักษรจีน'],['拼音','พินอิน'],['แปลไทย','ความหมายไทย'],['แปลลาว','ความหมายลาว'],['中文解释','คำอธิบายภาษาจีน'],['词性','ชนิดคำ'],['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน','ตัวอย่างภาษาจีน'],['พินอินประโยค','พินอินประโยค'],['แปลประโยคไทย','คำแปลตัวอย่างไทย'],['แปลประโยคลาว','คำแปลตัวอย่างลาว']];
  function openEditor(w){editingId=w?String(w['序号']):null;$('library-editor-title').textContent=w?'แก้ไข '+w['汉字']:'เพิ่มคำศัพท์';$('library-fields').innerHTML=editFields.map(([key,label],i)=>`<label for="edit-field-${i}">${label}<input id="edit-field-${i}" class="canva-input" name="${esc(key)}" value="${esc(w?.[key]||'')}" ${key==='序号'&&w?'readonly':''} ${['序号','汉字','แปลไทย'].includes(key)?'required':''}></label>`).join('');$('library-editor').classList.remove('hidden');$('library-editor').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'})}
  $('library-add').onclick=()=>openEditor(null);$('library-cancel').onclick=()=>$('library-editor').classList.add('hidden');$('library-import').onclick=()=>{navigate('management');$('manage-file').focus()};$('library-export').onclick=()=>{let text=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_csv(XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(words().map(w=>Object.fromEntries(editFields.map(([key])=>[key,w[key]||''])))));let area=document.createElement('textarea');area.value=text;document.body.appendChild(area);area.select();let ok=document.execCommand('copy');area.remove();$('library-message').textContent=ok?'คัดลอก CSV แล้ว วางใน Excel หรือไฟล์ CSV ได้':'คัดลอกไม่สำเร็จ โปรดลองอีกครั้ง'};
  $('library-form').onsubmit=async e=>{e.preventDefault();let button=$('library-save');if(button.disabled)return;let w=Object.fromEntries(editFields.map(([key],i)=>[key,$('edit-field-'+i).value.trim()])),id=w['序号'];if(!/^\d+$/.test(id)||Number(id)<1){$('library-message').textContent='ลำดับต้องเป็นจำนวนเต็มบวก';return}if(!editingId&&words().some(x=>String(x['序号'])===id)){$('library-message').textContent='เลขลำดับนี้มีอยู่แล้ว';return}let old=records.find(r=>r.record_type==='word'&&r.record_key===id);if(!old&&records.length>=999){$('library-message').textContent='Canva Sheet เต็ม (999 รายการ)';return}let base=words().find(x=>String(x['序号'])===id),data=old?{...old,payload:JSON.stringify({...base,...w}),status:''}:{record_type:'word',record_key:id,payload:JSON.stringify({...base,...w}),status:'',score:0,created_at:new Date().toISOString()};button.disabled=true;try{let result=await(old?window.dataSdk.update(data):window.dataSdk.create(data));if(!result.isOk)throw Error('ไม่สามารถยืนยันการบันทึกได้ โปรดรีเฟรชก่อนลองใหม่');$('library-message').textContent='บันทึกคำศัพท์แล้ว';$('library-editor').classList.add('hidden');if(old)records=records.map(r=>r.__backendId===old.__backendId?data:r);else records.push(data);render()}catch(err){$('library-message').textContent=err.message}finally{button.disabled=false}};
  async function removeWord(id,button){let w=words().find(x=>String(x['序号'])===id);if(!w)return;let old=records.find(r=>r.record_type==='word'&&r.record_key===id),data=old?{...old,status:'deleted'}:{record_type:'word',record_key:id,payload:JSON.stringify(w),status:'deleted',score:0,created_at:new Date().toISOString()};if(!old&&records.length>=999){$('library-message').textContent='Canva Sheet เต็ม';return false}button.disabled=true;try{let result=await(old?window.dataSdk.update(data):window.dataSdk.create(data));if(!result.isOk)throw Error('ไม่สามารถยืนยันการลบได้ โปรดรีเฟรชก่อนลองใหม่');if(old)records=records.map(r=>r.__backendId===old.__backendId?data:r);else records.push(data);selectedWords.delete(id);render();return true}catch(err){$('library-message').textContent=err.message;return false}finally{button.disabled=false}}
  $('library-list').onchange=e=>{let box=e.target.closest('[data-select]');if(!box)return;box.checked?selectedWords.add(box.dataset.select):selectedWords.delete(box.dataset.select);$('library-delete-selected').disabled=!selectedWords.size;$('library-count').textContent=$('library-count').textContent.replace(/เลือก \d+ คำ/,`เลือก ${selectedWords.size} คำ`)};$('library-list').onclick=e=>{let edit=e.target.closest('[data-edit]'),del=e.target.closest('[data-remove]');if(edit)openEditor(words().find(w=>String(w['序号'])===edit.dataset.edit));if(del){if(del.dataset.confirm!=='yes'){del.dataset.confirm='yes';del.textContent='ยืนยันลบ?';setTimeout(()=>{if(del.isConnected){del.dataset.confirm='';del.textContent='ลบ'}},4000)}else removeWord(del.dataset.remove,del)}};
  $('library-delete-selected').onclick=async e=>{let b=e.currentTarget;if(b.dataset.confirm!=='yes'){b.dataset.confirm='yes';b.textContent=`ยืนยันลบ ${selectedWords.size} คำ?`;return}b.dataset.confirm='';let ids=[...selectedWords];for(let id of ids){let row=$('library-list').querySelector(`[data-remove="${id}"]`);if(!row){row=document.createElement('button')}if(!await removeWord(id,row))break;await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,550))}b.textContent='ลบที่เลือก'};$('library-prev').onclick=()=>{libraryPage--;renderLibrary()};$('library-next').onclick=()=>{libraryPage++;renderLibrary()};document.querySelectorAll('#library .toolbar input,#library .toolbar select').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener(el.tagName==='INPUT'?'input':'change',()=>{libraryPage=0;renderLibrary()}));
  $('search-input').addEventListener('input',renderLibrary);$('library-day').addEventListener('change',renderLibrary);$('study-day').addEventListener('change',()=>{index=0;renderStudy()});
  $('start-study').onclick=()=>{let mode=$('settings-mode').value;if(mode==='due'&&dueWords().length){startSubset(dueWords());return}if(mode==='shuffle'){order=shuffled(words().map(w=>String(w['序号'])));index=0;$('study-day').value=''}navigate('study')};$('start-quiz').onclick=()=>{navigate('quiz');beginQuiz()};
  $('flip-scene').onclick=()=>{if(current())$('flashcard').classList.toggle('flipped')};
  $('flip-scene').onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();$('flip-scene').click()}};
  $('prev-btn').onclick=()=>{index--;renderStudy()};$('next-btn').onclick=()=>{index++;renderStudy()};
  $('shuffle-btn').onclick=()=>{for(let i=order.length-1;i>0;i--){let j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[order[i],order[j]]=[order[j],order[i]]}index=0;renderStudy()};
  $('speak-btn').onclick=()=>{let w=current();if(!w)return;if(!('speechSynthesis'in window)){message('เบราว์เซอร์นี้ไม่รองรับเสียงอ่าน');return}speechSynthesis.cancel();let u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(w['汉字']);u.lang='zh-CN';u.rate=Number($('settings-voice').value)||.85;speechSynthesis.speak(u)};
  async function saveMark(status,button){let w=current();if(!w||busy)return;busy=true;button.disabled=true;let existing=markFor(w),data=existing?{...existing,status}:{record_type:'mark',record_key:String(w['序号']),payload:'',status,score:0,created_at:new Date().toISOString()};try{if(!existing&&records.length>=999){message('จำนวนรายการใน Canva Sheet ถึงขีดจำกัดแล้ว');return}let r=await(existing?window.dataSdk.update(data):window.dataSdk.create(data));if(!r.isOk){message('ไม่สามารถยืนยันการบันทึกได้ โปรดรีเฟรชก่อนลองอีกครั้ง');return}if(existing)records=records.map(x=>x.__backendId===existing.__backendId?data:x);else records.push(data);trackActivity(w,null,false);if(status==='known')document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('zran-mastered',{detail:String(w['序号'])}));render()}finally{busy=false;button.disabled=false}}
  $('known-mark').onclick=e=>saveMark('known',e.currentTarget);$('review-mark').onclick=e=>saveMark('review',e.currentTarget);
  function shuffled(a){let x=[...a];for(let i=x.length-1;i>0;i--){let j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[x[i],x[j]]=[x[j],x[i]]}return x}
  let quizTimer=null,quizStarted=0,quizSource=null,quizDeadline=0,quizAnswers=[];
  $('quiz-timed').onchange=()=>{$('quiz-minutes').disabled=!$('quiz-timed').checked};
  function quizRemaining(){return Math.max(0,Math.ceil((quizDeadline-Date.now())/1000))}
  function updateQuizClock(){if(!quizDeadline)return;let left=quizRemaining();$('quiz-clock').textContent=`เหลือ ${Math.floor(left/60)}:${String(left%60).padStart(2,'0')}`;if(left===0)finishQuiz(true)}
  function beginQuiz(){let pool=quizSource||words(),hsk=$('quiz-hsk').value,difficulty=$('quiz-difficulty').value;pool=pool.filter(w=>(!hsk||String(w.HSK||w['ระดับ HSK']||'')===hsk)&&(difficulty==='all'||difficulty==='new'&&!extraFor(w).studyCount||difficulty==='hard'&&(extraFor(w).wrongActive||extraFor(w).level==='hard')||difficulty==='known'&&extraFor(w).correctCount>0));if(!pool.length){$('quiz-setup-message').textContent=`ยังไม่มีคำศัพท์สำหรับ HSK ${hsk||'ทุกระดับ'} ในหมวดความยากที่เลือก กรุณาเปลี่ยนหมวดความยากหรือระดับ HSK`;return}if($('quiz-mode').value==='lao')pool=pool.filter(w=>w['แปลลาว']||w['ຄຳແປລາວ']);if($('quiz-mode').value==='sentence')pool=pool.filter(w=>w['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน']);if(!pool.length){$('quiz-setup-message').textContent='รูปแบบนี้ต้องมีข้อมูลคำแปลหรือประโยคก่อน';return}if($('quiz-timed').checked&&(!Number.isInteger(Number($('quiz-minutes').value))||Number($('quiz-minutes').value)<1||Number($('quiz-minutes').value)>180)){$('quiz-setup-message').textContent='กรุณากำหนดเวลาระหว่าง 1–180 นาที';$('quiz-minutes').focus();return}quiz=shuffled(pool).slice(0,Math.max(1,Math.min(100,Number($('quiz-count').value)||10)));quizSource=null;qi=0;score=0;wrong=[];quizAnswers=[];quizStarted=Date.now();clearInterval(quizTimer);quizDeadline=$('quiz-timed').checked?quizStarted+Number($('quiz-minutes').value)*60000:0;$('quiz-clock').textContent='';$('quiz-setup-message').textContent='';$('quiz-home').classList.add('hidden');$('quiz-results').classList.add('hidden');$('quiz-play').classList.remove('hidden');renderQuestion();if(quizDeadline){updateQuizClock();quizTimer=setInterval(updateQuizClock,250)}}
  $('quiz-begin').onclick=beginQuiz;$('quiz-retry').onclick=beginQuiz;
  function renderQuestion(){answered=false;let w=quiz[qi],mode=$('quiz-mode').value;$('quiz-position').textContent=`ข้อ ${qi+1} / ${quiz.length}`;$('quiz-live-score').textContent=`คะแนน ${score}`;$('quiz-fill').style.width=Math.round(qi/quiz.length*100)+'%';$('quiz-word').textContent=mode==='audio'?'🔊':mode==='pinyin'?w['拼音']:mode==='pinyin-type'?w['汉字']:mode==='reverse'||mode==='fill'?w['แปลไทย']:mode==='sentence'?'เลือกประโยคที่มี '+w['汉字']:mode==='comparison'?'เลือกคำที่มีความหมาย '+w['แปลไทย']:w['汉字'];$('quiz-feedback').textContent='';$('quiz-feedback').className='quiz-feedback';$('quiz-next').classList.add('hidden');$('quiz-audio').classList.toggle('hidden',mode!=='audio');$('quiz-fill-answer').classList.toggle('hidden',mode!=='fill');$('quiz-input').value='';let key=mode==='meaning'?'แปลไทย':mode==='lao'?'แปลลาว':mode==='sentence'?'ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน':'汉字',value=o=>mode==='lao'?(o['แปลลาว']||o['ຄຳແປລາວ']):o[key],others=shuffled(words().filter(x=>value(x)&&value(x)!==value(w)&&String(x['序号'])!==String(w['序号']))).slice(0,3);$('quiz-options').innerHTML=mode==='fill'?'':shuffled([w,...others]).map(o=>`<button class="option canva-button" data-answer="${esc(o['序号'])}">${esc(value(o))}</button>`).join('');if(mode==='audio')playQuizAudio()}
  function submitQuizAnswer(correct,b){if(answered||$('quiz-play').classList.contains('hidden'))return;if(quizDeadline&&quizRemaining()===0){finishQuiz(true);return}answered=true;let w=quiz[qi],mode=$('quiz-mode').value,correctText=mode==='meaning'?w['แปลไทย']:mode==='lao'?(w['แปลลาว']||w['ຄຳແປລາວ']):mode==='sentence'?w['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน']:w['汉字'],given=mode==='fill'?$('quiz-input').value.trim():b?.textContent||'';quizAnswers.push({number:qi+1,word:w['汉字'],given,correctText,correct});if(correct)score++;else wrong.push(w);document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('zran-quiz-answer',{detail:{mode,correct}}));recordAnswer(w,correct);trackActivity(w,correct,true);document.querySelectorAll('.option').forEach(x=>{x.disabled=true;if(x.dataset.answer===String(w['序号']))x.classList.add('correct');else if(x===b)x.classList.add('wrong')});$('quiz-feedback').textContent=correct?'ถูกต้อง!':'ยังไม่ถูก — คำตอบคือ '+($('quiz-mode').value==='meaning'?w['แปลไทย']:$('quiz-mode').value==='lao'?(w['แปลลาว']||w['ຄຳແປລາວ']):$('quiz-mode').value==='sentence'?w['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน']:w['汉字'])+' · '+(w['中文解释']||w['แปลไทย']||'')+' · '+(w['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน']||'');$('quiz-feedback').classList.toggle('wrong',!correct);$('quiz-live-score').textContent=`คะแนน ${score}`;$('quiz-next').textContent=qi===quiz.length-1?'ดูผลคะแนน →':'คำถัดไป →';$('quiz-next').classList.remove('hidden')}
  $('quiz-options').onclick=e=>{let b=e.target.closest('[data-answer]');if(b)submitQuizAnswer(b.dataset.answer===String(quiz[qi]['序号']),b)};
  $('quiz-submit').onclick=()=>submitQuizAnswer($('quiz-input').value.trim()===quiz[qi]['汉字']);$('quiz-input').onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')$('quiz-submit').click()};function playQuizAudio(){if(!('speechSynthesis'in window))return;speechSynthesis.cancel();let u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(quiz[qi]['汉字']);u.lang='zh-CN';u.rate=Number($('settings-voice').value)||.85;speechSynthesis.speak(u)}$('quiz-audio').onclick=playQuizAudio;
  $('quiz-next').onclick=()=>{qi++;if(qi<quiz.length)renderQuestion();else finishQuiz()};
  async function finishQuiz(timedOut=false){clearInterval(quizTimer);quizTimer=null;if($('quiz-play').classList.contains('hidden'))return;quizDeadline=0;if('speechSynthesis'in window)speechSynthesis.cancel();$('quiz-play').classList.add('hidden');$('quiz-results').classList.remove('hidden');let missed=quiz.length-quizAnswers.length;$('result-title').textContent=timedOut?'หมดเวลา · สรุปผลการทดสอบ':`${score} / ${quiz.length} คะแนน`;$('result-detail').textContent=`${$('quiz-mode').selectedOptions[0].textContent} · ถูก ${score} ข้อ · ผิด ${wrong.length} ข้อ · ยังไม่ได้ตอบ ${missed} ข้อ · รวม ${quiz.length} ข้อ · ใช้เวลา ${Math.floor((Date.now()-quizStarted)/1000)} วินาที`;$('wrong-list').innerHTML=quizAnswers.filter(a=>!a.correct).map(a=>`<article class="card vocab-row canva-card"><div><span class="tag canva-tag">ข้อ ${a.number} · ผิด</span><h3>${esc(a.word)}</h3><p>ตอบ: ${esc(a.given||'ไม่ได้ระบุ')} · คำตอบที่ถูก: ${esc(a.correctText)}</p></div></article>`).join('')+(missed?`<p class="hint">ข้อ ${quizAnswers.length+1}–${quiz.length} ยังไม่ได้ตอบ${timedOut?'เพราะหมดเวลา':''}</p>`:'');$('review-wrongs').classList.toggle('hidden',!wrong.length);icons();if(!timedOut)document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('zran-quiz-complete',{detail:{score,total:quiz.length}}));if(timedOut){$('result-detail').textContent+=' · ผลรอบนี้ไม่บันทึกลง Canva Sheet';return}if(records.length>=999){$('result-detail').textContent+=' · ไม่สามารถบันทึกคะแนน: Canva Sheet เต็ม';return}let r=await window.dataSdk.create({record_type:'score',record_key:Date.now().toString(),payload:String(quiz.length),status:timedOut?'หมดเวลา':'',score,created_at:new Date().toISOString()});if(!r.isOk)$('result-detail').textContent+=' · ไม่สามารถยืนยันการบันทึกคะแนนได้';else $('result-detail').textContent+=' · บันทึกคะแนนแล้ว'}
  $('review-wrongs').onclick=()=>{order=wrong.map(w=>String(w['序号']));$('study-day').value='';index=0;navigate('study')};
  function normalize(raw){let out={};for(let f of fields){let key=(aliases[f]||[f]).find(k=>raw[k]!==undefined);out[f]=key===undefined?'':String(raw[key]??'').trim()}if(!out.Day)out.Day='15';return out}
  function validate(incoming){let issues=[],existing=words(),seenNums=new Set(existing.map(w=>Number(w['序号']))),seenWords=new Set(existing.map(w=>`${w.Day}|${w['汉字']}`));incoming.forEach((w,i)=>{let n=Number(w['序号']);if(!Number.isInteger(n)||n<1)issues.push(`แถว ${i+2}: 序号 ต้องเป็นจำนวนเต็มบวก`);else if(seenNums.has(n))issues.push(`แถว ${i+2}: เลขลำดับ ${n} ซ้ำ`);else seenNums.add(n);if(!w['汉字']||!w['แปลไทย'])issues.push(`แถว ${i+2}: ต้องมีคำศัพท์และคำแปลไทย`);let key=`${w.Day}|${w['汉字']}`;if(w['汉字']&&seenWords.has(key))issues.push(`แถว ${i+2}: คำ ${w['汉字']} ซ้ำใน Day ${w.Day}`);seenWords.add(key)});
    let nums=[...seenNums].filter(Number.isInteger).sort((a,b)=>a-b);for(let n=nums[0];n<=nums.at(-1)&&issues.length<25;n++)if(!seenNums.has(n))issues.push(`เลขลำดับ ${n} ตกหล่น`);return issues}
  $('file-input').addEventListener('change',async e=>{let file=e.target.files[0];if(!file)return;let control=e.target;message('กำลังตรวจสอบไฟล์…');try{let rows;if(file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.json')){let parsed=JSON.parse(await file.text());rows=Array.isArray(parsed)?parsed:parsed.words}else{if(!window.XLSX)throw Error('ไม่สามารถเปิดตัวอ่านไฟล์ได้');let book=XLSX.read(await file.arrayBuffer(),{type:'array'});rows=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(book.Sheets[book.SheetNames[0]],{defval:''})}if(!Array.isArray(rows)||!rows.length)throw Error('ไฟล์ไม่มีรายการคำศัพท์');let incoming=rows.map(normalize),issues=validate(incoming);if(issues.length)throw Error('ยังไม่นำเข้าข้อมูล:\n'+issues.slice(0,20).join('\n'));if(records.length+incoming.length>999)throw Error('จำนวนรายการจะเกินขีดจำกัด 999 รายการ');control.disabled=true;let saved=0;for(let w of incoming){let r=await window.dataSdk.create({record_type:'word',record_key:String(w['序号']),payload:JSON.stringify(w),status:'',score:0,created_at:new Date().toISOString()});if(!r.isOk)throw Error(`บันทึกได้ ${saved} คำ แต่ไม่สามารถยืนยันคำถัดไปได้ โปรดรีเฟรชก่อนดำเนินการต่อ`);saved++;message(`กำลังบันทึก ${saved} / ${incoming.length} คำ…`,true);await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,550))}message(`นำเข้าสำเร็จ ${saved} คำ`,true)}catch(err){message(err.message||'ไม่สามารถอ่านไฟล์ได้')}finally{control.disabled=false;control.value=''}});
  let timer;const handler={onDataChanged(data){clearTimeout(timer);records=data;ready=true;render();document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('zran-records',{detail:data}))}};
  async function init(){timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!ready){message('ไม่สามารถโหลดข้อมูลที่บันทึกไว้ได้ กรุณากด “ลองเชื่อมต่ออีกครั้ง”');$('import-message').innerHTML+=' <button id="retry-load" class="btn small">ลองเชื่อมต่ออีกครั้ง</button>';$('retry-load').onclick=init}},8000);try{let r=await window.dataSdk.init(handler);if(!r.isOk){clearTimeout(timer);message('เชื่อมต่อข้อมูลไม่สำเร็จ');$('import-message').innerHTML+=' <button id="retry-load" class="btn small">ลองเชื่อมต่ออีกครั้ง</button>';$('retry-load').onclick=init}}catch{clearTimeout(timer);message('เชื่อมต่อข้อมูลไม่สำเร็จ')}} 
  const L={th:['智能复习','错题本','收藏夹','词语辨析','ถึงกำหนดทบทวน','เริ่มทบทวน','เริ่มฝึกใหม่','เริ่ม Quiz รายการโปรด','จำได้ง่าย','จำได้ยาก','ยังจำไม่ได้','พลิกบัตร ↻','ไม่มีข้อมูล','คำที่ 1','คำที่ 2','ความแตกต่าง','ฝึกครบเกณฑ์ · นำออกจากรายการ','ผิด','ครั้ง · ล่าสุด','บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ โปรดลองใหม่','ต้องเลือกสองคำต่างกัน','คำศัพท์ที่ถึงกำหนดและกำลังฝึก','ประวัติคำตอบผิดจะยังคงอยู่'],lo:['ທົບທວນອັດສະລິຍະ 智能复习','ສົມຸດຂໍ້ຜິດ 错题本','ຄຳສັບໂປດ 收藏夹','ປຽບທຽບຄຳ 词语辨析','ຄຳຮອດກຳນົດ','ເລີ່ມທົບທວນ','ເລີ່ມຝຶກໃໝ່','ທົດສອບຄຳໂປດ','ຈື່ໄດ້ງ່າຍ','ຈື່ໄດ້ຍາກ','ຍັງຈື່ບໍ່ໄດ້','ພິກບັດ ↻','ບໍ່ມີຂໍ້ມູນ','ຄຳທີ 1','ຄຳທີ 2','ຄວາມແຕກຕ່າງ','ຝຶກຄົບເກນ · ນຳອອກ','ຜິດ','ຄັ້ງ · ລ່າສຸດ','ບັນທຶກບໍ່ສຳເລັດ','ເລືອກສອງຄຳຕ່າງກັນ','ຄຳທີ່ຮອດກຳນົດ','ປະຫວັດຄຳຕອບຜິດຍັງຢູ່'],zh:['智能复习','错题本','收藏夹','词语辨析','到期词汇','开始复习','重新练习','收藏词测验','容易记住','记得困难','还没记住','翻转卡片 ↻','暂无数据','第一个词','第二个词','区别','达到标准 · 移出练习','答错','次 · 最近','保存失败，请重试','请选择两个不同的词','到期和正在练习的词汇','错误记录将保留']};
  const langNow=()=>document.getElementById('interface-language').value||'th', tr=i=>L[langNow()][i];
  const pendingExtra=new Map();let writeChain=Promise.resolve(),lastWrite=0;
  function extraFor(w){let id=String(w['序号']),m=markFor(w),base={};try{base=JSON.parse(m?.payload||'{}')||{}}catch{}return {...base,...(pendingExtra.get(id)||{})}}
  function updateCardExtras(){let w=current();if(!w)return;let x=extraFor(w);$('favorite-mark').textContent=x.favorite?'★ '+tr(2):'☆ '+tr(2);$('flip-button').textContent=tr(11);['again','hard','good','easy'].forEach((k,i)=>{$('rate-'+k).textContent=i===2?({th:'จำได้',lo:'ຈື່ໄດ້',zh:'记得'}[langNow()]):tr([10,9,0,8][i])});}
  function schedule(x,level){let streak=level==='easy'?(x.streak||0)+1:level==='good'?(x.streak||0)+1:level==='hard'?Math.max(0,x.streak||0):0;let accuracy=(x.correctCount||0)/Math.max(1,(x.correctCount||0)+(x.wrongCount||0));let minutes=level==='again'?10:level==='hard'?Math.min(1440*3,1440*Math.max(1,streak)):level==='good'?1440*Math.min(30,Math.max(1,streak*2)) :1440*Math.min(60,Math.pow(2,streak+1));if(level!=='again')minutes=Math.max(60,Math.round(minutes*(.7+accuracy*.6)));return {level,streak,studyCount:(x.studyCount||0)+1,correctCount:(x.correctCount||0)+(level==='again'?0:1),dueAt:new Date(Date.now()+minutes*60000).toISOString(),lastReviewed:new Date().toISOString(),memoryStatus:level==='again'?'ยังจำไม่ได้':level==='hard'?'กำลังฝึก':level==='good'?'จำได้':'แม่นยำ'}}
  function mutateExtra(w,change){let id=String(w['序号']);pendingExtra.set(id,{...extraFor(w),...change});if(page==='study')updateCardExtras();else if(['smart','wrongbook','favorites'].includes(page))renderExtra();writeChain=writeChain.catch(()=>{}).then(async()=>{let latest=pendingExtra.get(id);if(!latest)return;let wait=Math.max(0,550-(Date.now()-lastWrite));if(wait)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,wait));let existing=markFor(w);if(!existing&&records.length>=999){message('จำนวนรายการใน Canva Sheet ถึงขีดจำกัดแล้ว');pendingExtra.delete(id);render();return}let now=new Date().toISOString();let data=existing?{...existing,payload:JSON.stringify(latest)}:{record_type:'mark',record_key:id,payload:JSON.stringify(latest),status:'',score:0,created_at:now};lastWrite=Date.now();let result=await(existing?window.dataSdk.update(data):window.dataSdk.create(data));if(!result.isOk){pendingExtra.delete(id);message(tr(19));render();return}if(existing)records=records.map(r=>r.__backendId===existing.__backendId?data:r);else records.push(data);if(JSON.stringify(pendingExtra.get(id))===JSON.stringify(latest))pendingExtra.delete(id);render()});return writeChain}
  function rate(w,level){if(w){trackActivity(w,null,false);document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('zran-srs-review',{detail:String(w['序号'])}))}let x=extraFor(w),change=schedule(x,level);if(x.wrongCount){change.correctStreak=level==='again'?0:(x.correctStreak||0)+1;if(change.correctStreak>=2)change.wrongActive=false}mutateExtra(w,change)}
  function recordAnswer(w,correct){let x=extraFor(w);if(correct){let change=schedule(x,'good');if(x.wrongActive){change.correctStreak=(x.correctStreak||0)+1;if(change.correctStreak>=2)change.wrongActive=false}mutateExtra(w,change)}else mutateExtra(w,{...schedule(x,'again'),wrongCount:(x.wrongCount||0)+1,lastWrong:new Date().toISOString(),wrongActive:true,correctStreak:0})}
  function dueWords(){return words().filter(w=>{let x=extraFor(w);return x.dueAt&&new Date(x.dueAt).getTime()<=Date.now()}).sort((a,b)=>String(extraFor(a).dueAt).localeCompare(String(extraFor(b).dueAt)))}
  function rowsExtra(ws,kind){return ws.length?ws.map(w=>{let x=extraFor(w);return `<article class="card vocab-row canva-card"><div><span class="tag canva-tag">${esc(w.Day)} · #${esc(w['序号'])}</span><h3>${esc(w['汉字'])}</h3><p>${esc(w['拼音'])} · ${esc(w['แปลไทย'])}</p><p>${kind==='wrong'?`${tr(17)} ${x.wrongCount||0} ${tr(18)} ${esc(x.lastWrong?new Date(x.lastWrong).toLocaleDateString():'—')}`:x.dueAt?new Date(x.dueAt).toLocaleString():'—'}</p>${kind==='wrong'?`<p>✓ ${esc(w['แปลไทย']||'—')} · 中文解释: ${esc(w['中文解释']||'—')}</p><p>${esc(w['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน']||'—')} · ${esc(w['แปลประโยคไทย']||'—')}</p>`:`<p>เรียน ${x.studyCount||0} · ถูก ${x.correctCount||0} · ผิด ${x.wrongCount||0} · ${esc(x.memoryStatus||'ใหม่')}</p><p>ล่าสุด ${esc(x.lastReviewed?new Date(x.lastReviewed).toLocaleString():'—')}</p>`}</div><div class="actions"><button class="btn small canva-button" data-open="${esc(w['序号'])}">Flashcard</button><button class="btn secondary small canva-button" data-favorite="${esc(w['序号'])}">${x.favorite?'★':'☆'}</button>${kind==='wrong'&&x.correctStreak>=2&&x.wrongActive?`<button class="btn secondary small canva-button" data-clearwrong="${esc(w['序号'])}">${tr(16)}</button>`:''}${kind==='favorite'?`<button class="btn secondary small canva-button" data-rowrate="${esc(w['序号'])}">${tr(8)}</button>`:''}</div></article>`}).join(''):`<div class="empty canva-panel"><h3>${tr(12)}</h3></div>`}
  function startSubset(ws,quizMode=false){if(!ws.length)return;if(quizMode){quizSource=ws;navigate('quiz');beginQuiz()}else{order=ws.map(w=>String(w['序号']));index=0;$('study-day').value='';navigate('study')}}
  function renderExtra(){let due=dueWords(),wrongs=words().filter(w=>extraFor(w).wrongCount),fav=words().filter(w=>extraFor(w).favorite),q=$('favorite-search').value.toLowerCase(),day=$('favorite-day').value;
    [['smart-title',0],['wrong-title',1],['favorites-title',2],['compare-title',3],['start-smart',5],['start-wrong',6],['favorite-quiz',7],['compare-first-label',13],['compare-second-label',14],['favorite-search-label',langNow()==='zh'?'搜索':langNow()==='lo'?'ຄົ້ນຫາ':'ค้นหา'],['compare-search-label',langNow()==='zh'?'搜索':langNow()==='lo'?'ຄົ້ນຫາ':'ค้นหา']].forEach(([id,i])=>$(id).textContent=typeof i==='number'?tr(i):i);
    $('smart-description').textContent=tr(21);$('wrong-description').textContent=tr(22);$('due-count').textContent=tr(4)+' '+due.length;$('start-smart').disabled=!due.length;$('start-wrong').disabled=!wrongs.some(w=>extraFor(w).wrongActive);$('favorite-quiz').disabled=!fav.length;$('smart-list').innerHTML=rowsExtra(due,'smart');$('wrongbook-list').innerHTML=rowsExtra(wrongs,'wrong');dayOptions($('favorite-day'));$('favorite-day').value=day;$('favorites-list').innerHTML=rowsExtra(fav.filter(w=>(!day||w.Day===day)&&(!$('favorite-hsk').value||String(w.HSK||w['ระดับ HSK']||'')===$('favorite-hsk').value)&&(!q||[w['汉字'],w['拼音'],w['แปลไทย']].some(v=>String(v).toLowerCase().includes(q)))),'favorite');
    let search=$('compare-search').value.toLowerCase(),found=words().filter(w=>[w['汉字'],w['拼音'],w['แปลไทย']].some(v=>String(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(search))).slice(0,120),a=$('compare-first').value,b=$('compare-second').value;for(let id of ['compare-first','compare-second']){$(id).innerHTML='<option value="">—</option>'+found.map(w=>`<option value="${esc(w['序号'])}">${esc(w['汉字'])} · ${esc(w['拼音'])}</option>`).join('')} $('compare-first').value=a;$('compare-second').value=b;let selected=[a,b].map(id=>words().find(w=>String(w['序号'])===id));$('compare-results').innerHTML=a&&b&&a!==b?selected.map(w=>`<article class="panel canva-panel"><h2>${esc(w['汉字'])} · ${esc(w['拼音'])}</h2><p>${esc(w['แปลไทย'])}</p><p>${esc(w['แปลลาว']||'—')}</p><p>${esc(w['词性']||'—')}</p><p>中文解释: ${esc(w['中文解释']||'—')}</p><p>${esc(w['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน']||'—')}<br>${esc(w['แปลประโยคไทย']||'—')}</p><p>${esc(w['ตัวอย่างประโยค 2']||'—')}<br>${esc(w['แปลไทย 2']||'—')}</p><p>${tr(15)}: ${esc(w['词语辨析']||'—')}</p></article>`).join(''):'';icons()}
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{let b=e.target.closest('[data-favorite],[data-clearwrong],[data-rowrate],[data-rate]');if(!b)return;e.stopPropagation();let w=b.dataset.rate?current():words().find(x=>String(x['序号'])===(b.dataset.favorite||b.dataset.clearwrong||b.dataset.rowrate));if(!w)return;if(b.dataset.favorite!==undefined)mutateExtra(w,{favorite:!extraFor(w).favorite});else if(b.dataset.clearwrong)mutateExtra(w,{wrongActive:false});else rate(w,b.dataset.rate||'easy')});
  $('favorite-mark').onclick=()=>{let w=current();if(w)mutateExtra(w,{favorite:!extraFor(w).favorite})};$('flip-button').onclick=()=>$('flip-scene').click();$('start-smart').onclick=()=>startSubset(dueWords());$('start-wrong').onclick=()=>startSubset(words().filter(w=>extraFor(w).wrongActive));$('favorite-quiz').onclick=()=>startSubset(words().filter(w=>extraFor(w).favorite),true);$('favorite-search').oninput=renderExtra;$('favorite-day').onchange=renderExtra;$('favorite-hsk').onchange=renderExtra;$('compare-search').oninput=renderExtra;$('compare-first').onchange=renderExtra;$('compare-second').onchange=renderExtra;$('interface-language').addEventListener('change',()=>{renderExtra();updateCardExtras()});
  const labels={th:{statistics:'สถิติการเรียน 学习统计',goal:'เป้าหมายรายวัน 每日学习目标',chooseGoal:'จำนวนคำต่อวัน',dailyProgress:'ความคืบหน้ารายวัน',advanced:'ค้นหาขั้นสูง 高级搜索',search:'ค้นหา',field:'ค้นหาใน',type:'ชนิดคำ',status:'สถานะ',management:'จัดการข้อมูล 数据管理',import:'นำเข้าคำศัพท์',selectFile:'เลือก Excel หรือ CSV',duplicates:'เลขซ้ำ',confirmImport:'ยืนยันนำเข้า',export:'ส่งออกข้อมูล',quizMode:'รูปแบบ Quiz 多种测验模式',replay:'ฟังอีกครั้ง',answer:'พิมพ์คำจีน',check:'ตรวจคำตอบ',total:'คำที่เรียน',known:'จำได้แล้ว',review:'ยังจำไม่ได้',accuracy:'ตอบถูก',streak:'วันต่อเนื่อง',done:'สำเร็จ',results:'ผลลัพธ์'},lo:{statistics:'ສະຖິຕິການຮຽນ 学习统计',goal:'ເປົ້າໝາຍປະຈຳວັນ 每日学习目标',chooseGoal:'ຄຳຕໍ່ມື້',dailyProgress:'ຄວາມຄືບໜ້າລາຍວັນ',advanced:'ຄົ້ນຫາຂັ້ນສູງ 高级搜索',search:'ຄົ້ນຫາ',field:'ຊ່ອງຄົ້ນຫາ',type:'ຊະນິດຄຳ',status:'ສະຖານະ',management:'ຈັດການຂໍ້ມູນ 数据管理',import:'ນຳເຂົ້າຄຳສັບ',selectFile:'ເລືອກ Excel ຫຼື CSV',duplicates:'ເລກຊ້ຳ',confirmImport:'ຢືນຢັນນຳເຂົ້າ',export:'ສົ່ງອອກຂໍ້ມູນ',quizMode:'ໂໝດທົດສອບ 多种测验模式',replay:'ຟັງອີກ',answer:'ພິມຄຳຈີນ',check:'ກວດຄຳຕອບ',total:'ຄຳທີ່ຮຽນ',known:'ຈື່ໄດ້',review:'ຍັງບໍ່ຈື່',accuracy:'ຕອບຖືກ',streak:'ມື້ຕໍ່ເນື່ອງ',done:'ສຳເລັດ',results:'ຜົນການຄົ້ນຫາ'},zh:{statistics:'学习统计',goal:'每日学习目标',chooseGoal:'每日词汇目标',dailyProgress:'每日学习进度',advanced:'高级搜索',search:'搜索',field:'搜索字段',type:'词性',status:'状态',management:'数据管理',import:'导入词汇',selectFile:'选择 Excel 或 CSV',duplicates:'重复序号',confirmImport:'确认导入',export:'导出数据',quizMode:'多种测验模式',replay:'重听',answer:'输入汉字',check:'检查答案',total:'已学习词汇',known:'已掌握',review:'待复习',accuracy:'正确率',streak:'连续学习天数',done:'已达成',results:'搜索结果'}};
  const T=k=>labels[langNow()][k]||k, localDay=()=>{let d=new Date();return [d.getFullYear(),String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')].join('-')};
  function trackActivity(w,correct,isQuiz){let x=extraFor(w),d=localDay(),daily={...(x.daily||{})},entry={...(daily[d]||{reviews:0,quiz:0,correct:0})};entry.reviews++;if(isQuiz){entry.quiz++;if(correct)entry.correct++}daily[d]=entry;mutateExtra(w,{daily})}
  function renderStatistics(){let ws=words(),active=ws.filter(w=>Object.keys(extraFor(w).daily||{}).length||markFor(w)?.status),known=ws.filter(w=>markFor(w)?.status==='known'),review=ws.filter(w=>markFor(w)?.status==='review'),days={};ws.forEach(w=>Object.entries(extraFor(w).daily||{}).forEach(([day,v])=>{let d=days[day]||(days[day]={ids:new Set(),quiz:0,correct:0});d.ids.add(String(w['序号']));d.quiz+=v.quiz||0;d.correct+=v.correct||0}));let all=Object.values(days),attempts=all.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.quiz,0),correct=all.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.correct,0),streak=0,dt=new Date();if(!days[localDay()])dt.setDate(dt.getDate()-1);while(days[[dt.getFullYear(),String(dt.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),String(dt.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')].join('-')]){streak++;dt.setDate(dt.getDate()-1)}let stats=[[T('total'),active.length],[T('known'),known.length],[T('review'),review.length],[T('accuracy'),attempts?Math.round(correct/attempts*100)+'%':'—'],[T('streak'),streak]];$('stats-grid').innerHTML=stats.map(([name,num])=>`<div class="card stat canva-card"><strong>${num}</strong><span>${esc(name)}</span></div>`).join('');let goal=Number((records.find(r=>r.record_type==='setting'&&r.record_key==='goal')||{}).payload)||20;$('daily-goal').value=String(goal);let n=days[localDay()]?.ids.size||0;$('goal-summary').textContent=`${n} / ${goal} · ${n>=goal?T('done'):Math.round(n/goal*100)+'%'}`;$('goal-fill').style.width=Math.min(100,n/goal*100)+'%';$('daily-chart').innerHTML=Object.entries(days).sort((a,b)=>b[0].localeCompare(a[0])).slice(0,30).map(([day,v])=>`<div style="margin:12px 0"><span>${esc(day)} · ${v.ids.size}</span><div class="track"><div class="fill" style="width:${Math.min(100,v.ids.size/goal*100)}%"></div></div></div>`).join('')||'<p>—</p>'}
  function renderAdvanced(){dayOptions($('advanced-day'));let type=$('advanced-type').value,types=[...new Set(words().map(w=>w['词性']).filter(Boolean))].sort();$('advanced-type').innerHTML='<option value="">'+T('field')+': '+T('results')+'</option>'+types.map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x)}">${esc(x)}</option>`).join('');$('advanced-type').value=type;let q=$('advanced-query').value.trim().toLowerCase(),field=$('advanced-field').value,day=$('advanced-day').value,status=$('advanced-status').value;let result=words().filter(w=>(!day||w.Day===day)&&(!type||w['词性']===type)&&(!q||(field==='all'?['汉字','拼音','แปลไทย','แปลลาว','ຄຳແປລາວ']: [field]).some(k=>String(w[k]||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)))&&(status==='all'||status==='known'&&markFor(w)?.status==='known'||status==='review'&&markFor(w)?.status==='review'||status==='favorite'&&extraFor(w).favorite||status==='smart'&&extraFor(w).dueAt));$('advanced-count').textContent=`${T('results')}: ${result.length}`;$('advanced-results').innerHTML=result.slice(0,150).map(w=>row(w)).join('')||empty('—');icons()}
  document.querySelectorAll('#advanced input,#advanced select').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener(el.tagName==='INPUT'?'input':'change',renderAdvanced));$('daily-goal').onchange=async e=>{let v=e.target.value,old=records.find(r=>r.record_type==='setting'&&r.record_key==='goal');if(!old&&records.length>=999){$('manage-message').textContent='Canva Sheet limit';return}let data=old?{...old,payload:v}:{record_type:'setting',record_key:'goal',payload:v,status:'',score:0,created_at:new Date().toISOString()};let r=await(old?window.dataSdk.update(data):window.dataSdk.create(data));if(!r.isOk)$('goal-summary').textContent='Unable to confirm save';else{if(old)records=records.map(x=>x.__backendId===old.__backendId?data:x);else records.push(data);renderStatistics()}};
  let pendingImport=null;function manageMessage(text,ok=false){$('manage-message').textContent=text;$('manage-message').className='message'+(ok?' ok':'')}
  $('manage-file').onchange=async e=>{pendingImport=null;$('manage-commit').classList.add('hidden');try{let file=e.target.files[0];if(!file)return;if(!window.XLSX)throw Error('Excel reader unavailable');let book=XLSX.read(await file.arrayBuffer(),{type:'array'}),sheet=book.Sheets[book.SheetNames[0]],header=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet,{header:1})[0]||[];if(!['序号','拼音','แปลไทย'].every(k=>header.includes(k))||!header.some(k=>['汉字','词语'].includes(k)))throw Error('Missing columns: 序号, 汉字/词语, 拼音, แปลไทย');let raw=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet,{defval:''}),rows=raw.map(normalize),seen=new Set(),issues=[];rows.forEach((w,i)=>{let id=String(w['序号']);if(!/^\d+$/.test(id)||Number(id)<1||seen.has(id)||!w['汉字']||!w['拼音']||!w['แปลไทย'])issues.push('Row '+(i+2)+': invalid or duplicate 序号 / required fields');seen.add(id)});if(issues.length)throw Error(issues.slice(0,10).join('\n'));let existing=new Map(words().map(w=>[String(w['序号']),w]));pendingImport=rows;$('manage-preview').textContent=`${rows.length} rows · ${rows.filter(w=>!existing.has(String(w['序号']))).length} new · ${rows.filter(w=>existing.has(String(w['序号']))).length} existing`;$('manage-samples').innerHTML=rows.slice(0,5).map(w=>`<p>${esc(w['序号'])} · ${esc(w['汉字'])} · ${esc(w['拼音'])} · ${esc(w['แปลไทย'])}</p>`).join('');$('manage-commit').classList.remove('hidden');manageMessage('')}catch(err){manageMessage(err.message)}};
  $('manage-commit').onclick=async e=>{if(!pendingImport)return;let b=e.currentTarget;b.disabled=true;let count=0;try{for(let w of pendingImport){let id=String(w['序号']),saved=records.find(r=>r.record_type==='word'&&r.record_key===id),base=bundled.find(x=>String(x['序号'])===id);if((saved||base)&&$('duplicate-action').value==='skip')continue;if(!saved&&records.length>=999)throw Error('Canva Sheet limit (999)');let payload=JSON.stringify(base?{...base,...w}:w),data=saved?{...saved,payload}:{record_type:'word',record_key:id,payload,status:'',score:0,created_at:new Date().toISOString()};let r=await(saved?window.dataSdk.update(data):window.dataSdk.create(data));if(!r.isOk)throw Error('Could not confirm row '+id+'; refresh before retry');if(saved)records=records.map(x=>x.__backendId===saved.__backendId?data:x);else records.push(data);count++;await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,550))}manageMessage(`✓ ${count} rows saved`,true);pendingImport=null;$('manage-commit').classList.add('hidden');render()}catch(err){manageMessage(err.message)}finally{b.disabled=false}};
  function exportData(format){if(!window.XLSX){manageMessage('Excel reader unavailable');return}let rows=words().map(w=>({...w,_record:undefined,status:markFor(w)?.status||'',favorite:!!extraFor(w).favorite,wrongCount:extraFor(w).wrongCount||0,dueAt:extraFor(w).dueAt||'',daily:JSON.stringify(extraFor(w).daily||{})}));let sheet=XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows),book=XLSX.utils.book_new();XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(book,sheet,'Vocabulary');try{let text=format==='csv'?XLSX.utils.sheet_to_csv(sheet):XLSX.utils.sheet_to_csv(sheet,{FS:'\t'});let area=document.createElement('textarea');area.value=text;document.body.appendChild(area);area.select();let copied=document.execCommand('copy');area.remove();manageMessage(copied?(format==='csv'?'คัดลอก CSV แล้ว':'คัดลอกตารางแล้ว วางใน Excel เพื่อบันทึกเป็น .xlsx'):'คัดลอกไม่สำเร็จ',copied)}catch(err){manageMessage('ไม่สามารถส่งออกได้: '+err.message)}}$('export-csv').onclick=()=>exportData('csv');$('export-xlsx').onclick=()=>exportData('xlsx');
  const comparisonGuides=[
[['仍然','réngrán','副','ยังคง','ຍັງຄົງ','表示情况持续不变','เน้นความต่อเนื่องแม้มีอุปสรรค','ใช้เมื่อสภาพยังคงเดิม','下雨了，他仍然去跑步。','ฝนตกแล้ว เขาก็ยังไปวิ่ง'],['依然','yīrán','副','ยังคงเหมือนเดิม','ຍັງຄົງຄືເກົ່າ','表示保持原来的样子','เน้นสภาพเหมือนเดิม มักเป็นภาษาเขียน','ใช้บรรยายภาพหรือสภาพที่ไม่เปลี่ยน','多年后，这里依然很美。','หลายปีผ่านไป ที่นี่ยังคงสวยงาม']],
[['居然','jūrán','副','กลับกลายเป็นว่า','ກັບກາຍເປັນວ່າ','表示出乎意料，语气较强','ผู้พูดตกใจหรือไม่คาดคิด','ใช้เน้นความประหลาดใจ','他居然忘了我的名字。','เขากลับลืมชื่อฉันเสียได้'],['竟然','jìngrán','副','อย่างไม่น่าเชื่อ','ຢ່າງບໍ່ໜ້າເຊື່ອ','表示结果出乎意料','เน้นผลลัพธ์เกินคาด ใช้ได้ทั้งภาษาพูดและเขียน','ใช้กับเหตุการณ์ที่ผลไม่คาดหมาย','他竟然通过了考试。','เขาสอบผ่านอย่างไม่น่าเชื่อ']],
[['突然','tūrán','形／副','กะทันหัน','ກະທັນຫັນ','表示事情发生得急促','ใช้เป็นคำคุณศัพท์หรือคำวิเศษณ์ได้','ใช้กับเหตุการณ์ฉับพลัน','突然下起雨来。','จู่ ๆ ฝนก็ตก'],['忽然','hūrán','副','จู่ ๆ','ທັນໃດນັ້ນ','表示短时间内发生变化','เป็นคำวิเศษณ์เท่านั้น','ใช้ก่อนกริยาในเรื่องเล่า','他忽然笑了。','จู่ ๆ เขาก็หัวเราะ']],
[['适合','shìhé','动','เหมาะกับ','ເໝາະກັບ','符合某人或某种条件','กริยา: สิ่งหนึ่งเหมาะกับอีกสิ่ง','ใช้ว่า A 适合 B','这件衣服适合你。','เสื้อผ้าชุดนี้เหมาะกับคุณ'],['合适','héshì','形','เหมาะสม','ເໝາະສົມ','符合实际需要或情况','คำคุณศัพท์: สิ่งนั้นมีความเหมาะสม','ใช้ว่า 很合适','这个时间很合适。','เวลานี้เหมาะสมมาก']],
[['必须','bìxū','副','จำเป็นต้อง','ຈຳເປັນຕ້ອງ','表示一定要做','วางหน้ากริยาเพื่อบอกหน้าที่','ใช้กับการกระทำที่ต้องทำ','你必须按时到。','คุณต้องมาตรงเวลา'],['必需','bìxū','动／形','จำเป็นต่อการใช้','ສິ່ງຈຳເປັນ','表示不可缺少','ขยายคำนามว่าสิ่งนั้นขาดไม่ได้','ใช้กับสิ่งของหรือปัจจัยจำเป็น','水是生活必需品。','น้ำเป็นสิ่งจำเป็นต่อชีวิต']]];
$('compare-guides').innerHTML=comparisonGuides.map(pair=>`<div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(240px,1fr));gap:16px;margin-bottom:18px">${pair.map(w=>`<article class="panel canva-card"><h3>${w[0]} <small>${w[1]}</small></h3><p><b>词性</b> ${w[2]} · <b>ไทย</b> ${w[3]} · <b>ລາວ</b> ${w[4]}</p><p><b>中文解释</b> ${w[5]}</p><p><b>ความแตกต่าง</b> ${w[6]}</p><p><b>การใช้งาน</b> ${w[7]}</p><p>${w[8]}<br>${w[9]}</p></article>`).join('')}</div>`).join('');
const settingsDefaults={theme:'light',mode:'sequential',voice:'0.85',notify:'off'};let pendingSettings=null,settingsQueue=Promise.resolve();
function savedSettings(){let found=records.find(r=>r.record_type==='setting'&&r.record_key==='preferences');try{return {...settingsDefaults,...JSON.parse(found?.payload||'{}')}}catch{return {...settingsDefaults}}}
function showSettings(){let opts=pendingSettings||savedSettings();for(let key of Object.keys(settingsDefaults)){let el=$('settings-'+key);if(document.activeElement!==el)el.value=opts[key]||settingsDefaults[key]}document.body.classList.toggle('night-mode',opts.theme==='dark');$('settings-language').value=$('interface-language').value;$('settings-goal').value=String(Number(records.find(r=>r.record_type==='setting'&&r.record_key==='goal')?.payload)||20);let today=localDay(),done=words().filter(w=>extraFor(w).daily?.[today]).length,goal=Number($('settings-goal').value)||20;$('settings-reminder').textContent=opts.notify==='on'&&done<goal?({th:`วันนี้เรียนแล้ว ${done} / ${goal} คำ · มาเรียนต่อกันเถอะ`,lo:`ມື້ນີ້ຮຽນແລ້ວ ${done} / ${goal} ຄຳ · ມາຮຽນຕໍ່`,zh:`今天已学习 ${done} / ${goal} 词 · 继续加油`,en:`Today: ${done} / ${goal} words · Keep learning!`}[langNow()]):'';let reminder=$('dashboard-reminder');reminder.textContent=$('settings-reminder').textContent;reminder.classList.toggle('hidden',!reminder.textContent)}
$('settings-language').onchange=e=>{$('interface-language').value=e.target.value;$('interface-language').dispatchEvent(new Event('change'))};$('interface-language').addEventListener('change',showSettings);
$('settings-goal').onchange=e=>{$('daily-goal').value=e.target.value;$('daily-goal').dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));$('settings-message').textContent='กำลังบันทึกเป้าหมาย…'};
for(let key of Object.keys(settingsDefaults))$('settings-'+key).addEventListener('change',e=>{pendingSettings={...(pendingSettings||savedSettings()),[key]:e.target.value};showSettings();$('settings-message').textContent='กำลังบันทึก…';settingsQueue=settingsQueue.catch(()=>{}).then(async()=>{let snapshot={...pendingSettings};await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,550));let existing=records.find(r=>r.record_type==='setting'&&r.record_key==='preferences');if(!existing&&records.length>=999){pendingSettings=null;showSettings();$('settings-message').textContent='Canva Sheet เต็ม ไม่สามารถบันทึกได้';return}let data=existing?{...existing,payload:JSON.stringify(snapshot)}:{record_type:'setting',record_key:'preferences',payload:JSON.stringify(snapshot),status:'',score:0,created_at:new Date().toISOString()};try{let result=await(existing?window.dataSdk.update(data):window.dataSdk.create(data));if(!result.isOk)throw Error('ไม่สามารถยืนยันการบันทึกได้ โปรดรีเฟรชก่อนลองใหม่');if(existing)records=records.map(r=>r.__backendId===existing.__backendId?data:r);else records.push(data);if(JSON.stringify(pendingSettings)===JSON.stringify(snapshot)){pendingSettings=null;$('settings-message').textContent='บันทึกการตั้งค่าแล้ว'}showSettings()}catch(err){pendingSettings=null;showSettings();$('settings-message').textContent=err.message}})});
$('settings-import').onclick=()=>{navigate('management');$('manage-file').focus()};$('settings-export').onclick=()=>{navigate('management');$('export-csv').focus()};
const oldRender=render;render=function(){oldRender();showSettings();if(page==='statistics')renderStatistics();if(page==='advanced')renderAdvanced();document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el=>el.textContent=T(el.dataset.i18n));document.querySelectorAll('[data-newlabel]').forEach(el=>el.textContent=T(el.dataset.newlabel))};$('interface-language').addEventListener('change',render);render();init();
})();
</script>
  <style>
  .settings-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));gap:16px}.settings-grid .panel{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px}.settings-grid h2{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin:0 0 8px}.settings-grid label{font-weight:700;margin-top:8px}.settings-grid select{width:100%;padding:12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:11px;background:var(--paper);color:var(--ink)}.settings-grid .actions{margin-top:8px}.settings-grid p{margin-bottom:0}.nav button svg{vertical-align:middle;width:16px;height:16px;margin-right:7px}.card,.panel{transition:transform .22s ease,box-shadow .22s ease}.card:hover,.panel:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 12px 32px rgba(23,63,53,.09)}button:not(:disabled):active{transform:scale(.97)}.week-bar{animation:barRise .55s ease-out both;transform-origin:bottom}@keyframes barRise{from{transform:scaleY(.08)}to{transform:scaleY(1)}}#quiz-results:not(.hidden){animation:finishRise .45s ease both}@keyframes finishRise{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(18px) scale(.98)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}.fill{transition:width .55s ease}
  body.night-mode{--bg:#14241f;--paper:#20362e;--ink:#eef4e8;--forest:#406d57;--muted:#b9cbbd;--line:#456052;--soft:#304b3d;--gold:#d7bd82;color:var(--ink)}body.night-mode .nav{background:#10251d}body.night-mode .nav button.active{background:#365e4a}body.night-mode .face.back,body.night-mode .empty,body.night-mode .upload{background:#294135}body.night-mode .option,body.night-mode .toolbar input,body.night-mode .toolbar select,body.night-mode .editor-grid input,body.night-mode .editor-grid textarea,body.night-mode .upload input,body.night-mode .language-control select{background:#294135;color:var(--ink);border-color:var(--line)}body.night-mode .btn.secondary,body.night-mode .tag{background:#375644;color:var(--ink)}body.night-mode .track{background:#365544}body.night-mode .vocab-row p,body.night-mode .panel p,body.night-mode .detail{color:var(--muted)}body.night-mode .seal{color:#fff}body.night-mode .owner-label{color:var(--ink)}
  .daily-reminder{padding:13px 17px;margin:0 0 18px;border:1px solid var(--gold);border-left:4px solid var(--gold);border-radius:14px;background:var(--paper);color:var(--ink);box-shadow:0 8px 25px rgba(23,63,53,.06);font-weight:600}.settings-grid .panel{border-radius:22px}.settings-grid select:focus-visible,.flip-scene:focus-visible{outline:3px solid var(--gold);outline-offset:3px}.flip-scene:hover .flashcard:not(.flipped){transform:translateY(-3px)}.flip-scene:hover .flashcard.flipped{transform:rotateY(180deg) translateY(-3px)}@media(max-width:760px){.settings-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.settings-grid .panel{padding:20px}.status-actions .btn{flex:1 1 125px}.study-controls .btn{min-height:44px}}@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.page,.flashcard,.card,.panel,.fill,.week-bar,#quiz-results:not(.hidden){animation:none!important;transition:none!important}html{scroll-behavior:auto}}
  .dashboard-insights{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.3fr .7fr;gap:16px;margin-top:16px}.dash-week{margin-top:16px}.dash-week h2{margin-top:8px}.week-bars{display:flex;align-items:end;gap:14px;height:130px;padding-top:10px}.week-item{flex:1;min-width:0;text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:11px}.week-item strong{display:block;color:var(--ink)}.week-bar{background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--forest),#88aa91);border-radius:6px 6px 2px 2px;min-height:5px;margin:5px auto;width:min(100%,48px)}.library-actions{margin-bottom:14px}.library-pagination{display:flex;justify-content:center;align-items:center;gap:18px;margin:20px 0}.library-tools label{font-size:12px;font-weight:700}.vocab-row .row-main{flex:1}.row-select{width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:var(--forest)}.editor-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));gap:14px;margin:18px 0}.editor-grid label{display:block;font-weight:600}.editor-grid input,.editor-grid textarea{display:block;width:100%;padding:10px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:9px;background:white;color:var(--ink);margin-top:5px;font:inherit}.editor-grid textarea{min-height:75px}.flashcard{height:440px}.face.back{padding:35px}.face.back .detail{max-height:265px;text-align:left;width:100%;line-height:1.9}.face.back h2{margin:0 0 10px}.detail div{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding:3px 0}@media(max-width:760px){.dashboard-insights,.editor-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.flashcard{height:460px}.face.back .detail{max-height:310px}.week-bars{gap:5px}.library-tools select{flex:1;min-width:120px}}.owner-label{color:var(--ink);font-size:12px;font-weight:700;background:var(--soft);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:9px 12px;white-space:nowrap}.language-control{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;margin-left:auto;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;font-weight:700}.language-control select{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:9px 12px;background:var(--paper);color:var(--ink);font-weight:700}@media(max-width:760px){.top{flex-wrap:wrap}.owner-label{white-space:normal}.language-control{margin-left:0;width:100%;justify-content:flex-end}}
  /* Premium learning finish — retains the original forest, cream and gold palette. */

  .top{border-bottom-color:rgba(198,163,107,.38)}.nav button{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:11px;min-height:43px}.nav button svg{margin-right:0;opacity:.78}.nav button.active .icon{border:1px solid rgba(198,163,107,.35)}.stat strong{font-family:"Inter","Noto Sans Thai",sans-serif;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-1.5px}.btn,.option,.nav button{transition:transform .22s premiumEnter{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(11px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}.goal-layout{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:24px;margin-top:14px}.goal-copy{flex:1;min-width:0}.goal-copy h2 span{font-size:14px;color:var(--forest);white-space:nowrap}.goal-ring{--progress:0%;width:116px;height:116px;flex:none;border-radius:50%;padding:9px;background:conic-gradient(var(--forest) strong{font:700 25px "Inter",sans-serif;color:var(--ink)}.goal-ring small{font:700 8px "Inter",sans-serif;letter-spacing:1px;color:var(--muted)}.week-bars{height:150px}.week-bar{box-shadow:0 3px 8px rgba(36,89,74,.14)}.empty{border-color:rgba(198,163,107,.6);background:linear-gradient(145deg,var(--paper),var(--soft));padding:48px 22px}.empty .empty-icon{border:1px solid textarea:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--gold);outline-offset:2px}
  @media(max-width:1000px){.dashboard-insights{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.goal-ring{width:95px;height:95px}.goal-ring strong{font-size:21px}}@media(max-width:760px){.nav button{flex:none;min-height:44px;gap:6px}.nav button:hover{transform:none}.dashboard-insights{grid-template-columns:1fr}.goal-ring{width:100px;height:100px}.heading{padding-bottom:12px}.hanzi{font-size:clamp(68px,17vw,108px)}}@media(max-width:420px){.goal-layout{gap:12px}.goal-ring{width:85px;height:85px;padding:7px}.goal-ring strong{font-size:18px}.goal-ring small{font-size:7px}.panel{padding:19px}.week-item{font-size:9px}}@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.page,.nav button,.btn,.option{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}}

  /* Midnight Indigo glass theme */
  :root{--bg:#080e25;--paper:rgba(25,36,72,.78);--ink:#f2f7ff;--forest:#35c9df;--muted:#b5c5e0;--line:rgba(132,188,238,.25);--red:#e5a0c9;--soft:rgba(79,115,180,.25);--gold:#bba8ff}
  body,body.night-mode{background:radial-gradient(ellipse 48% 40% at 88% 12%,rgba(90,71,180,.31),transparent 75%),radial-gradient(ellipse 46% 45% at 19% 84%,rgba(30,184,207,.17),transparent 75%),#080e25;color:var(--ink);background-attachment:fixed}
  body.night-mode{--bg:#080e25;--paper:rgba(25,36,72,.78);--ink:#f2f7ff;--forest:#35c9df;--muted:#b5c5e0;--line:rgba(132,188,238,.25);--soft:rgba(79,115,180,.25);--gold:#bba8ff}

  .top,.heading{border-bottom-color:var(--line)}.eyebrow,.nav:before,.nav button.active svg{color:#aab8ff}.seal{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#285f9c,#7355b3);border-color:rgba(160,216,255,.5);color:#fff}

  .btn{background:linear-gradient(115deg,#177baf,#6754b7);color:#fff;box-shadow:0 5px 18px rgba(44,161,207,.2)}.btn:not(:disabled):hover{background:linear-gradient(115deg,#209ac5,#8068d1);box-shadow:0 9px 24px rgba(54,202,232,.25)}.btn.secondary,body.night-mode .btn.secondary{background:rgba(81,112,181,.28);color:#eef7ff}.btn.secondary:hover{background:rgba(100,144,204,.4)}.btn.red{background:#945e9f}.tag,body.night-mode .tag,.owner-label{background:var(--soft);color:var(--ink);border-color:var(--line)}

  /* The light choice must override the final indigo theme, not just the earlier palette. */
  body:not(.night-mode){--bg:#f6f5fc;--paper:#ffffff;--ink:#20304b;--forest:#246d91;--muted:#596a83;--line:#d7dfed;--soft:#eaf1fa;--gold:#7355bc;--red:#a75070;background:radial-gradient(ellipse 48% 40% at 88% 12%,rgba(197,204,250,.34),transparent 75%),radial-gradient(ellipse 46% 45% at 19% 84%,rgba(159,224,235,.25),transparent 75%),#f6f5fc;color:var(--ink)}

  body:not(.night-mode) .heading h1,body:not(.night-mode) .stat strong{color:var(--ink)}body:not(.night-mode) .eyebrow,body:not(.night-mode) .nav:before{color:#6551a3}

  body:not(.night-mode) .option,body:not(.night-mode) .toolbar input,body:not(.night-mode) .toolbar select,body:not(.night-mode) .editor-grid input,body:not(.night-mode) .editor-grid textarea,body:not(.night-mode) .upload input,body:not(.night-mode) .language-control select,body:not(.night-mode) .settings-grid select,body:not(.night-mode) #quiz-home .quiz-mode-field select{background:#fff;color:var(--ink);border-color:var(--line)}
  body:not(.night-mode) .face.back,body:not(.night-mode) .empty,body:not(.night-mode) .upload{background:#f1f5fc;border-color:var(--line)}body:not(.night-mode) .face.front{background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 45%,#e8f5fc,transparent 60%),#fff}body:not(.night-mode) .goal-ring-inner{background:#fff}body:not(.night-mode) .track{background:#dce7f4}body:not(.night-mode) .btn.secondary,body:not(.night-mode) .tag{background:#e6eefb;color:var(--ink)}body:not(.night-mode) .btn{background:linear-gradient(115deg,#237fa9,#6955af);color:#fff}body:not(.night-mode) .btn.secondary{background:#e6eefb;color:var(--ink)}body:not(.night-mode) .owner-label{background:#e6eefb;color:var(--ink)}body:not(.night-mode) .pinyin{color:#236f91}body:not(.night-mode) input::placeholder,body:not(.night-mode) textarea::placeholder{color:#667891}

 const english=new Map(entries.filter(e=>e[3]).map(e=>[e[0],e[3]]));const map=new Map(entries.map(([th,lo,zh])=>[th,{lo,zh}]));let lang='th';try{lang=localStorage.getItem('vocab-interface-language')||'th'}catch{}if(!['th','lo','zh','en'].includes(lang))lang='th';
 const patterns=[[/^มีคำศัพท์ (\d+) คำ พร้อมเริ่มเรียนและทบทวน$/,(n,l)=>l==='lo'?`ມີຄຳສັບ ${n} ຄຳ ພ້ອມຮຽນແລະທົບທວນ`:`共有 ${n} 个词汇，可以开始学习和复习`],[/^มีคำศัพท์ (\d+) คำ พร้อมทดสอบความจำ$/,(n,l)=>l==='lo'?`ມີຄຳສັບ ${n} ຄຳ ພ້ອມທົດສອບ`:`共有 ${n} 个词汇，可以开始测验`],[/^คำที่ (\d+) \/ (\d+)$/,(a,b,l)=>l==='lo'?`ຄຳທີ ${a} / ${b}`:`第 ${a} / ${b} 词`],[/^(\d+)% จำได้แล้ว$/,(n,l)=>l==='lo'?`ຈື່ໄດ້ ${n}%`:`已掌握 ${n}%`],[/^ข้อ (\d+) \/ (\d+)$/,(a,b,l)=>l==='lo'?`ຂໍ້ ${a} / ${b}`:`第 ${a} / ${b} 题`],[/^คะแนน (\d+)$/,(n,l)=>l==='lo'?`ຄະແນນ ${n}`:`得分 ${n}`],[/^(\d+) \/ (\d+) คะแนน$/,(a,b,l)=>l==='lo'?`${a} / ${b} ຄະແນນ`:`${a} / ${b} 分`],[/^คะแนนครั้งล่าสุด: (\d+)\/(\d+)$/,(a,b,l)=>l==='lo'?`ຄະແນນຫຼ້າສຸດ: ${a}/${b}`:`上次得分：${a}/${b}`],[/^ยังไม่มีคะแนน Quiz$/,(l)=>l==='lo'?'ຍັງບໍ່ມີຄະແນນແບບທົດສອບ':'暂无测验成绩'],[/^ตอบผิด (\d+) คำ — กลับไปทบทวนได้ด้านล่าง$/,(n,l)=>l==='lo'?`ຕອບຜິດ ${n} ຄຳ — ທົບທວນໄດ້ດ້ານລຸ່ມ`:`答错 ${n} 题 — 可在下方复习`],[/^กำลังบันทึก (\d+) \/ (\d+) คำ…$/,(a,b,l)=>l==='lo'?`ກຳລັງບັນທຶກ ${a} / ${b} ຄຳ…`:`正在保存 ${a} / ${b} 个词…`],[/^นำเข้าสำเร็จ (\d+) คำ$/,(n,l)=>l==='lo'?`ນຳເຂົ້າສຳເລັດ ${n} ຄຳ`:`成功导入 ${n} 个词`]];
 const fragments=[['ยังไม่ถูก — คำตอบคือ ','ຍັງບໍ່ຖືກ — ຄຳຕອບແມ່ນ ','回答不正确 — 正确答案是 '],['บันทึกคะแนนแล้ว','ບັນທຶກຄະແນນແລ້ວ','成绩已保存'],['ไม่สามารถยืนยันการบันทึกคะแนนได้','ບໍ່ສາມາດຢືນຢັນການບັນທຶກຄະແນນ','无法确认成绩已保存'],['ไม่สามารถบันทึกคะแนน: Canva Sheet เต็ม','ບັນທຶກຄະແນນບໍ່ໄດ້: Canva Sheet ເຕັມ','无法保存成绩：Canva Sheet 已满'],['ตรวจสอบข้อมูลไม่ผ่าน:','ກວດຂໍ້ມູນບໍ່ຜ່ານ:','数据验证失败：'],['ข้อมูลจากไฟล์ XLSX ที่แนบ:','ຂໍ້ມູນຈາກໄຟລ໌ XLSX ທີ່ແນບ:','附件 XLSX 数据：'],['รายการ (','ລາຍການ (','条（'],['รายการ','ລາຍການ','条'],['เพิ่ม 150','ເພີ່ມ 150','新增 150'],['เดิม 1650','ເດີມ 1650','原有 1650'],['รวม ','ລວມ ','总计 '],['เลข 序号 ซ้ำ','ເລກ 序号 ຊ້ຳ','重复序号'],['คำศัพท์ซ้ำ','ຄຳສັບຊ້ຳ','重复词汇'],['ช่องที่ไม่มีในไฟล์เว้นว่าง','ຊ່ອງທີ່ບໍ່ມີໃນໄຟລ໌ປະໄວ້ຫວ່າງ','文件缺失的字段留空'],['ไม่สามารถโหลดข้อมูลที่บันทึกไว้ได้ กรุณากด “ลองเชื่อมต่ออีกครั้ง”','ບໍ່ສາມາດໂຫຼດຂໍ້ມູນທີ່ບັນທຶກໄດ້ ກະລຸນາລອງເຊື່ອມຕໍ່ອີກ','无法加载已保存数据，请重试连接'],['จำนวนรายการจะเกินขีดจำกัด 999 รายการ','ຈຳນວນລາຍການຈະເກີນຂີດຈຳກັດ 999','记录将超过 999 条上限'],['ต้องมีคำศัพท์อย่างน้อย 4 คำเพื่อสร้างตัวเลือก Quiz','ຕ້ອງມີຄຳສັບຢ່າງໜ້ອຍ 4 ຄຳເພື່ອສ້າງແບບທົດສອບ','至少需要 4 个词汇才能生成测验选项']];
 fragments.push(...[['คำที่จำได้','ຄຳທີ່ຈື່ໄດ້','已掌握词汇'],['เข้าใช้ต่อเนื่อง','ເຂົ້າໃຊ້ຕໍ່ເນື່ອງ','连续使用'],['เป้าหมายต่อไป:','ເປົ້າໝາຍຕໍ່ໄປ:','下一个目标：'],['ได้รับฉายาระดับ','ໄດ້ຮັບສາຍາລະດັບ','获得称号等级'],['ต่อเนื่อง','ຕໍ່ເນື່ອງ','连续'],['ระดับ ','ລະດັບ ','等级 '],['จำได้ ','ຈື່ໄດ້ ','掌握 '],[' ครั้ง',' ຄັ້ງ',' 次'],[' วัน',' ມື້',' 天'],[' คำ',' ຄຳ',' 个词']]);
 fragments.push(...entries.slice(-12).map(([th,lo,zh])=>[th,lo,zh]));
 function translate(s){if(lang==='th')return s;let trimmed=s.trim();if(lang==='en'){let exact=english.get(trimmed);if(exact)return s.replace(trimmed,exact);let result=s;for(const [th,en] of english)if(th&&result.includes(th))result=result.split(th).join(en);return result}let found=map.get(trimmed);if(found)return s.replace(trimmed,found[lang]||trimmed);for(const [re,fn] of patterns){let m=trimmed.match(re);if(m)return s.replace(trimmed,fn(...m.slice(1),lang))}let result=s;for(const [th,lo,zh] of fragments)result=result.split(th).join(lang==='lo'?lo:zh);if(lang==='zh'){const more=[['ยังไม่ได้ตอบ','未作答'],['ใช้เวลา','用时'],['วินาที','秒'],['รวม','总计'],[' ข้อ',' 题'],['ข้อ ','第 '],['ตอบ:','作答：'],['คำตอบที่ถูก:','正确答案：'],['ไม่ได้ระบุ','未填写'],['แถว ','第 '],['ต้องเป็นจำนวนเต็มบวก','必须为正整数'],['ต้องมีคำศัพท์และคำแปลไทย','必须填写词汇和泰语翻译'],['เลขลำดับ','序号'],['ตกหล่น','缺失'],['ซ้ำใน Day','在 Day 中重复'],['หมดเวลา','时间到'],['สรุปผลการทดสอบ','测验结果'],['ผลรอบนี้ไม่บันทึกลง Canva Sheet','本次结果未保存到 Canva Sheet'],['บันทึกได้','已保存'],['แต่ไม่สามารถยืนยันคำถัดไปได้ โปรดรีเฟรชก่อนดำเนินการต่อ','但无法确认后续词汇，请刷新后继续'],['中文解释:','中文释义：'],['Quiz','测验'],['Flashcard','单词卡']];for(const [a,b] of more)result=result.split(a).join(b)}return result}
 const originals=new WeakMap(),shown=new WeakMap();function protectedNode(node){let el=node.parentElement;return !!el?.closest('#card-hanzi,#card-pinyin,#card-thai,#card-type,#card-day,#quiz-word,#quiz-options,.vocab-row h3')}
 function text(node){if(protectedNode(node))return;let current=node.nodeValue;if(!current.trim())return;let source=originals.get(node);if(source===undefined||current!==shown.get(node)){source=current;originals.set(node,source)}let output=translate(source);shown.set(node,output);if(current!==output)node.nodeValue=output}
 function walk(root){if(root.nodeType===3){text(root);return}if(root.nodeType!==1||root.matches('script,style'))return;const elements=[root,...root.querySelectorAll('*')];for(const el of elements){if(el.matches('script,style'))continue;for(const attr of ['placeholder','aria-label','title'])if(el.hasAttribute(attr)&&el.id!=='interface-language'){let key='data-i18n-'+attr,source=el.getAttribute(key),current=el.getAttribute(attr),last=el.getAttribute(key+'-shown');if(source===null||current!==last){source=current;el.setAttribute(key,source)}const output=translate(source);el.setAttribute(key+'-shown',output);if(current!==output)el.setAttribute(attr,output)}}let w=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);while(w.nextNode())text(w.currentNode)}
 const select=document.getElementById('interface-language');select.value=lang;document.documentElement.lang=lang;walk(document.body);const observer=new MutationObserver(list=>{for(const mutation of list){if(mutation.type==='characterData')text(mutation.target);else if(mutation.type==='attributes')walk(mutation.target);else mutation.addedNodes.forEach(walk)}});observer.observe(document.body,{subtree:true,childList:true,characterData:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['placeholder','aria-label','title']});select.addEventListener('change',()=>{lang=select.value;document.documentElement.lang=lang;try{localStorage.setItem('vocab-interface-language',lang)}catch{}walk(document.body)});
 })();
 </script>

ຫຼື ສຳເນົາຂໍ້ມູນ CSV / CSV / Excel 数据','导入数据','导出数据','设置']};copy.en=['Settings','Make your learning space yours','Appearance and language','Website language','Display mode','Light mode','Dark mode','Learning','Daily goal (words)','Default study mode','Study in order','Shuffle words','Review due words','Sound and reminders','Speech speed','Slow','Normal','Fast','Remind me of my goal when opening the site','Off','On','Your data','Import and preview vocabulary, or copy CSV / Excel data','Import data','Export data','Settings'];const ids=['settings-title','settings-intro','settings-appearance-title','settings-language-label','settings-theme-label','#settings-theme option:nth-child(1)','#settings-theme option:nth-child(2)','settings-learning-title','settings-goal-label','settings-mode-label','#settings-mode option:nth-child(1)','#settings-mode option:nth-child(2)','#settings-mode option:nth-child(3)','settings-audio-title','settings-voice-label','#settings-voice option:nth-child(1)','#settings-voice option:nth-child(2)','#settings-voice option:nth-child(2)','settings-data-title','settings-data-desc','settings-import','settings-export','settings-nav'];function update(){let language=document.getElementById('interface-language').value||'th';ids.forEach((id,i)=>{let el=id.startsWith('#')?document.querySelector(id):document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;let icon=el.querySelector('svg');el.textContent=(copy[language]||copy.th)[i];if(icon)el.prepend(icon)})}document.getElementById('interface-language').addEventListener('change',update);update()})();
