  <script>
   (()=>{const control=document.getElementById('world-motion');const language=document.getElementById('interface-language');let saved='on';try{saved=localStorage.getItem('zran4h-world-motion')||'on'}catch{}control.value=saved==='off'?'off':'on';function apply(){document.body.classList.toggle('world-paused',control.value==='off')}function labels(){const copy={th:['ภาพเคลื่อนไหวพื้นหลัง','เปิด','ปิด'],lo:['ພາບເຄື່ອນໄຫວພື້ນຫຼັງ','ເປີດ','ປິດ'],zh:['背景动画','开启','关闭']}[language.value]||['ภาพเคลื่อนไหวพื้นหลัง','เปิด','ปิด'];document.getElementById('world-motion-label').textContent=copy[0];control.options[0].textContent=copy[1];control.options[1].textContent=copy[2]}control.addEventListener('change',()=>{apply();try{localStorage.setItem('zran4h-world-motion',control.value)}catch{}});language.addEventListener('change',labels);apply();labels()})();
  </script>
  <style>
  .learner-chip{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;border:1px solid rgba(117,227,244,.35);background:rgba(42,62,112,.48);border-radius:18px;padding:7px 12px;color:#fff;min-width:0;max-width:310px;text-align:left;backdrop-filter:blur(16px)}.learner-chip:hover{border-color:#8cebf6;box-shadow:0 0 22px rgba(67,220,242,.2)}.learner-avatar{width:44px;height:44px;flex:none;border-radius:50%;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:25px;background:#202a58;border:2px solid var(--aura,#67ddeb);box-shadow:0 0 15px var(--aura,#67ddeb),inset 0 0 12px rgba(255,255,255,.12);overflow:hidden}.learner-avatar canvas{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover}.learner-info{min-width:0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap}.learner-name{display:inline-block;font-weight:800;font-size:14px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;background:linear-gradient(100deg,#fff 15%,var(--aura,#83eafa) 45%,#fff 60%,#c9b5ff 85%);background-size:250% auto;background-clip:text;-webkit-background-clip:text;color:transparent;animation:learner-shimmer 5s linear infinite}.learner-title{display:inline-block;max-width:100%;font-size:10px;color:#e4d9ff;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:20px;border:1px solid var(--aura,#67ddeb);background:rgba(105,95,186,.22);box-shadow:0 0 9px rgba(128,181,249,.2)}.learner-chip[data-tier="high"]{--aura:#eac47b}.learner-chip[data-tier="mid"]{--aura:#baa0ff}@keyframes learner-shimmer{to{background-position:250% center}}.learner-dialog{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:3000;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:18px;background:rgba(4,8,30,.78);backdrop-filter:blur(13px)}.learner-dialog[hidden]{display:none}.learner-box{width:min(100%,480px);max-height:min(90vh,760px);overflow:auto;position:relative;padding:32px;border-radius:26px;border:1px solid rgba(116,215,250,.32);background:linear-gradient(145deg,rgba(29,43,91,.98),rgba(15,22,57,.98));box-shadow:0 28px 90px rgba(0,0,0,.5),0 0 45px rgba(85,90,224,.2);color:#f5f8ff}.learner-box h2{font-size:25px;margin:5px 0 10px}.learner-box p{color:#b8c9e7}.learner-box label{display:block;font-weight:700;margin:18px 0 8px}.learner-box input[type=text]{width:100%;padding:13px 15px;border-radius:12px;border:1px solid #6682bb;background:#101b42;color:#fff}.learner-box input[type=file]{width:100%;color:#cbd8f5}.avatar-choices{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:9px}.avatar-choice{width:47px;height:47px;border-radius:50%;border:1px solid #6375af;background:#273b74;font-size:24px}.avatar-choice[aria-pressed=true]{border:2px solid #77ebf0;box-shadow:0 0 14px #53cfe8}.learner-actions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;margin-top:23px}.learner-actions button{min-height:42px}.learner-close{position:absolute;right:19px;top:15px;border:0;background:transparent;color:#dceaff;font-size:27px}.learner-progress{margin:16px 0}.learner-progress .track{margin:9px 0}.title-list{list-style:none;padding:0;margin:14px 0;display:grid;gap:8px}.title-list li{padding:11px 13px;border:1px solid rgba(145,175,235,.22);border-radius:12px;background:rgba(95,113,180,.12);color:#b8c5e0}.title-list li.unlocked{color:#f5f7ff;border-color:rgba(99,228,239,.52);background:rgba(71,201,224,.15)}.title-list b{display:block}.title-list small{font-size:11px}.level-celebration{text-align:center}.level-celebration .sparkle{font-size:50px;animation:celebrate-pop .8s ease infinite alternate}@keyframes celebrate-pop{to{transform:scale(1.18) rotate(8deg);filter:drop-shadow(0 0 20px #9ce9ff)}}.top .learner-chip{margin-left:4px}@media(max-width:760px){.top .learner-chip{order:4;max-width:none;flex:1 1 100%}.learner-box{padding:25px 20px}}@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.learner-name,.level-celebration .sparkle{animation:none}}
  .top{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:24px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding-top:24px;padding-bottom:24px}.top .brand{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:15px;min-width:0;flex:0 1 auto;letter-spacing:0}.top .seal{flex:0 0 48px;width:48px;height:48px;line-height:1}.brand-copy{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:2px;min-width:0;line-height:1.4}.brand-copy .brand-name{font-size:23px;line-height:1.2;white-space:nowrap}.brand-copy small{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:500;color:var(--muted);white-space:normal}.brand-copy .top-note{display:block;font-size:12px;line-height:1.5;font-weight:500;letter-spacing:0;white-space:normal}.top-right{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-end;gap:16px;flex:1 1 auto;min-width:0;flex-wrap:wrap}.top-right .learner-chip{order:0;margin-left:0;max-width:340px;flex:0 1 auto;position:relative;z-index:2;min-height:48px;touch-action:manipulation;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:rgba(140,235,246,.25)}.top-right .language-control{order:1;margin-left:0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:flex-end;white-space:normal}.top-right .language-control label{white-space:nowrap}.top-right .learner-info{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:3px;flex-wrap:nowrap}.top-right .learner-name{max-width:100%}.top-right .learner-title{white-space:normal;overflow:visible;text-overflow:clip;line-height:1.4}@media(max-width:1100px){.top-right{flex-basis:100%;justify-content:flex-start}.top-right .language-control{justify-content:flex-start}}@media(max-width:760px){.top{gap:16px}.top .brand{width:100%;flex:none}.top-right{width:100%;gap:12px}.top-right .learner-chip{order:0;flex:0 1 auto;max-width:100%}.top-right .language-control{order:1;width:auto;justify-content:flex-start}}@media(max-width:420px){.top-right .learner-chip{width:100%}.top-right .language-control{width:100%}}
  </style>
  <style>
  /* Keep learner dialogs above the entire page, independent of page panels. */
  body.learner-modal-open{overflow:hidden}
  .learner-dialog{position:fixed!important;inset:0!important;z-index:100000!important;display:flex!important;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:100vw;height:100dvh;padding:max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-top)) max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-right)) max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-bottom)) max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-left));overflow:hidden;overscroll-behavior:contain;background:rgba(5,10,30,.82);isolation:isolate}
  .learner-dialog[hidden]{display:none!important}
  .learner-dialog>.learner-box{position:relative;z-index:1;flex:none;width:min(100%,680px);max-width:100%;max-height:calc(100dvh - 32px);min-height:0;overflow-y:auto;overscroll-behavior:contain;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;padding:32px;border-radius:24px;background:#182750!important;color:#f5f8ff!important;transform:none!important;box-shadow:0 25px 80px rgba(0,0,0,.55)!important}
  .learner-dialog>.learner-box:hover{transform:none!important}
  #titles-dialog .learner-box{width:min(100%,760px)}
  #titles-dialog .title-list{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));gap:10px}
  #titles-dialog .title-list li{min-width:0;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;line-height:1.5;background:#263965;color:#f3f7ff}
  #titles-dialog .title-list li.locked{color:#cfdaef;background:#202e52}
  #titles-dialog .title-list b{overflow-wrap:anywhere}
  #titles-dialog .title-medal{font-size:22px;flex:none}
  #titles-dialog .title-condition{margin-top:7px;font-size:12px}
  #titles-dialog .title-condition .track{margin:5px 0 0}
  #titles-dialog .learner-progress small{display:block;line-height:1.6;color:#e5edff}
  #titles-dialog .learner-close{position:sticky;float:right;top:0;right:0;z-index:2;display:grid;place-items:center;width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:12px;background:#334b80;color:white;line-height:1;touch-action:manipulation}
  #titles-dialog .learner-close:hover,#titles-dialog .learner-close:focus-visible{background:#4667a6}
  #titles-dialog .btn.secondary{background:#dcecff;color:#172b50;min-height:44px}
  @media(max-width:700px){#titles-dialog .title-list{grid-template-columns:1fr}.learner-dialog{align-items:center;padding:12px max(12px,env(safe-area-inset-right)) max(12px,env(safe-area-inset-bottom)) max(12px,env(safe-area-inset-left))}.learner-dialog>.learner-box{max-height:calc(100dvh - 24px);padding:22px 17px;border-radius:18px}#titles-dialog .learner-box h2{font-size:23px}#titles-dialog .title-list li{padding:11px}}
  </style>
  <div id="learner-entry" class="learner-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="learner-entry-title" hidden>
   <div class="learner-box canva-panel"><span class="eyebrow">WELCOME TO ZRAN4H</span>
    <h2 id="learner-entry-title">ยินดีต้อนรับสู่ Zran4h</h2>
    <form id="learner-form"><label for="learner-input">ชื่อของคุณ</label><input id="learner-input" class="canva-input" type="text" maxlength="40" autocomplete="nickname"><label>เลือกรูปโปรไฟล์</label>
     <div id="learner-choices" class="avatar-choices" role="group" aria-label="เลือกรูปโปรไฟล์"></div><label for="learner-file">หรืออัปโหลดรูปของคุณ</label><input id="learner-file" class="canva-input" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif">
     <div class="learner-actions"><button id="learner-submit" class="btn canva-button" type="submit">เข้าสู่เว็บ →</button><button id="learner-cancel" class="btn secondary canva-button" type="button" hidden>ยกเลิก</button><button id="learner-logout" class="btn secondary canva-button" type="button" hidden>ออกจากระบบ</button>
     </div>
    </form>
   </div>
  </div>
  <div id="titles-dialog" class="learner-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="titles-heading" hidden>
   <div class="learner-box canva-panel"><button id="titles-close" class="learner-close canva-button" type="button" aria-label="ปิด">×</button><span class="eyebrow">THE TITLE COLLECTION</span>
    <h2 id="titles-heading">เส้นทาง 12 ฉายา</h2>
    <p id="titles-summary"></p>
    <div class="learner-progress">
     <div class="track">
      <div id="titles-progress" class="fill"></div>
     </div><small id="titles-next"></small>
    </div>
    <ol id="titles-list" class="title-list canva-list"></ol><button id="titles-exit" class="btn secondary canva-button" type="button">กลับหน้าหลัก</button>
   </div>
  </div>
  <div id="level-dialog" class="learner-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="level-heading" hidden>
   <div class="learner-box level-celebration canva-panel">
    <div class="sparkle" aria-hidden="true">
     ✦ ✧ ✦
    </div><span class="eyebrow">TITLE UNLOCKED</span>
    <h2 id="level-heading">ปลดล็อกฉายาใหม่!</h2>
    <p id="level-description"></p><button id="level-close" class="btn canva-button" type="button">สุดยอด! ไปเรียนต่อ</button>
   </div>
  </div>
  <script>
  (()=>{
    const $=id=>document.getElementById(id), titles=[['ผู้เริ่มต้นแห่งอักษรจีน','初学者','Chinese Beginner',{},'🎋'],['ศิษย์พู่กันหมึก','笔墨学徒','Ink Brush Apprentice',{known:50},'🖌️'],['นักล่าอักษร','字猎人','Character Hunter',{known:150,streak:3},'📜'],['ผู้ฟังสี่เสียง','四声听者','Four-Tone Listener',{known:300,audio:100},'🎧'],['กระบี่พู่กัน','剑笔客','Brush Swordsman',{known:500,streak:7},'⚔️'],['นักเดินทางสายหยก','玉路行者','Jade Path Wanderer',{known:700,srs:300},'🟢'],['จอมยุทธ์พินอิน','拼音侠','Pinyin Hero',{known:900,pinyin:200},'💠'],['มังกรเหนือทะเลหมึก','墨海游龙','Ink Sea Dragon',{known:1100,streak:14},'🐉'],['บัณฑิตภูผาสายน้ำ','山河学士','Mountain Scholar',{known:1300,perfect:5},'🏔️'],['ทัพหน้าทะลวงค่าย','破阵先锋','Vanguard',{known:1500,streak:30},'🛡️'],['ปรมาจารย์ภาษาจีน','汉语宗师','Chinese Grandmaster',{known:1700,accuracy:85},'👑'],['ตำนานแห่งมังกรอักษร','龙字传说','Legend of the Dragon Script',{known:1800},'🐲']];
    const avatars=['📚','🐼','🐉','🌙','⭐','🦊'];let profile=null,selected=avatars[0],pendingPhoto=null,previousFocus=null,announcements=[];
    const key='zran4h-learner-profile-v1',today=()=>{const d=new Date();return [d.getFullYear(),String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')].join('-')};
    function load(){try{const p=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key));if(p&&typeof p.name==='string'&&p.name.trim())return {...p,known:Array.isArray(p.known)?p.known:[],visits:Array.isArray(p.visits)?p.visits:[],quizzes:Number(p.quizzes)||0,audio:Number(p.audio)||0,srs:Number(p.srs)||0,pinyin:Number(p.pinyin)||0,perfect:Number(p.perfect)||0,level:Math.min(12,Math.max(1,Number(p.level)||1))}}catch{}return null}
    function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(profile))}catch{}return true}
    function streak(){const days=new Set(profile?.visits||[]);let d=new Date(),n=0;if(!days.has(today()))d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);while(days.has([d.getFullYear(),String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')].join('-'))){n++;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1)}return n}
    function metrics(){let rows=window.zranTitleRecords||[];let marks=rows.filter(r=>r.record_type==='mark'&&r.status==='known').map(r=>String(r.record_key));let scores=rows.filter(r=>r.record_type==='score');let known=new Set([...profile.known,...marks]);let total=scores.reduce((n,r)=>n+(Number(r.payload)||0),0),right=scores.reduce((n,r)=>n+(Number(r.score)||0),0);return {known:known.size,streak:streak(),audio:profile.audio||0,srs:profile.srs||0,pinyin:profile.pinyin||0,perfect:Math.max(profile.perfect||0,scores.filter(r=>Number(r.payload)>0&&Number(r.score)===Number(r.payload)).length),accuracy:total?Math.round(right/total*100):0}}function earned(){let level=1,m=metrics();for(let i=1;i<titles.length;i++){if(Object.entries(titles[i][3]).every(([key,target])=>m[key]>=target))level=i+1;else break}return level}
    function titleName(i){let lang=$('interface-language').value;return titles[i][lang==='zh'?1:lang==='en'?2:0]}function paintAvatar(el,p){el.replaceChildren();if(p.photo){const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=canvas.height=96;el.append(canvas);const im=new Image();im.onload=()=>canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(im,0,0,96,96);im.src=p.photo}else el.textContent=p.avatar||avatars[0]}
    function refresh(){const chip=$('learner-chip');chip.hidden=false;if(!profile){$('learner-chip-avatar').textContent='📚';$('learner-chip-name').textContent='โปรไฟล์ผู้เรียน';$('learner-chip-title').textContent='แตะเพื่อตั้งค่า (ไม่บังคับ)';return}chip.dataset.tier=profile.level>=10?'high':profile.level>=5?'mid':'low';paintAvatar($('learner-chip-avatar'),profile);$('learner-chip-name').textContent=profile.name;$('learner-chip-title').textContent=`✦ Lv.${profile.level} · ${titleName(profile.level-1)}`;if($('dashboard-title'))$('dashboard-title').textContent=`✦ Lv.${profile.level} · ${titleName(profile.level-1)} · ${titles[profile.level-1][1]}`}
    function celebrate(level){announcements.push(level);if(document.querySelector('.learner-dialog:not([hidden])'))return;let next=announcements.shift();if(!next)return;$('level-description').textContent=`${profile.name} ได้รับฉายาระดับ ${next} · ${titles[next-1][0]} (${titles[next-1][1]})`;open($('level-dialog'))}
    function assess(){if(!profile)return;const level=earned(),old=profile.level||1;if(level>old){profile.level=level;if(save()){refresh();for(let n=old+1;n<=level;n++)celebrate(n)}}else refresh()}
    function visit(){if(!profile)return;profile.visits=[...new Set([...profile.visits,today()])].sort();save();assess()}
    function open(dialog){if(!dialog.hidden)return;const active=document.querySelector('.learner-dialog:not([hidden])');if(active&&active!==dialog)return;previousFocus=document.activeElement;dialog.hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('learner-modal-open');document.querySelector('.app')?.setAttribute('inert','');dialog.querySelector('button:not([hidden]),input:not([hidden])')?.focus()}
    function close(dialog){if(dialog.hidden)return;dialog.hidden=true;if(dialog.id==='titles-dialog')chip.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');if(!document.querySelector('.learner-dialog:not([hidden])')){document.body.classList.remove('learner-modal-open');document.querySelector('.app')?.removeAttribute('inert')}if(previousFocus?.isConnected)previousFocus.focus();else chip.focus()}
    function edit(first=false){$('learner-entry-title').textContent=first?'ยินดีต้อนรับสู่ Zran4h':'แก้ไขโปรไฟล์ผู้เรียน';$('learner-submit').textContent=first?'เข้าสู่เว็บ →':'บันทึกโปรไฟล์';$('learner-cancel').hidden=false;$('learner-logout').hidden=first;$('learner-input').value=first?'':profile.name;$('learner-file').value='';selected=first?avatars[0]:(profile.avatar||avatars[0]);pendingPhoto=first?null:profile.photo||null;choices();open($('learner-entry'))}
    function choices(){$('learner-choices').innerHTML='';avatars.forEach(emoji=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='avatar-choice canva-button';b.textContent=emoji;b.setAttribute('aria-label','รูป '+emoji);b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!pendingPhoto&&selected===emoji));b.onclick=()=>{selected=emoji;pendingPhoto=null;$('learner-file').value='';choices()};$('learner-choices').append(b)})}
    const chip=document.createElement('button');chip.id='learner-chip';chip.type='button';chip.className='learner-chip canva-card';chip.setAttribute('aria-label','เปิดฉายาของฉัน');chip.setAttribute('aria-haspopup','dialog');chip.setAttribute('aria-controls','titles-dialog');chip.innerHTML='<span id="learner-chip-avatar" class="learner-avatar"></span><span class="learner-info"><span id="learner-chip-name" class="learner-name"></span><span id="learner-chip-title" class="learner-title"></span></span>';document.querySelector('.top-right').prepend(chip);
    const view=document.createElement('button');view.id='learner-view-titles';view.type='button';view.className='btn secondary small canva-button';view.textContent='✦ ดูฉายาทั้งหมด';$('dashboard').querySelector('.heading').after(view);function showTitles(){if(!profile){edit(true);return}assess();const level=profile.level,m=metrics(),lang=$('interface-language').value,labels={th:{known:'จำได้',streak:'เรียนต่อเนื่อง',audio:'ฟังถูก',srs:'ทบทวน SRS',pinyin:'พิมพ์พินอินถูก',perfect:'Quiz 100%',accuracy:'ความแม่นยำ Quiz',missing:'ขาดอีก',next:'ฉายาถัดไป',unit:'คำ'},zh:{known:'已掌握',streak:'连续学习',audio:'听力答对',srs:'SRS 复习',pinyin:'拼音输入正确',perfect:'满分测验',accuracy:'测验正确率',missing:'还差',next:'下一称号',unit:'词'},en:{known:'Known',streak:'Study streak',audio:'Listening correct',srs:'SRS reviews',pinyin:'Pinyin typed correctly',perfect:'Perfect quizzes',accuracy:'Quiz accuracy',missing:'Need',next:'Next title',unit:'words'}},t=labels[lang]||labels.th;let conditions=(i)=>Object.entries(titles[i][3]).map(([k,v])=>{let n=m[k],difference=Math.max(0,v-n);return `<div class="title-condition"><span>${t[k]} ${n}/${v}${k==='accuracy'?'%':''}${difference?' · '+t.missing+' '+difference+(k==='accuracy'?'%':''):''}</span><div class="track"><div class="fill" style="width:${Math.min(100,n/v*100)}%"></div></div></div>`}).join('');$('titles-heading').textContent=lang==='zh'?'我的称号':lang==='en'?'My Titles':'ฉายาของฉัน';$('titles-summary').textContent=`${t.known} ${m.known}/1800 ${t.unit} · ${t.streak} ${m.streak}`;$('titles-list').innerHTML=titles.map((row,i)=>`<li class="${i<level?'unlocked':'locked'} tier-${Math.floor(i/3)}"><span class="title-medal" aria-hidden="true">${row[4]}</span><div><b>Lv.${i+1} · ${titleName(i)} <span lang="zh">${row[1]}</span></b>${conditions(i)}</div></li>`).join('');$('titles-progress').style.width=(level/12*100)+'%';$('titles-next').innerHTML=level<12?`${t.next}: ${titleName(level)} · ${Object.entries(titles[level][3]).filter(([k,v])=>m[k]<v).map(([k,v])=>`${t[k]} ${t.missing} ${v-m[k]}${k==='accuracy'?'%':''}`).join(' · ')}`:lang==='zh'?'已解锁全部 12 个称号！':lang==='en'?'All 12 titles unlocked!':'ปลดล็อกครบทั้ง 12 ฉายาแล้ว!';open($('titles-dialog'));chip.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true')}document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const target=e.target.closest?.('#learner-chip,#learner-view-titles,#titles-close,#titles-exit,#titles-dialog');if(!target)return;if(target.id==='learner-chip'||target.id==='learner-view-titles'){e.preventDefault();if(!document.querySelector('.learner-dialog:not([hidden])'))showTitles()}else if(target.id==='titles-close'||target.id==='titles-exit'||(target.id==='titles-dialog'&&e.target===target)){e.preventDefault();close($('titles-dialog'))}});
    $('learner-file').onchange=async e=>{const file=e.target.files[0];if(!file)return;if(!file.type.startsWith('image/')||file.size>8*1024*1024){e.target.value='';return}try{const bitmap=await createImageBitmap(file),canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=canvas.height=160;const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'),side=Math.min(bitmap.width,bitmap.height);ctx.drawImage(bitmap,(bitmap.width-side)/2,(bitmap.height-side)/2,side,side,0,0,160,160);bitmap.close();pendingPhoto=canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.75);choices()}catch{e.target.value=''}};
    $('learner-form').onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();const name=$('learner-input').value.trim();const old=profile;profile={name:name||'ผู้เรียน',avatar:selected,photo:pendingPhoto,known:old?.known||[],quizzes:old?.quizzes||0,audio:old?.audio||0,srs:old?.srs||0,pinyin:old?.pinyin||0,perfect:old?.perfect||0,visits:old?.visits||[],level:old?.level||1};if(!save()){profile=old;return}close($('learner-entry'));visit()};$('learner-cancel').onclick=()=>close($('learner-entry'));function signOut(){profile=null;announcements=[];try{localStorage.removeItem(key)}catch{}$('learner-entry').hidden=true;$('titles-dialog').hidden=true;$('level-dialog').hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('learner-modal-open');document.querySelector('.app')?.removeAttribute('inert');refresh();edit(true)}$('learner-logout').onclick=signOut;
    $('level-close').onclick=()=>{close($('level-dialog'));if(announcements.length){const next=announcements.shift();$('level-description').textContent=`${profile.name} ได้รับฉายาระดับ ${next} · ${titles[next-1][0]} (${titles[next-1][1]})`;open($('level-dialog'))}else chip.focus()};document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{const dialog=['level-dialog','titles-dialog','learner-entry'].map($).find(x=>!x.hidden);if(!dialog)return;if(e.key==='Escape'&&dialog.id!=='learner-entry'){close(dialog);e.preventDefault()}if(e.key==='Tab'){const controls=[...dialog.querySelectorAll('button:not([hidden]):not(:disabled),input:not([hidden])')],first=controls[0],last=controls.at(-1);if(e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){e.preventDefault();last.focus()}else if(!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){e.preventDefault();first.focus()}}});
    document.addEventListener('zran-mastered',e=>{if(!profile||profile.known.includes(e.detail))return;profile.known.push(e.detail);save();assess()});document.addEventListener('zran-quiz-complete',e=>{if(!profile)return;profile.quizzes++;if(e.detail.total>0&&e.detail.score===e.detail.total)profile.perfect++;save();assess()});document.addEventListener('zran-quiz-answer',e=>{if(!profile||!e.detail.correct)return;if(e.detail.mode==='audio')profile.audio++;if(e.detail.mode==='pinyin-type')profile.pinyin++;save();assess()});document.addEventListener('zran-srs-review',()=>{if(!profile)return;profile.srs++;save();assess()});document.addEventListener('zran-records',e=>{window.zranTitleRecords=e.detail;if(profile)assess()});$('interface-language').addEventListener('change',()=>{refresh();if(!$('titles-dialog').hidden)showTitles();if(!$('learner-entry').hidden){const first=!profile;$('learner-entry-title').textContent=first?'ยินดีต้อนรับสู่ Zran4h':'แก้ไขโปรไฟล์ผู้เรียน';$('learner-submit').textContent=first?'เข้าสู่เว็บ →':'บันทึกโปรไฟล์'}if(!$('level-dialog').hidden&&profile){const level=profile.level;$('level-description').textContent=`${profile.name} ได้รับฉายาระดับ ${level} · ${titleName(level-1)} (${titles[level-1][1]})`}});profile=load();refresh();if(profile)visit();
  })();
  </script><!-- Decor add-on: independent of vocabulary data -->
  <style>
  [hidden]{display:none!important}#bg{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:-1;overflow:hidden;pointer-events:none}html.deco,html.deco body{background:transparent!important}#bg::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--tint,transparent)}#bg>*{position:absolute}
  .cl,.fi{left:-200px;animation:drift var(--d) linear infinite}.cl svg,.fi svg{width:100%;height:auto;display:block}@keyframes drift{to{transform:translateX(calc(100vw + 400px))}}
  .pt{top:-30px;width:var(--s);height:calc(var(--s)*.7);background:#ffb3cf;border-radius:150% 0 150% 0;animation:fall var(--d) linear infinite}@keyframes fall{to{transform:translate(var(--x),110vh) rotate(540deg)}}
  .st{width:var(--s);height:var(--s);background:#fff;border-radius:50%;animation:tw 2.4s ease-in-out infinite alternate}@keyframes tw{from{opacity:.15;transform:scale(.6)}to{opacity:1;transform:scale(1.25)}}
  .sh{width:90px;height:2px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,#fff);opacity:0;animation:shoot 7s linear infinite}@keyframes shoot{0%{opacity:0;transform:translate(0,0) rotate(25deg)}4%{opacity:1}18%{opacity:0;transform:translate(280px,130px) rotate(25deg)}100%{opacity:0}}
  .bb{bottom:-50px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid rgba(255,255,255,.75);background:rgba(255,255,255,.2);animation:rise var(--d) ease-in infinite}@keyframes rise{to{transform:translate(var(--x),-115vh)}}
  .mo{top:7%;right:7%;width:84px;animation:bob 4s ease-in-out infinite}@keyframes bob{50%{transform:translateY(-9px)}}
  .bu{position:fixed;width:12px;height:12px;margin:-6px 0 0 -6px;clip-path:polygon(50% 0,62% 38%,100% 50%,62% 62%,50% 100%,38% 62%,0 50%,38% 38%);z-index:30;pointer-events:none;animation:bu .8s ease-out forwards}@keyframes bu{to{transform:translate(var(--dx),var(--dy)) scale(.2) rotate(200deg);opacity:0}}
  #pd{position:fixed;left:12px;bottom:calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));width:62px;height:62px;border:0;padding:0;background:transparent;z-index:14;animation:bob 2.6s ease-in-out infinite;filter:drop-shadow(0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.25))}
  #fb{position:fixed;right:12px;bottom:calc(16px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));z-index:15;border:0;border-radius:999px;padding:12px 18px;font-weight:700;background:#1E7A68;color:#fff;box-shadow:0 12px 32px rgba(20,32,46,.25)}
  #dp{position:fixed;right:12px;bottom:calc(74px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));width:min(330px,calc(100vw - 24px));max-height:70vh;overflow:auto;background:#fff;border-radius:20px;box-shadow:0 12px 32px rgba(20,32,46,.25);padding:14px;z-index:15;color:#14202E}#dp,#dp *{box-sizing:border-box;font-family:inherit}#dp b{display:block;margin:6px 0}#dp .mrow{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin-top:10px}#dp input[type=range]{flex:1;accent-color:#1E7A68}#dp .chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}#dp .chip{border:1.5px solid #C9D8D4;background:#fff;border-radius:999px;padding:6px 12px;font-size:14px;color:#14202E}#dp .chip[aria-pressed=true]{background:#1E7A68;border-color:#1E7A68;color:#fff}#dp select{width:100%;padding:10px;border-radius:12px;border:1.5px solid #C9D8D4;background:#fff;color:#14202E}#dp .btn{border:0;border-radius:14px;font-weight:700;background:#1E7A68;color:#fff;padding:10px 14px;white-space:nowrap}#dp .sub{color:#5A6B77}#tt{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:90px;transform:translate(-50%,30px);background:#14202E;color:#fff;padding:10px 16px;border-radius:12px;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;transition:.25s;z-index:40;max-width:90vw}#tt.on{opacity:1;transform:translate(-50%,0)}#fb:focus-visible,#pd:focus-visible,#dp button:focus-visible,#dp select:focus-visible,#dp input:focus-visible{outline:3px solid #f9ad52;outline-offset:3px}@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){#bg *,#pd,.bu{animation:none!important}}
  </style>
  <script>
  (function(){
  const CFG={darkUI:true,knownText:['จำได้','Known']},$=s=>document.querySelector(s);
  const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.innerHTML='<div id="bg" aria-hidden="true"></div>'+'<button id="pd" class="canva-button" type="button" aria-label="แพนด้าให้กำลังใจ" hidden><svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="22" cy="24" r="12" fill="#222"/><circle cx="78" cy="24" r="12" fill="#222"/><circle cx="50" cy="54" r="36" fill="#fff" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/><ellipse cx="36" cy="50" rx="9" ry="12" fill="#222" transform="rotate(20 36 50)"/><ellipse cx="64" cy="50" rx="9" ry="12" fill="#222" transform="rotate(-20 64 50)"/><circle cx="37" cy="50" r="3.5" fill="#fff"/><circle cx="63" cy="50" r="3.5" fill="#fff"/><ellipse cx="50" cy="64" rx="5" ry="3.5" fill="#222"/><path d="M44 71q6 6 12 0" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/><circle cx="26" cy="66" r="5" fill="#f99" opacity=".6"/><circle cx="74" cy="66" r="5" fill="#f99" opacity=".6"/></svg></button>'+'<button id="fb" class="canva-button" type="button" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="dp">ตกแต่ง / เพลง</button>'+'<div id="dp" class="canva-panel" hidden><b>พื้นหลังการ์ตูน</b><div class="chips" id="tc"></div><b>เพลงประกอบ</b><select id="tr" class="canva-dropdown" aria-label="เลือกเพลง"></select><div class="mrow"><button class="btn canva-button" id="pl" type="button">เปิดเพลง</button><input class="canva-input" type="range" id="vol" min="0" max="1" step=".05" aria-label="ระดับเสียงเพลง"></div><p class="sub" style="margin:10px 0 0;font-size:13px">กดปุ่มเปิดเพลงเพื่อเริ่ม กดอีกครั้งเพื่อปิด</p></div>'+'<div id="tt" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>';while(wrap.firstChild)document.body.appendChild(wrap.firstChild);
  function toast(t){const e=$('#tt');e.textContent=t;e.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(toast.t);toast.t=setTimeout(()=>e.classList.remove('on'),2200)}
  let D={th:'sky',tr:0,vol:.5};try{Object.assign(D,JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('hsk6fc_deco')||'{}'))}catch(e){};
  const dsave=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem('hsk6fc_deco',JSON.stringify(D))}catch(e){}},rnd=(a,b)=>a+Math.random()*(b-a);
  const TH={sky:'ท้องฟ้า',sakura:'ซากุระ',space:'อวกาศ',sea:'ใต้ทะเล',none:'ไม่มีพื้นหลัง'},G={sky:'linear-gradient(#bfe6ff,#e8f7ff 60%,#d9f2d0)',sakura:'linear-gradient(#ffd9e8,#fff1f6 55%,#ffe0c8)',space:'radial-gradient(at 20% 10%,#3a2a7a,#0e0a2b 70%)',sea:'linear-gradient(#aef0ee,#52bfdc 55%,#2a86b3)'};
  const CLOUD='<svg viewBox="0 0 120 70"><g fill="#fff"><circle cx="35" cy="42" r="22"/><circle cx="62" cy="30" r="26"/><circle cx="90" cy="44" r="20"/><rect x="30" y="42" width="65" height="22" rx="11"/></g><circle cx="52" cy="46" r="2.5" fill="#345"/><circle cx="72" cy="46" r="2.5" fill="#345"/><path d="M57 52q5 5 10 0" stroke="#345" fill="none" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/><circle cx="46" cy="52" r="4" fill="#fbb" opacity=".7"/><circle cx="78" cy="52" r="4" fill="#fbb" opacity=".7"/></svg>';
  const FISH='<svg viewBox="0 0 60 36"><ellipse cx="26" cy="18" rx="22" ry="13" fill="#ff9f68"/><path d="M46 18 60 6v24z" fill="#ff7a45"/><circle cx="14" cy="14" r="3" fill="#fff"/><circle cx="14" cy="14" r="1.5" fill="#234"/></svg>',MOON='<svg viewBox="0 0 80 80"><circle cx="40" cy="40" r="34" fill="#ffe9a3"/><circle cx="28" cy="46" r="4" fill="#f3d37a"/><circle cx="54" cy="30" r="5" fill="#f3d37a"/><circle cx="32" cy="36" r="3" fill="#345"/><circle cx="50" cy="36" r="3" fill="#345"/><path d="M34 46q7 7 14 0" stroke="#345" fill="none" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>';
  function mk(cls,html,st){const e=document.createElement('div');e.className=cls;e.style.cssText=st;if(html)e.innerHTML=html;$('#bg').appendChild(e)}
  function drawTc(){$('#tc').innerHTML=Object.entries(TH).map(([k,t])=>`<button type="button" class="chip canva-button" data-th="${k}" aria-pressed="${D.th===k}">${t}</button>`).join('')}
  function applyTheme(){const h=document.documentElement,bg=$('#bg');bg.innerHTML='';if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(TH,D.th))D.th='sky';h.classList.toggle('deco',D.th!=='none');if(D.th==='none'){bg.style.background='';$('#pd').hidden=true;drawTc();return}bg.style.background=G[D.th];bg.style.setProperty('--tint',CFG.darkUI&&D.th!=='space'?'rgba(20,16,60,.5)':'transparent');$('#pd').hidden=false;const N=(n,f)=>{for(let i=0;i<n;i++)f()};if(D.th==='sky')N(7,()=>mk('cl',CLOUD,`top:${rnd(2,72)}%;width:${rnd(90,170)}px;opacity:${rnd(.7,1)};--d:${rnd(50,95)}s;animation-delay:-${rnd(0,90)}s`));if(D.th==='sakura')N(28,()=>mk('pt','',`left:${rnd(0,100)}%;--s:${rnd(10,18)}px;--x:${rnd(-140,140)}px;--d:${rnd(8,16)}s;animation-delay:-${rnd(0,16)}s`));if(D.th==='space'){N(70,()=>mk('st','',`left:${rnd(0,100)}%;top:${rnd(0,100)}%;--s:${rnd(2,5)}px;animation-delay:-${rnd(0,3)}s`));N(3,()=>mk('sh','',`top:${rnd(0,35)}%;left:${rnd(0,50)}%;animation-delay:-${rnd(0,7)}s`));mk('mo',MOON,'')}if(D.th==='sea'){N(20,()=>mk('bb','',`left:${rnd(0,100)}%;width:${rnd(8,26)}px;height:${rnd(8,26)}px;--x:${rnd(-60,60)}px;--d:${rnd(7,14)}s;animation-delay:-${rnd(0,14)}s`));N(4,()=>mk('fi',FISH,`top:${rnd(15,80)}%;width:${rnd(40,70)}px;--d:${rnd(25,45)}s;animation-delay:-${rnd(0,45)}s`))}drawTc()}
  function burst(x,y){const c=['#ffd54a','#ff7eb6','#6be3c3','#7aa8ff','#ffffff'];for(let i=0;i<14;i++){const e=document.createElement('i'),a=Math.random()*6.28,r=rnd(40,110);e.className='bu';e.style.cssText=`left:${x}px;top:${y}px;background:${c[i%5]};--dx:${Math.cos(a)*r}px;--dy:${Math.sin(a)*r}px`;document.body.appendChild(e);setTimeout(()=>e.remove(),850)}}
  const PS=[72,74,76,79,81,84,86],TR=[{n:'เมฆลอยลม (กล่องดนตรี)',bpm:100,w:'triangle',ch:[[60,64,67,71],[57,60,64,69],[53,57,60,65],[55,59,62,67]],p:[0,1,2,3,2,1,2,3]},{n:'ซากุระยามเย็น',bpm:76,w:'sine',ch:[[57,60,64,67],[53,57,60,64],[60,64,67,71],[52,59,64,67]],p:[0,2,1,3,2,3,1,2],l:3,b:1},{n:'ขนมหวานเด้งดึ๋ง',bpm:126,w:'square',ch:[[60,64,67],[55,59,62],[57,60,64],[53,57,60]],p:[0,2,1,2,0,2,1,2],l:1,b:1,g:.07},{n:'ใต้ทะเลสีฟ้า',bpm:66,w:'sine',ch:[[62,66,69,73],[59,62,66,69],[55,59,62,66],[57,61,64,69]],p:[0,1,2,3,2,1,0,1],l:4,b:1},{n:'ดาวระยิบระยับ',bpm:92,w:'triangle',ch:[[60,64,67],[57,60,64],[53,57,60],[55,59,62]],r:.55,sc:PS,b:1},{n:'เช้าสดใส',bpm:112,w:'triangle',ch:[[55,59,62,67],[50,54,57,62],[52,55,59,64],[48,52,55,60]],p:[0,2,3,2,1,2,3,2],b:1},{n:'ชานมไข่มุก',bpm:90,w:'sine',ch:[[65,69,72,76],[62,65,69,72],[60,64,67,72],[58,62,65,70]],p:[0,3,1,3,2,3,1,3],l:2,b:1},{n:'มังกรน้อยเหาะ',bpm:134,w:'triangle',ch:[[57,60,64],[55,59,62],[53,57,60],[52,56,59]],p:[0,1,2,1,0,1,2,2],l:1,o:24,b:1}];
  let AC,mg,dl,timer,step=0,nextT=0;const fq=m=>440*Math.pow(2,(m-69)/12);
  function initAudio(){AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();mg=AC.createGain();mg.gain.value=D.vol*.8;mg.connect(AC.destination);dl=AC.createDelay();dl.delayTime.value=.3;const fb=AC.createGain();fb.gain.value=.3;dl.connect(fb);fb.connect(dl);dl.connect(mg)}
  function note(f,t,d,w,g){const o=AC.createOscillator(),e=AC.createGain();o.type=w;o.frequency.value=f;e.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t);e.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(g,t+.012);e.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+d);o.connect(e);e.connect(mg);e.connect(dl);o.start(t);o.stop(t+d+.05)}
  function sched(){const T=TR[D.tr],sp=60/T.bpm/2;while(nextT<AC.currentTime+.3){const c=T.ch[Math.floor(step/8)%T.ch.length],k=step%8,g=T.g||.17;if(T.r){if(Math.random()<T.r)note(fq(T.sc[Math.floor(Math.random()*T.sc.length)]),nextT,sp*3,T.w,g)}else note(fq(c[T.p[k]%c.length]+(T.o||12)),nextT,sp*(T.l||2),T.w,g);if(k===0)note(fq(c[0]-12),nextT,sp*6,'sine',.2);if(k===4&&T.b)note(fq(c[2%c.length]-12),nextT,sp*3,'sine',.12);nextT+=sp;step++}}
  const playing=()=>!!timer;function musicOn(){if(!AC)initAudio();AC.resume();step=0;nextT=AC.currentTime+.1;clearInterval(timer);timer=setInterval(sched,100);$('#pl').textContent='ปิดเพลง'}function musicOff(){clearInterval(timer);timer=null;$('#pl').textContent='เปิดเพลง'}
  $('#tr').innerHTML=TR.map((t,i)=>`<option value="${i}">${t.n}</option>`).join('');if(!Number.isInteger(D.tr)||D.tr<0||D.tr>=TR.length)D.tr=0;if(!Number.isFinite(D.vol))D.vol=.5;D.vol=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,D.vol));$('#tr').value=D.tr;$('#vol').value=D.vol;
  $('#tr').onchange=e=>{D.tr=+e.target.value;dsave();if(playing())musicOn()};$('#vol').oninput=e=>{D.vol=+e.target.value;dsave();if(mg)mg.gain.value=D.vol*.8};$('#pl').onclick=()=>{try{playing()?musicOff():musicOn()}catch(e){musicOff();toast('เบราว์เซอร์นี้เล่นเพลงไม่ได้')}};$('#fb').onclick=()=>{const p=$('#dp');p.hidden=!p.hidden;$('#fb').setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!p.hidden))};$('#tc').onclick=e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-th]');if(b){D.th=b.dataset.th;dsave();applyTheme()}};
  const CH=['สู้ๆ! อีกนิดก็จำได้แล้ว','เก่งมาก! 加油','จำได้ทีละคำ ก็ครบ 1,800 คำแน่นอน','พักสายตาแป๊บนึงก็ดีนะ','วันนี้ทบทวนแล้ว เยี่ยมเลย'];$('#pd').onclick=e=>{burst(e.clientX,e.clientY);toast(CH[Math.floor(Math.random()*CH.length)])};window.decorBurst=burst;document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('button');if(b&&CFG.knownText.some(k=>b.textContent.trim()===k))burst(e.clientX,e.clientY)},true);applyTheme();
  })();
  </script><!-- Practice add-on: reads the existing vocabulary without changing it. -->
  <style>
  #pxf{position:fixed;left:12px;bottom:calc(92px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));z-index:45;border:0;border-radius:99px;padding:12px 19px;background:#6c4df6;color:#fff;font:700 16px "Noto Sans Thai",sans-serif;box-shadow:0 8px 24px #0003}#px{--jade:#1e7a68;--pale:#d6ebe4;position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:50;overflow:auto;background:#edf3f1;color:#14202e;font:16px/1.55 "Noto Sans Thai",sans-serif}#px[hidden]{display:none}#px *{box-sizing:border-box}#px button,#px input,#px select,#px textarea{font:inherit}#px button{cursor:pointer}#px button:focus-visible,#px input:focus-visible,#px select:focus-visible,#px textarea:focus-visible,#pxf:focus-visible{outline:3px solid #d48420;outline-offset:2px}#px .pxh{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:2;background:#edf3f1;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:12px 16px}#px .pxw{max-width:760px;margin:auto;padding:8px 16px 50px}#px h1{font-size:26px}#px h2{font-size:20px}#px .chips,#px .acts{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:12px 0}#px .chip,#px .opt,#px .mi,#px .btn,#px .ib{border:1px solid #c9d8d4;border-radius:13px;padding:11px 14px;background:white;color:#14202e}#px .chip{border-radius:99px;padding:6px 12px;font-size:14px}#px .chip[aria-pressed=true],#px .btn{background:var(--jade);color:white;border-color:var(--jade)}#px .mi{display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;margin:8px 0;font-weight:700;box-shadow:0 2px 8px #14202e18}#px .panel{padding:18px;background:white;border-radius:20px;box-shadow:0 10px 30px #14202e17;margin:12px 0}#px .opts{display:grid;gap:10px;margin-top:14px}#px .opt{text-align:left;width:100%;font-weight:600}#px .opt.good{background:#d6ebe4;border-color:#1e7a68}#px .opt.bad{background:#fbe2df;border-color:#b3382f}#px .opt.sel{background:#fbeed2;border-color:#a86c0f}#px .mt{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px}#px .zh{text-align:center;font:700 clamp(38px,10vw,72px) "Noto Sans SC",sans-serif;color:#173f35}#px .py{text-align:center;font-size:23px;color:var(--jade)}#px .qz{text-align:center;margin:12px 0}#px .bar{height:9px;background:var(--pale);border-radius:9px;overflow:hidden;flex:1}#px .bar i{display:block;background:var(--jade);height:100%}#px .prog{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}#px .score{font-size:52px;font-weight:700;color:var(--jade)}#px input,#px select,#px textarea{display:block;width:100%;padding:12px;border:1px solid #c9d8d4;border-radius:11px;background:white;margin:10px 0}#px .row{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;padding:10px;border-bottom:1px solid #ddd}#px .row span{flex:1}#px .stats{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px}#px .stats .panel{margin:0}#pxt{position:fixed;bottom:24px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:60;background:#14202e;color:white;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 16px;display:none}#pxt.on{display:block}.pxb{position:fixed;width:12px;height:12px;pointer-events:none;z-index:70;animation:pxburst .8s ease-out forwards}@keyframes pxburst{to{transform:translate(var(--dx),var(--dy)) scale(.2) rotate(200deg);opacity:0}}@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.pxb{animation-duration:.01s}}
  </style>
  <script>
  (()=>{
    const CFG={getWords:()=>window.zranPracticeWords?.()||null,setSize:50},$=s=>document.querySelector(s),esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])),mix=a=>{a=[...a];for(let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){let j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[a[i],a[j]]=[a[j],a[i]]}return a};
    const LS='prac_v1';let S={srs:{},wr:{},log:{},goal:20,lg:'th',sc:'all'};try{Object.assign(S,JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LS)||'{}'))}catch(e){}const save=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(LS,JSON.stringify(S))}catch(e){toast('ไม่สามารถบันทึกความคืบหน้าได้')}};
    const dict={ 'ฝึกภาษาจีน':['汉语练习','Chinese practice'],'ทบทวนตามกำหนด (SRS)':['按计划复习 (SRS)','Scheduled review (SRS)'],'ฟังแล้วเลือกคำแปล':['听音选义','Listen & choose'],'พิมพ์พินอิน':['输入拼音','Type pinyin'],'เติมคำในประโยค':['句子填空','Fill in the blank'],'ฟังประโยคแล้วพิมพ์ตาม':['听写句子','Dictation'],'จับคู่ 汉字 กับคำแปล':['汉字配对','Match pairs'],'สถิติ & สมุดคำผิด':['统计与错题本','Stats & mistakes'],'กลับเมนู':['返回菜单','Back to menu'],'ข้อถัดไป':['下一题','Next question'],'ตรวจคำตอบ':['检查答案','Check answer'],'แสดงคำตอบ':['显示答案','Show answer'],'ลืมแล้ว':['忘了','Forgot'],'จำได้':['记住了','Remembered'],'ฟังเสียง':['听发音','Listen'],'ฟังอีกครั้ง':['再听一次','Listen again'],'ถูกต้อง!':['正确！','Correct!'],'ยังไม่ถูก คำตอบ:':['不对，答案：','Not quite. Answer:'],'ผลการฝึก':['练习结果','Practice results'],'ทำอีกครั้ง':['再做一次','Try again'],'เล่นอีกครั้ง':['再玩一次','Play again'],'เก่งมาก!':['太棒了！','Great job!'],'เป้าหมายรายวัน':['每日目标','Daily goal'],'7 วันล่าสุด':['最近7天','Last 7 days'],'วันต่อเนื่อง':['连续天数','Day streak'],'ถึงกำหนดทบทวน SRS':['今日待复习','Due for SRS review'],'คำที่ผิดบ่อย':['常错词','Most missed words'],'สำรองข้อมูล':['备份数据','Back up'],'นำเข้าข้อมูล':['导入数据','Import data'],'นำเข้า':['导入','Import'],'ปิด':['关闭','Close'],'ฝึก':['练习','Practice'],'ทุกคำ':['全部词','All words'],'พิมพ์พินอิน (ใส่เลข 1-4 แทนวรรณยุกต์ได้)':['输入拼音（可用数字声调）','Type pinyin (tone numbers accepted)'],'พิมพ์ประโยคที่ได้ยิน':['输入你听到的句子','Type the sentence you hear'],'ยังไม่พบข้อมูลคำศัพท์ ให้ตั้งค่า CFG.getWords ให้ส่งคืนรายการคำศัพท์เดิมของเว็บ':['未找到词汇数据，请设置 CFG.getWords 返回网站现有的词表','No vocabulary found. Set CFG.getWords to return the existing word list.']};
    const tr=s=>S.lg==='th'?s:(dict[s]?.[S.lg==='zh'?0:1]||s),btn=(text,a,extra='')=>`<button type="button" class="btn canva-button" data-a="${a}" ${extra}>${tr(text)}</button>`,toast=t=>{let e=$('#pxt');e.textContent=tr(t);e.classList.add('on');clearTimeout(toast.t);toast.t=setTimeout(()=>e.classList.remove('on'),2300)};
    let WL=[],WM={},V=null,bk='',LX=innerWidth/2,LY=innerHeight/2;function load(){let src;try{src=CFG.getWords()}catch(e){}WL=Array.isArray(src)?src.map((o,i)=>({id:String(o['序号']||o.id||'w'+i),zh:String(o['汉字']||o.zh||''),py:String(o['拼音']||o.py||''),th:String(o['แปลไทย']||o.th||''),ex:[[o['ตัวอย่างประโยคจีน']||o['ตัวอย่างประโยค 1'],o['แปลประโยคไทย']||o['แปลไทย 1']],[o['ตัวอย่างประโยค 2'],o['แปลไทย 2']]].filter(x=>x[0])})).filter(w=>w.zh&&w.th):[];WM=Object.fromEntries(WL.map(w=>[w.id,w]))}const pool=()=>S.sc==='all'?WL:WL.slice((+S.sc-1)*CFG.setSize,+S.sc*CFG.setSize);const day=(n=0)=>{let d=new Date();d.setDate(d.getDate()+n);return [d.getFullYear(),String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')].join('-')};
    function say(t){if(!('speechSynthesis'in window)){toast('เบราว์เซอร์นี้ไม่รองรับเสียงอ่าน');return}speechSynthesis.cancel();let u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(t);u.lang='zh-CN';u.rate=.85;u.voice=speechSynthesis.getVoices().find(v=>/^zh|^cmn/i.test(v.lang))||null;speechSynthesis.speak(u)}function burst(){for(let i=0;i<14;i++){let e=document.createElement('i'),a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,r=40+Math.random()*70;e.className='pxb';e.style.cssText=`left:${LX}px;top:${LY}px;background:${['#ffd54a','#ff7eb6','#6be3c3','#7aa8ff'][i%4]};--dx:${Math.cos(a)*r}px;--dy:${Math.sin(a)*r}px`;document.body.append(e);setTimeout(()=>e.remove(),850)}}function log(){let k=day();S.log[k]=(S.log[k]||0)+1;save();if(S.log[k]===S.goal){toast('ครบเป้าหมายวันนี้!');burst()}}function wrong(w){S.wr[w.id]=(S.wr[w.id]||0)+1;save()}function opts(w,key){let values=new Set([w[key]]);for(let n=0;n<500&&values.size<4;n++)values.add(WL[Math.floor(Math.random()*WL.length)][key]);return mix([...values])}
    function start(t){let p=pool();if(t==='stats'){V={t};render();return}if(t==='match'){if(p.length<6){toast('คำไม่พอ');return}let x=mix(p).slice(0,6);V={t,L:mix(x),R:mix(x),done:{},sel:null,miss:0,t0:Date.now()};render();return}if(t==='fill')p=p.filter(w=>w.ex.some(e=>e[0].includes(w.zh)));if(t==='dict')p=p.filter(w=>w.ex.length);let it=t==='srs'?[...p.filter(w=>S.srs[w.id]?.due<=day()),...p.filter(w=>!S.srs[w.id]).slice(0,10)]:mix(p).slice(0,10);if(!it.length){toast('ไม่มีคำให้ฝึกในขอบเขตนี้');return}V={t,it,i:0,ok:0};prep();render()}function prep(){let v=V,w=v.it[v.i];v.answer=null;v.reveal=false;if(v.t==='listen'){v.options=opts(w,'th');setTimeout(()=>say(w.zh),250)}if(v.t==='fill'){v.example=w.ex.find(e=>e[0].includes(w.zh));v.options=opts(w,'zh')}if(v.t==='dict'){v.example=w.ex[Math.floor(Math.random()*w.ex.length)];setTimeout(()=>say(v.example[0]),250)}}function next(){V.i++;if(V.i<V.it.length)prep();render();$('#px').scrollTop=0}function result(ok,w){if(V.answer!==null)return;V.answer=ok;if(ok){V.ok++;burst()}else wrong(w);log();render()}
    function stats(){let t=S.log[day()]||0,g=S.goal,ds=Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>day(i-6)),max=Math.max(1,...ds.map(k=>S.log[k]||0)),st=0;for(let i=S.log[day()]?0:1;S.log[day(-i)];i++)st++;let due=WL.filter(w=>S.srs[w.id]?.due<=day()).length,wr=Object.entries(S.wr).filter(([id])=>WM[id]).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,10);return `<h2>${tr('เป้าหมายรายวัน')}</h2><div class="panel"><b>${t}/${g}</b><div class="bar"><i style="width:${Math.min(100,t/g*100)}%"></i></div><div class="chips">${[10,20,50,100].map(n=>`<button type="button" class="chip canva-button" data-a="goal" data-v="${n}" aria-pressed="${g===n}">${n}</button>`).join('')}</div></div><h2>${tr('7 วันล่าสุด')}</h2><div class="panel" style="display:flex;align-items:end;gap:8px;min-height:130px">${ds.map(k=>`<div style="flex:1;text-align:center;font-size:12px">${S.log[k]||0}<div style="height:${Math.max(3,(S.log[k]||0)/max*70)}px;background:#1e7a68;border-radius:6px"></div>${k.slice(5)}</div>`).join('')}</div><div class="stats"><div class="panel">${st}<br>${tr('วันต่อเนื่อง')}</div><div class="panel">${due}<br>${tr('ถึงกำหนดทบทวน SRS')}</div></div>${wr.length?`<h2>${tr('คำที่ผิดบ่อย')}</h2>${wr.map(([id,c])=>`<button type="button" class="row canva-button" style="width:100%;background:white;border:0;text-align:left" data-a="say" data-t="${esc(WM[id].zh)}"><strong>${esc(WM[id].zh)}</strong><span>${esc(WM[id].py)} · ${esc(WM[id].th)} (×${c})</span>🔊</button>`).join('')}`:''}<div class="acts">${btn('สำรองข้อมูล','backup')}${btn('นำเข้าข้อมูล','import')}</div>${bk==='out'?`<label for="pbackup">${tr('สำรองข้อมูล')}</label><textarea id="pbackup" readonly rows="5">${esc(JSON.stringify(S))}</textarea>`:''}${bk==='in'?`<label for="bi">${tr('นำเข้าข้อมูล')}</label><textarea id="bi" rows="5"></textarea>${btn('นำเข้า','apply')}`:''}${btn('กลับเมนู','menu')}`}
    function content(){if(!WL.length)return `<div class="panel">${tr('ยังไม่พบข้อมูลคำศัพท์ ให้ตั้งค่า CFG.getWords ให้ส่งคืนรายการคำศัพท์เดิมของเว็บ')}</div>`;if(!V)return `<h1>${tr('ฝึกภาษาจีน')}</h1><label for="psc">${tr('ทุกคำ')}</label><select id="psc" class="canva-dropdown"><option value="all">${tr('ทุกคำ')} (${WL.length})</option>${Array.from({length:Math.ceil(WL.length/50)},(_,i)=>`<option value="${i+1}" ${S.sc==i+1?'selected':''}>${S.lg==='zh'?'第'+(i+1)+'组':S.lg==='en'?'Set '+(i+1):'ชุดที่ '+(i+1)} (${WL.slice(i*50,(i+1)*50).length})</option>`).join('')}</select>${[['srs','ทบทวนตามกำหนด (SRS)'],['listen','ฟังแล้วเลือกคำแปล'],['pinyin','พิมพ์พินอิน'],['fill','เติมคำในประโยค'],['dict','ฟังประโยคแล้วพิมพ์ตาม'],['match','จับคู่ 汉字 กับคำแปล'],['stats','สถิติ & สมุดคำผิด']].map(([k,t])=>`<button type="button" class="mi canva-button" data-a="start" data-v="${k}">${tr(t)}</button>`).join('')}`;let v=V;if(v.t==='stats')return stats();if(v.t==='match'){let n=Object.keys(v.done).length;if(n===6)return `<div class="panel qz"><h2>${tr('เก่งมาก!')}</h2><div class="score">${Math.round((Date.now()-v.t0)/1000)}s</div><p>${v.miss} ${S.lg==='en'?'misses':S.lg==='zh'?'次失误':'ครั้งที่พลาด'}</p></div><div class="acts">${btn('เล่นอีกครั้ง','start','data-v="match"')}${btn('กลับเมนู','menu')}</div>`;let tile=(side,w,i)=>`<button type="button" class="opt canva-button ${v.done[w.id]?'good':v.sel?.side===side&&v.sel.i===i?'sel':''}" data-a="tile" data-side="${side}" data-i="${i}" ${v.done[w.id]?'disabled':''}>${esc(side==='L'?w.zh:w.th)}</button>`;return `<div class="prog">${n}/6<div class="bar"><i style="width:${n/6*100}%"></i></div></div><div class="mt"><div class="opts">${v.L.map((w,i)=>tile('L',w,i)).join('')}</div><div class="opts">${v.R.map((w,i)=>tile('R',w,i)).join('')}</div></div>`}if(v.i>=v.it.length)return `<div class="panel qz"><h2>${tr('ผลการฝึก')}</h2><div class="score">${v.ok}/${v.it.length}</div></div><div class="acts">${btn('ทำอีกครั้ง','start',`data-v="${v.t}"`)}${btn('กลับเมนู','menu')}</div>`;let w=v.it[v.i],answered=v.answer!==null,bar=`<div class="prog">${v.i+1}/${v.it.length}<div class="bar"><i style="width:${v.i/v.it.length*100}%"></i></div></div>`,speech=t=>`<button type="button" class="ib canva-button" data-a="say" data-t="${esc(t)}">🔊 ${tr('ฟังอีกครั้ง')}</button>`,feedback=answered?`<p style="color:${v.answer?'#1e7a68':'#b3382f'}">${v.answer?tr('ถูกต้อง!'):tr('ยังไม่ถูก คำตอบ:')+' '+esc(v.t==='pinyin'?w.py:v.t==='dict'?v.example[0]:v.t==='fill'?w.zh:w.th)}</p>${btn('ข้อถัดไป','next')}`:'';if(v.t==='srs')return bar+`<div class="panel qz"><div class="zh">${esc(w.zh)}</div><div class="py">${esc(w.py)}</div>${speech(w.zh)}${v.reveal?`<h2>${esc(w.th)}</h2>${w.ex[0]?`<p>${esc(w.ex[0][0])}<br>${esc(w.ex[0][1])}</p>`:''}<div class="acts" style="justify-content:center">${btn('ลืมแล้ว','grade','data-v="0"')}${btn('จำได้','grade','data-v="1"')}</div>`:btn('แสดงคำตอบ','reveal')}</div>`;if(v.t==='listen'||v.t==='fill'){let key=v.t==='listen'?'th':'zh',prompt=v.t==='listen'?speech(w.zh):`<p style="font-size:22px">${esc(answered?v.example[0]:v.example[0].replace(w.zh,'＿＿'))}</p><p>${esc(v.example[1])}</p>`,correct=w[key];return bar+`<div class="panel"><div class="qz">${prompt}${answered&&v.t==='listen'?`<div class="zh">${esc(w.zh)}</div><div class="py">${esc(w.py)}</div>`:''}</div><div class="opts">${v.options.map((o,i)=>`<button type="button" class="opt canva-button ${answered?(o===correct?'good':v.pick===i?'bad':''):''}" data-a="pick" data-i="${i}" ${answered?'disabled':''}>${esc(o)}</button>`).join('')}</div>${feedback}</div>`}return bar+`<div class="panel"><div class="qz">${v.t==='pinyin'?`<div class="zh">${esc(w.zh)}</div>`:speech(v.example[0])}</div><label for="ti">${tr(v.t==='pinyin'?'พิมพ์พินอิน (ใส่เลข 1-4 แทนวรรณยุกต์ได้)':'พิมพ์ประโยคที่ได้ยิน')}</label><input id="ti" class="canva-input" autocomplete="off" ${answered?'disabled':''}>${answered&&v.t==='dict'?`<p>${esc(v.example[0])} · ${esc(v.example[1])}</p>`:''}${answered?feedback:btn('ตรวจคำตอบ','check')}</div>`}
    function render(){$('#pxb').innerHTML=content();$('#plc').innerHTML=[['th','ไทย'],['zh','中文'],['en','English']].map(([k,t])=>`<button type="button" class="chip canva-button" data-lg="${k}" aria-pressed="${S.lg===k}">${t}</button>`).join('');$('#px-title').textContent=tr('ฝึกภาษาจีน');$('#pxx').setAttribute('aria-label',tr('ปิด'));let input=$('#ti');if(input&&!input.disabled)input.focus()}
    const tone={a:'āáǎà',e:'ēéěè',i:'īíǐì',o:'ōóǒò',u:'ūúǔù','ü':'ǖǘǚǜ'},tonize=s=>s.toLowerCase().replace(/v/g,'ü').replace(/([a-zü]+)([1-4])/g,(_,y,n)=>{let k=y.includes('a')?'a':y.includes('e')?'e':y.includes('ou')?'o':[...y].reverse().find(c=>tone[c]);return k?y.replace(k,tone[k][n-1]):y}),clean=s=>s.toLowerCase().replace(/[\s'’]/g,''),han=s=>s.replace(/[^\u4e00-\u9fff]/g,'');
    const root=document.createElement('div');root.innerHTML='<button type="button" id="pxf" class="canva-button" aria-haspopup="dialog">ฝึก</button><section id="px" class="canva-panel" hidden role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="px-title"><header class="pxh canva-header"><b id="px-title">ฝึกภาษาจีน</b><div id="plc" class="chips"></div><button type="button" id="pxx" class="canva-button" aria-label="ปิด">✕</button></header><main class="pxw"><div id="pxb"></div></main></section><div id="pxt" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>';while(root.firstChild)document.body.append(root.firstChild);
    $('#pxf').onclick=()=>{load();V=null;$('#px').hidden=false;render();$('#pxx').focus()};$('#pxx').onclick=()=>{$('#px').hidden=true;V=null;$('#pxf').focus()};$('#px').addEventListener('click',e=>{LX=e.clientX;LY=e.clientY;let b=e.target.closest('[data-lg]');if(b){S.lg=b.dataset.lg;save();render();return}b=e.target.closest('[data-a]');if(!b)return;let a=b.dataset.a,w=V?.it?.[V.i];if(a==='start')start(b.dataset.v);if(a==='menu'){V=null;bk='';render()}if(a==='reveal'){V.reveal=true;render()}if(a==='say')say(b.dataset.t);if(a==='next')next();if(a==='grade'){let ok=b.dataset.v==='1',rank=ok?Math.min((S.srs[w.id]?.b||0)+1,5):0;S.srs[w.id]={b:rank,due:day([0,1,2,4,8,16][rank])};if(ok){V.ok++;burst()}else wrong(w);log();next()}if(a==='pick'&&V.answer===null){V.pick=+b.dataset.i;result(V.options[V.pick]===w[V.t==='listen'?'th':'zh'],w)}if(a==='check'&&V.answer===null){let x=$('#ti').value.trim();if(x)result(V.t==='pinyin'?clean(tonize(x))===clean(w.py):han(x)===han(V.example[0]),w)}if(a==='tile'){let side=b.dataset.side,i=+b.dataset.i;if(V.sel&&V.sel.side!==side){let l=side==='L'?V.L[i]:V.L[V.sel.i],r=side==='R'?V.R[i]:V.R[V.sel.i];if(l.id===r.id){V.done[l.id]=true;burst();log()}else{V.miss++;wrong(l)}V.sel=null}else{V.sel={side,i};if(side==='L')say(V.L[i].zh)}render()}if(a==='goal'){S.goal=+b.dataset.v;save();render()}if(a==='backup'||a==='import'){bk=a==='backup'?'out':'in';render();if(bk==='out')$('#pbackup').select()}if(a==='apply'){try{let data=JSON.parse($('#bi').value);if(!data||typeof data!=='object'||!data.srs||!data.log||!data.wr)throw Error();S={...S,...data};save();bk='';toast('นำเข้าแล้ว');render()}catch(e){toast('ข้อมูลไม่ถูกต้อง')}}});$('#px').addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target.id==='psc'){S.sc=e.target.value;save();render()}});$('#px').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&e.target.id==='ti'){e.preventDefault();$('#px [data-a="check"]')?.click()}if(e.key==='Escape')$('#pxx').click()});
  })();
  </script>
  <script>
  (()=>{
    const control=document.getElementById('settings-click-sound');
    const key='zran4h-click-sound';
    let enabled=true,ctx=null,active=new Set();
    try{enabled=localStorage.getItem(key)!=='off'}catch(e){}
    control.value=enabled?'on':'off';
    function silence(){for(const item of active){try{item.gain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(ctx.currentTime);item.gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0,ctx.currentTime);item.osc.stop()}catch(e){}}active.clear()}
    control.addEventListener('change',()=>{enabled=control.value==='on';if(!enabled)silence();try{localStorage.setItem(key,enabled?'on':'off')}catch(e){}});
    function tap(){
      if(!enabled)return;
      try{
        const AudioContextClass=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;
        if(!AudioContextClass)return;
        if(!ctx)ctx=new AudioContextClass();
        if(ctx.state==='suspended')ctx.resume().catch(()=>{});
        const osc=ctx.createOscillator(),gain=ctx.createGain(),now=ctx.currentTime,item={osc,gain};
        osc.type='sine';osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(660,now);osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(470,now+.055);
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,now);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.045,now+.008);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,now+.075);
        osc.connect(gain);gain.connect(ctx.destination);active.add(item);
        osc.onended=()=>{active.delete(item);osc.disconnect();gain.disconnect()};
        osc.start(now);osc.stop(now+.085);
      }catch(e){}
    }
    document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      const target=e.target instanceof Element?e.target.closest('button,[role="button"],select'):null;
      if(target&&!target.matches(':disabled')&&target!==control)tap();
    },true);
    document.addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target instanceof Element&&e.target.matches('select')&&e.target!==control)tap()},true);
  })();
  </script>
  <script>
  /* Keep the settings controls, dashboard and secondary panels on one language source. */
  (()=>{
    const language=document.getElementById('interface-language');
    const copy={
      th:{week:'การเรียนรู้ 7 วันล่าสุด',chart:'สถิติการเรียน 7 วันล่าสุด',click:'เสียงกด',on:'เปิด',off:'ปิด',reminder:'',motion:'ภาพเคลื่อนไหวพื้นหลัง'},
      lo:{week:'ການຮຽນຮູ້ 7 ມື້ຫຼ້າສຸດ',chart:'ສະຖິຕິການຮຽນ 7 ມື້ຫຼ້າສຸດ',click:'ສຽງກົດ',on:'ເປີດ',off:'ປິດ',motion:'ພາບເຄື່ອນໄຫວພື້ນຫຼັງ'},
      zh:{week:'最近 7 天的学习',chart:'最近 7 天学习统计',click:'按键音',on:'开启',off:'关闭',motion:'背景动画'},
      en:{week:'Learning in the last 7 days',chart:'Learning statistics for the last 7 days',click:'Click sound',on:'On',off:'Off',motion:'Background animation'}
    };
    function sync(){
      const lang=copy[language.value]?language.value:'th',t=copy[lang];
      document.getElementById('settings-language').value=lang;
      document.getElementById('dash-week-title').textContent=t.week;
      document.getElementById('dash-week-chart').setAttribute('aria-label',t.chart);
      document.getElementById('settings-click-sound-label').textContent=t.click;
      const sound=document.getElementById('settings-click-sound');
      sound.querySelector('[value="on"]').textContent=t.on;
      sound.querySelector('[value="off"]').textContent=t.off;
      if(lang==='en'){
        document.getElementById('world-motion-label').textContent=t.motion;
        const motion=document.getElementById('world-motion');motion.querySelector('[value="on"]').textContent=t.on;motion.querySelector('[value="off"]').textContent=t.off;
        const reminder=document.getElementById('settings-reminder');
        const match=reminder.textContent.match(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/);
        if(match){reminder.textContent=`Today: ${match[1]} / ${match[2]} words · Keep learning!`;document.getElementById('dashboard-reminder').textContent=reminder.textContent}
      }
      const practice=document.getElementById('pxf');if(practice)practice.textContent=({th:'ฝึก',lo:'ຝຶກ',zh:'练习',en:'Practice'})[lang];
    }
    language.addEventListener('change',sync);
    document.getElementById('settings-language').addEventListener('change',()=>queueMicrotask(sync));
    sync();
  })();
  </script>
  <style>
   /* A little extra light and motion, confined to the selected navigation. */
   .nav.canva-tabs:before{content:attr(data-brand-label)}
   .nav.canva-tabs button{position:relative;isolation:isolate;overflow:hidden}
   .nav.canva-tabs button:after{content:'';position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:-1;pointer-events:none;background:linear-gradient(110deg,transparent 20%,rgba(155,230,255,.14) 50%,transparent 80%);transform:translateX(-120%);transition:transform .55s ease}
   .nav.canva-tabs button:hover:after,.nav.canva-tabs button:focus-visible:after{transform:translateX(120%)}
   .nav.canva-tabs button:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #76e9f3;outline-offset:-2px}
   .nav.canva-tabs button.active{animation:nav-glow .35s ease-out}
   @keyframes nav-glow{from{filter:brightness(1.4)}to{filter:brightness(1)}}
   @media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.nav.canva-tabs button.active{animation:none}.nav.canva-tabs button:after{transition:none}}
  </style>
  <script>
   (()=>{
    const nav=document.querySelector('nav.nav.canva-tabs'),select=document.getElementById('interface-language');
    const labels={
     th:{dashboard:'ภาพรวม',study:'บัตรคำ',library:'คลังคำศัพท์',review:'ต้องทบทวน',quiz:'แบบทดสอบ',smart:'ทบทวนอัจฉริยะ',wrongbook:'สมุดข้อผิดพลาด',favorites:'รายการโปรด',compare:'เปรียบเทียบคำศัพท์',statistics:'สถิติการเรียน',advanced:'ค้นหาขั้นสูง',management:'จัดการข้อมูล',settings:'ตั้งค่า',menu:'เมนูหลัก',brand:'ZRAN4H  /  พื้นที่เรียนรู้'},
     lo:{dashboard:'ພາບລວມ',study:'ບັດຄຳ',library:'ຄັງຄຳສັບ',review:'ຄຳທີ່ຕ້ອງທົບທວນ',quiz:'ແບບທົດສອບ',smart:'ທົບທວນອັດສະລິຍະ',wrongbook:'ສະໝຸດຂໍ້ຜິດພາດ',favorites:'ລາຍການທີ່ມັກ',compare:'ປຽບທຽບຄຳສັບ',statistics:'ສະຖິຕິການຮຽນ',advanced:'ຄົ້ນຫາຂັ້ນສູງ',management:'ຈັດການຂໍ້ມູນ',settings:'ຕັ້ງຄ່າ',menu:'ເມນູຫຼັກ',brand:'ZRAN4H  /  ພື້ນທີ່ຮຽນຮູ້'},
     zh:{dashboard:'总览',study:'单词卡',library:'词汇库',review:'待复习',quiz:'测验',smart:'智能复习',wrongbook:'错题本',favorites:'收藏夹',compare:'词语辨析',statistics:'学习统计',advanced:'高级搜索',management:'数据管理',settings:'设置',menu:'主菜单',brand:'ZRAN4H  /  学习空间'},
     en:{dashboard:'Overview',study:'Flashcards',library:'Vocabulary Library',review:'Due for Review',quiz:'Quiz',smart:'Smart Review',wrongbook:'Mistake Book',favorites:'Favorites',compare:'Word Comparison',statistics:'Learning Statistics',advanced:'Advanced Search',management:'Data Management',settings:'Settings',menu:'Main menu',brand:'ZRAN4H  /  LEARNING SPACE'}
    };
    function sync(){const copy=labels[select.value]||labels.th;nav.setAttribute('aria-label',copy.menu);nav.dataset.brandLabel=copy.brand;nav.querySelectorAll('button[data-page]').forEach(button=>{const label=copy[button.dataset.page];if(!label)return;const icon=button.querySelector('svg, i[data-lucide]');button.replaceChildren(...(icon?[icon]:[]),document.createTextNode(' '+label));button.setAttribute('aria-label',label)});}
    select.addEventListener('change',sync);document.getElementById('settings-language').addEventListener('change',()=>queueMicrotask(sync));sync();
   })();
  </script>
 </body>
</html>
